viśvanāthapañcānanāḥ "anekaikatrabuddhiyogyatvābuddhirityante" iti kārikāvyākhyāyāṃ vidvānasūktāvacyā "yajñāniyataikatvajñānaṃ tatra citvādibhinnā bahutvasaṃkhyotpadyate yayā caināvacanāditi kandalīkāraḥ" mahādevabhaṭṭaśca sukṛtavallīkāyāṃ "pratyāropitaṃ nīlakuryaṃ sam iti kandalīkāramatam" ityādyuktavantaḥ |
sarvadarśanasaṃ kośe'pi kandalīpanthaḥ kaśca kandalīkāra iti nirṇīyumicchanhaṃ māyavīye kharvadarśanasaṃgrahe śrītatvayadīpane "dravyaṃ nava iti bhāṭṭā vedāntinaśca bhaṇanti" "āropitaṃ nīlakuryamiti śrīdharācāryāḥ" iti dṛṣṭvā vaiśeṣikabhāṣyasyāsyaiva kāryatvaṃ kandalīpanthaḥ śrīdharācāryasyaiva kandalīkāra ityanumitavān |tattacca samayādivaiḥ śrīmadrudrasvayambhūlottanāmīyataṃ "nyāyakandalī prathaktapādabhāṣyaṭīkā tatputtakaṃ ca vārāṇasīstharājakīyaveṅkuṭapāṭhaśālīyasya prathamanyāyādhyāpakasya paṇḍitalakṣmīnṛsiṃhaśāstriṇo'pi pustakālaye(1) vartate" iti |

" ityājñātamathayastāt |

" iti "praśastapādārtho'pyu vidvadarśeṣu na jñānāntaramityāhu"riti vaiśeṣikasūtropaskāre (6a. 2 ā. 13 sū.) adgurumiśrā: |

yatu "ityetadasadādīnā pratyakṣam |
asmadbṛṣṭigṛhānā tu yoginā"mityādi praśastapādabhāṣyam tatu laukikābhiprāyeṇa svasaṃvedyam bodhyam |

dhyānināmityathaiḥ |

anyathā "manasā svātmāntarābhāvagadāṣkālaparamāṇuvāyumanaḥsu tatsamaveteṣu guṇakarmeṣāmānyaviśeṣeṣu samavāye cā'rhataṃ svarūpadarśānamutpadyate" iti pūrvebhāṣyaya virodhāt vadato vyāghātaḥ syāditi ||

17 ||"  "śrīrāmagītāmāhātmyaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jānāti śaṅkaraḥ |

47 ||"  ityādi vadato brahmaṇo'pi sarvajñatvaṃ daivatvaṃ ca na syādityalaṃ prasaṅgena ||

svapaṭāni ca vayente bhāvye bhāvyavido viduḥ|
iti bhāvyatvalakṣaṇasya tatvānupalambhāt|" "tatkārāśo bhāvyamiti sphuṭam|
prayatnapāṭavārtho'pi svanirmite granthe saṃgrahadvutapraveśa nirdideśa na bhāvyatayā tayā ca tasyāvyaḥ rūpokeḥ|
praṇamya hetumośvaraṃ suni kaṇādamādarāt(2)|
padārthe'mesaṃgrahaḥ pravacyate mahādayaḥ||" "ityeṣu vaiyathyamātrāt bhāvyasya ca dvinaratvā" dityādi||
teṣāmasamādhāyaḥ anyeṣāṃ bhāvyāṇāṃ visanīyaitvāt asya laghutve bhāvyatvaṃ na saṃbhavati laghutvena bhāvyatvena ca virodhāditi|
atra samādhīyate'maḥ|
ādyarūpokena paṭārthe 'mesaṃgraho 'yaṃ na bhāvyamiti tu nirṇītam|
prayatnapāṭavārthamṛṣartha racina iti kaṇādamagatapuḥ ādyarūpokenaivānupalabdhāt|
pradye ca "iti prayatnapāṭavārthadarśivairla dṛṣṭaṭādipaṭārṭapaṭakabhāvye 3) sadā|" iti prayānteṣupaṅktyā|
granyāntaraiya vā prayatnapāṭavārthapramīnaṃ ityucyate tadā āntyamapaṅktau bhāvyatvollekhāt antyalokācadibhiḥ(4) citsūtrārthoddibhiśca(5) bhāvyatvenāvayāritatvāt kaṇamasya bhāvyatvaṃ nirūhanto suvaintyādunikavaiṣaiśikāḥ|
api ca "svanirmite granthe saṃgrahadvutapraveśa nirdideśa na bhāvyatayā"  ityatrāpipravṛttavādaḥ pravṛtto munīnāṃ śāstrāya śāstraṃ jagato jagāda |
kulāṅkikājñāninivṛttihetuḥ kāriṣyate tasya mayā nibandhaḥ ||
ityudaghoṣakārācāryeṇyuktatvāt tasya bārttikasyābhāve nyāyaśāstrāntargataghaṭakāsyāmiviṣayatvena ityācāryeṣācyattamiśrāṇāmuttaramatānusāreṇāpi |
yadi ca mahābhāṣyārambhe "yaśa śabdānuśāsanaṃ"miti paṭhanti tisṛhastrabhiruktatvād bhāṣyatvābhāve "mahābhāṣyaṇe bācāraparīkṣā"miti bhāṣyapradīpārambhe keyaṭokte. "prātañjale mahābhāṣye kṛtabhūripariśramaṃ" iti nāgeśamaṭṭokterapi unmattapralāpatvāpattiśca ||
uddyutnācāryeṇa kiraṇāvalyāmu "sūtreṣu veyarthyamāśaṅkaya bhāṣyasya ca vistaratvā"diti yathottamu tathā'nupadameva "prakaraṇādīnāṃ caikadeśatvādi"-tyuktam |
tasmādidaṃ prakaraṇamu kintu saṃpūrṇaṃ śāstram |
ata eva "śāstrārambhe sadācāraparigrāhayathā"iti kiraṇāvalau, "śāstrārambhe rakṣimatāṃ devatā"mityādinyāyakandalyā~ "tattvapravṛttau ca śāstra kṛtabhūkṛtaṃ syāt ataḥ śāstrārambhamādayaṃtaḥ prekṣāvantaścumbantyahaṃ tasya śabdasya prayojanaṃ cādau pratīkasyottaratvena kathayanti |
padārthagrahaṃ"tītyādi-kandalīgranthaśca saṅgacchate ||
ityamasya padārthagrahasaṃgrāhimayasya bhāṣyatve cācāryairūpaśyārite |
"sūtrāṇāṃ vaṇyaṃte yatra padaiḥ suvānupūrvibhiḥ |
svapadāni ca vaṇyaṃnte bhāṣyaṃ bhāṣyavido viduḥ ||"  iti bhāṣyalakṣaṇasya sāmañjasyaṃ bhaviṣyamahaiti |
tathāhi |
"sūtrāṇāṃ vaṇyaṃte yatra" asya sāmañjasye prathāhaṃ uddyutnācāryaḥ kiraṇavākyānu "bhūtena ṣoḍaśabhyaḥ padaiḥ" vyākhyātam |
"bhūtenāśīto dharme vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |yato gurudamaniḥ prathamasiddhiḥ sa dharmaḥ |
tadgucanādātmāyamāsakhyamiti citurtho vyākhyātā |
yate ca guṇaviniśeṣādhikāre rūpādayo guṇaviśeṣāḥ siddhāḥ iti bhāṣyam guṇaviniśeṣādhikāro dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ |
tathā ca sucam iti kiraṇāvalyām ||
nyāyakandalyām "nānaṃ sucārthe |
tamevārtha darśayati |
śabde viceṣadarśanādityādinā" |
sucārthe sucakārānumati darśayati |
tathā bāheryādinā" ||
yadya kiraṇāvalyām nyāyakandalyāmapi tatra tatra padārthanirūpaṇe bhāṣyamuktāni sucāṇi śrīdharādayanācāryodibhiruddhṛtāni |
asmābhistvatre pradarśayiṣyante ||
na ca sakalasucāṇāmanokarūpeṇa vyākhyānānupalambhādatra bhāṣyāditi śaṅkyam mahābhāṣye maharṣiṇā patañjalinā sarveṣāṃ sucāṇakhyānyātānīti tasya mahattvaṃ tu dūre gatam bhāṣyatvamevānupapannaṃ syāditi ||
nanu laghutve sucitārthoktyādisamastasya sucalakṣaṇasya pratyekamupap sambhavaya iva sucārtho varṇyate yajaityādisamastasya bhāṣyalakṣaṇasya pratyekamupasambhāveṣu samanvayo'sāvadam bhāṣyamiti cen |
uktaṭopatādaseṅgyāt samastasya lakṣaṇatve padaiḥ sucānugatirmirityādāyanaḥ sucārtho varṇyate yajaityanena saha vā svapadāni ca varṇyante ityanena baiti vikalpyoktyānugṛhīti sucākhyākṛtitvasya bhāṣye bhāṣyavido vidurityaśra bahuvacanānupalambhāt yathāsambhavameteya samnvayaḥ kārya iti na kāpyanupapattiḥ ||
yadi ca sucārtho varṇyate yajaityasya sucavyākhyānātmakaṃ śiṣṭaṃ pravatoktyartha |
tathā ca parāśaropurāṇe 18 adhyāye |
"paducchedaḥ padārthoktirvigraho vākyayojanā |
ākṣepaṣu samādhānaṃ vyākhyānaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam" ||ityanena padārthokterapi vyākhyānatvāniktakaraṇadhyasya dṛṣṭoktapadārthoktyāpi bhāṣyatvam|
atra padacchedādi: pratyekamapi lakṣaṇam|
anyathā yeniṣṭāgrāmāṇikadyutya padacchedakṛṣṇasya rādhaṇabhāṣyasya bhāṣyatvānupapatte:|
goḍapādācāryapraṇītasya kārikāgranthasya draṣṭaśārthayāgatipādakasya brahmadṛṣṭibhāṣyasya bhāṣyatvānupapatteśca||
"paṭe: sūtratuṣārādi:" asya samanvaye "hūparasagandhasparśā: saṃkhyā: parimāṇāni pṛthaktvaṃ saṃyogavibhāgau paratvāparatve buddhaya: sukhadu:khe icchādveṣau prayatnāśca guṇā:" itikāṇādasūtram casya bhāṣyam  "guṇā: rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvabuddhi-sukhadu:khecchādveṣaprayatnāśceti kāśyoktā: samadaśa cabdasamabhihitāsca gurutvadravatvavegasaṃskārādṛṣṭaśabdā: ṣoḍaśaiveyaṃ caturviṃśatiguṇā:" |
api ca saṃyoganirūpaṇe bhāṣye "yathā caturviṃśaṃ parimāṇamutpādyadravyāha dravya parimaṇḍalamityevamanyatrakarmajādisaṃyoga-sutpādyasuktā pṛthaṅgityaṃ dravyāt tvaṅmanasorvṛt tatsadbhāvātyaya: saṃyoga iti|"  anumānaprakaraṇe ca bhāṣye "yatra yathoktānu viddhuṣā-vijñānena dharmeṇa draṣṭavyau vā viparīte tadvadvameyasyāpigame liṅgaṃ na bhavatī-tvetadyāha subakāraḥ |
"agnidivyaujanapadeśo'nac ṣandirathaprābdānudeśe iti"  evamanyatrāpi |
"svapadāni ca vaṇyante" asya samanvayo yathā nyāyakandalyāmū  "jyotsnā vivṛṇoti |
paśubādhavenetyādinā |"  "manas svapnena vivṛṇoti |
pratipipādayiṣitetyādinā |"  "anyārthe krayati |
yadanumeyenetyādinā |" "jyotsnā saṃyaha vākya vivṛṇotiviśeṣasya pratyakṣānumānayoratnabhāva darśayati |
" evamanekaśa sva yameva svapadāni varṇitānīti sarve samajhjasam ||
rājaśekharenāpāmarga nyāyakandalyauṭīkāyāṃ patrikākhyāyāṃ śabda mālāyāmārya bhāṣyaṭīkākāreṇa krame'kathāyita: |
"iha kila pūrveṣujijñāsābhyāvadarīkṛtāpamādāya manaye kathādāya sva yamīśvara udūjṛmbhaṇaprārī pratyakṣobhaya dṛṣṭyaguṇakamavidyāmanya veṣeṣasamprāyalakṣaṇaṃ  padārtheṣṭastuṣadideśa |
tadanu ca madarśilaukānukampayā paṭṭapadārthairahasyapraśu naparāni munīni racayāñcakāra |
teṣu mṛṣeṣu prathamaskandheśo bhārgava saṅkaduru thānmāviśya maheśapadaprakṛtipāhāradurupekṣitakṣetrave cakrāra |
tatra vanaiśa vṛtta yo nirvṛttā: |
ekā jyotsnānītyākhvā vṛtti jyotsnāvidyācāryo nṛpuṇya |
dvitīyāṃ  tu nyāyakandalyādhiṣṭhānāṃ nīyadāharo: ṣandadarai |
tṛtīyāṃ kilpāalaunāmno mudrayanācāryestanāna |
caturthīṃ tu jīvāsanotirdhyātāṃ nīlanyāsācāryo va-anya |
tataścāpi gambhīrāśīḥ satyeṣāṃ nyāyakandalīṃ prāktanābhyaḥ śrīdharadeva ityādi |
atra taturthe bhāṣyavyākhyāne tu uttarottarāpekṣayā pūrvapūrvasya prācīnatvamiti nāyamabhiprāyo rājaśekharasya "śukrāṃ śrīdakṣiṇīnāmnīṃ vṛtti"mityuktvāt prathamāmityanuktvādupasnigdhādarśitvāttu |
pūrvapūrvasya prācīnatvāpekṣe tu līlāvatyāṃ kandalyyāḥ kiraṇāvalyāścottaratvāt līlāvatyāpekṣayā prācīnatvepi śrīmatsītureḥ kandalyādiṣvanuddhārāt vaśeṣeṇyā prācīnatvaṃ na sambhavati |
tadevaṃ tāvat samudri kandalīkiraṇāvalyau, prācīnanavīnabhāvena vidhīyete ||
tatrodayanācāryo buddhinikaṣaṇāntameva kiraṇāvalīṃ nirmāya paralokamanaśchaditi śrīdharacāryaiḥ praśastapadbhāṣyavyākhyānasya samāptisiddhattra prathagnā-mnor nyāyakandalīnāmakaṃ sampūrṇa vyākhyānaṃ cakāra kandalīpanthasya kiraṇāvalyāṣchāyānupanyāsabuddhinikaṣaṇāntaṃ vyākhyānaṃ svayaṃ svataḥ paramavaiśeṣikena granthaḥ pūrṇatāṃ nīta iti buddhāḥ |
etadanuguṇyeva kiraṇāvalīmudrikārthaṃ śrīdharācāryopadeśā udayanācāryasya pūrvaśāstravyatthitvaṃ pratipāditamasmābhiḥ ||
vastutastu kiraṇāvalīmavalambya nyāyakandalīnirmāyāṃ vā nyāyakandalīmavalambya kiraṇāvalīnirmāṇamityubhayoḥ saṃvādenaiva sphuṭamavagamyateti pūrvapara-bhāvo nāvagamyate |
tathā ca kiraṇāvalyāṃ pramāṇatvenoddhṛtāni vacanānikandavyāṃ kiñcidu vyākhyākṛpaṇodāharaṇyante kiñcicca brūtakṛpeṇa udayamanvāgmanaṃ ca kandavyāṃ "nirayaikaḥ" ityākāreṇa dṛśyate tasmānnu kambhavati kiraṇāvalīmavalambya kandalīnamīkṣaṇam|
kintu kiraṇāvalyaiviyaṃ kandalīmanyasya śithilatvāt pūrve ṣaṣṭīkīnasya vyākhyānasya ghanerapi paścāt śithilavyā-khyānatanimīkṣaṇyāvāyogāmapyuktatvād śrīdharāvadhīnasya kiraṇāvalyāṃ khaṇḍanācca kandalīmavalambya kiraṇāvalīnimīkṣaṇaṃ kambhavati|
parantu yāvatparyantamekasminnuyatra prapañcasya tantvaśunaiva nāma nopalabhyate tāvat pūrvaparādvābhinīyaiḥ samyagbhinnairnāhainīti pañcabhiḥ pūrvaparaniścayābhāve 'pi krīdharācāryodayanākṣāpeyāyeṣa ca khadānakṣālādvayādhitvaṃ sambhavati tathā kiṃṇābaddhyā uparyadvāre pravṛjitvayāmaḥ |
śrīdharaṇṭācāryeṇa mātā cacchokṣā cacchoṃkṣā vā pitā baladevaḥ vaṅgadeśāparapayodhe goṅadeśe rāḍhāpurauḥ dakṣiṇabhāge bhūriṣṭhudrigrāme nivāsantāsīt |
ayaṃ ca pāṇabuddhārākhyena kāvyacchando'nu prāṇitaḥ cyātmakavaiśānarasavaṃśataśākābde (693) udbhū nyāyakandalīlekhyaṃ praṇītavāniti tadupanyāstisamayavailakṣyīyate tathāhi |
cārvīdṛṅviṇarāḍhāyāṃ dvijānāṃ puṇyakṣetrayām |
bhūriṣṭhudriti grāme bhūriṣṭhudrijanāśrayaḥ ||
kṛṣṇārādhaurvihasmādbhūva bilivandramā |
jagadānandacandrasyau vṛddhapatiriva dvijaḥ ||
tasmādviśuddhaguṇaratnamahāsamudro vidyālatāsamalambanabhūruhe 'bhūt |
svacchāśayo vividhadhītinadīpravāhaprasyandanotsavajalo baladevanāmā ||tattvābhramṛtiyaśo viṣṇuduṃkulasambhavā |
acchomityacintaguṇā śubhinī gṛhmedhinī ||
śacchāyasphūlakalavedī bahuśākhā dvijāśrayaḥ |
tasyāṃ śrīdhara ityucairdikṣvalpadumo 'bhavat ||
adyo vidyādidagdhānāmāmūtaṃ pravaṇocitam |
ṣaṭapadārthaikatāmenāṃ havirāṃ nyāyakandalīm ||
nyāyikaṭaśīnaratnaśatadhākāṇḍe (893) nyāyakandalīracitā |
śrīpāṇḍurākhyācintamaṭṭaśrīśrīdhareṇaiyam ||
śrīdharabhaṭṭācāryeracitā anye'pi granthā nyāyakandavyākhyānudṛtā yathā  anuyarviduḥ |
tattvabodho vā tattvaprabodhaḥ |
tattvabodhādinī |
saṃśaṭīkā ca |

dāṣu nirdeśaṃ|
dāṣu nadā nāmantyaṃ tadānantyaṃ dvidvatvasāmānya dvidvatvasāmānya dvidvatvasāmānya dvidvatvasāmānya nirmitakārūṇānirmitakārūṇādidripa nāgrādripa dvidvayupurājāna dvidvayupurājñāna guṇāṅkuṭṭirdinamya guṇāṅkuṭṭirdinaya kau tenvati kāryenvati saṃvarttakā samvarttakā saṃvidyā anya saṃvidyānya nyaṭṭudra nyaṭṭudre sakalyeva praṇaṭa sakalyeva pranaṭa syogyanyā syogyanyā dvidvakāle dvidvasāmānyudvikāle vipreṣaṇānukta vipreṣaṇānuktatvād‌ tvād‌ śāntye jau śāntyejau niṣame na niṣame na pravaya pravaya ānutyasamsane |

praśastapādabhāṣyam |

nyāyakandalīṭīkā |
anādinidhanaṃ devaṃ jagatkāraṇamīśvaram |
praṇamya satyasaṅkalpaṃ nityanirjñānanigraham ||
dhyānaikatānamanaso vigatapracārāḥ paśyanti yaṃ kṣayapi nirmalamadvitīyam |
jñānātmane vighaṭitakhilabandhanāya tasmai namo bhagavate puruṣottamāya ||
śāstrārambhe praṇamato devatāṃ śāstrasya praṇetāraṃ guruṃ ca stautīkṣasya pūrvoddeśaṃ namasyati |
praṇamyeti|
kamīrambhe hi devatā guruśca namaskriyante iti śiṣṭācāro'yam |
phalaṃ ca namaskārasya vighnopaśamaḥ na sākṣātpramāṇaḥ prakṣābuddhinugṛhītvāt |
anyaprakṣo'pi na kamīrambhe niyamenānugṛhītaiva avighna prārimbhanaparisamāptostadānīmanugṛhītatvāt phalonnatasthānabhivahitatvāt |
nanu kiṃ namaskārādeva vighnopaśamaḥ utā'nyasmādapi bhavati na tāvannamaskārādevetyasti niyamaḥ avaśyāpi namaskāre nyāyamīmāṃsābhāṣyādauḥ parisamāptvāt yadā cānyasmādapi tadā niyamenopādānaṃ viṣayapatikṣam |
abodhyate |
namaskārādeva vighnopaśamaḥ kamīrambhe śaktinirṇayena tasyopādānāt |
na ca nyāyamīmāṃsābhāṣyakārabhyāṃ na kṛto namaskāraḥ kiṃ tu savajñānibaddhaḥ kathayeva pratītiriti cet kṣataṃ: śiṣṭaprayogeva cakṣu vā sākṣadevaḥsaṭīkabhāsyārthapāṭhamāhe  prakṣābādhaṃ dvaiṣṭyepi sādhubhutaurapi karmasu na pravartate yāvidyuktaṃ namasyati  yadīmau paramārtinikau panijnajaghastvāminau nānunigṛhya ityasambhāvanāni dam|
yadvarāyau vyākhyate prapaśyati|
prakṛtedarśinā praśnena bhaktipradvāni śramapūrvakaṃ namaskāramātraṃ sa hi dharmotpādakīrtirapyasmatparāyaṇojaṃ nāpara:  ata eva kṛtanamaskārasyāpi kādambaryāderaparisamāptiṃ viśiṣṭanamaskārabhā vāp tadvaiśiṣṭyasya kṣīyamāṇatvāt acaiva ca namaskāra: kriyamāṇopi keri ṣyamāṇapadārthasaṃsevahaprabandhanopekṣayā pūrvakālabhāvīti jñāpityarthenāmiyojate  tadekavākyatāmāpādyitum na tvasya pūrvakālatāmācakṣānamanudyate anuvāde vā  prayojanābhāvāt|
hetumiti nirviśeṣaṇena hetupadena sarvatvaniyatāṃ nimittatā  pratijñānīte|
īśvaramiti viśiṣṭadevatāyāḥ abhidhānam loke lokapratyave kṣāsya padasya prasiddheḥ lokasiddhārthopasaṃgrahatvādsya śāstrasya|
munimiti  śuddhātmajñānapradīpacitanamasamanyuyatapaśca śabdāduṣattvāvavodheyuktam pṛṣa kṣinupemāha ityasman paścārthe muniśabdasya loke darśanāt|
kṛpādamiti labhya  kṛpāto vṛnimunimuno rathyāniyatinālisaṅulakṣaṇānādyaya pratyahaṃ kṛtārha datvamimāṃ pūjāṃ ata eva niravakrāpāḥ kṛpāp vā bhaktyatvityupalakṣaṇasambhava lāp|
idaṃ hi tasya nāmeti tacchabdavaśakīrtanaṃ kṛtaṃ praśastadevena na tviyaṃ  tadupanibandhaviśiṣṭaprādhyāpanāya yuktirabhihitā tadupanibandhaviśiṣṭasya mantra diśabdaghaṭanahājanaparigrahādeva pratīteḥ na cāsya kṛpādaśāstrasyānanena  kiṃcidupayojanamasti tāvatā tatuṣarvikasya yantrasya vaiśiṣṭyasiddhiriti cet  avaśyaṃ tatpūrvakatvena svayantrasya vaiśiṣṭyasiddhiḥ kartavyetyupāyārthosyāpi  nibandhasya sambhavanāyadatvāt sambhāvitaprāmāṇye praśastadeve puruṣadeṇāṇā sambhava iti cet pūrvaṃ siddhaṃ yathā kṛpādadarśinā saṅkṣipyāṇāṃ puruṣapratyayo deva tadyātmaniṣṭhatāt tadanunibandhe pravṛtirapūrvā ca puruṣāntarasaṃvādādeva  praśastadevakṛto(3)panibandhopa saṅkṣipyāṇāmadarśoṃ ca pravṛttinirvighyatīti  nāśestatpūrvaṃkasvadhyānanena kimaya siddhaṃ kṛpādevanamaskāraḥ vidyāprabhāvopetyu-kratītyanupūrvītyatra tasya samanantaralakṣaṇānāṃ svābhāvikayā: ṣaḍguṇāsmaṇāthanaur ṣaḍguṇārthaṃ pratyagātmatvātmasādanañcedvāt kiṃ ca pūrveṣaṇā: svarasaniveśā:  śrayamapūrvo jāta: dhantānubandho na vidyate bandhyamokṣau caikādhikaraṇau viṣayame dena vartate ya eva ca pravartate prāpya ca nivṛtto bhavati |
prakṛtipuruṣavivekadya nāduparatāyāṃ prakṛtau puruṣasya svahṛdayāvasthānaṃ mokṣa ityucyate |
tatra |
pravṛtti svabhāvāyā: prakṛteredāsīnyāyogāt puruṣārthanibandhayā tasyā: pravṛtti: vive kasyātisva puruṣārthe tasyāṃ sujñātāyāṃ sā nivartate kṛtakṛtyaitvāditi cetta  kasyā acetanāyā vipuṣyakāritvābhāvāt yathaivaṃ kṛtepi śabdādupakrame puna stathaiva pravartate tathā vivekasyāto kṛtasāyāmapi punastathaiva pravartiṣyate svabhā vasyānapāyitvāt |

pārthivaparamā ṇuhrādisvatantrena vyādhivārāt |
ayarīraṃ vāva santo(1) priyāpriye na spṛśata  ityādayo vedāntā: pramāṇamiti tu vayam |
śrutāthenaviṣayapramāṇasyasad  iti cenna pratyakṣeṇāvedāntikatvāt |
atha mataṃ śrutāthenaviṣadakaṃ vacanamanuvā dakaṃ syāt tataścāpramāṇatvaṃ pramāṇāntarahapekṣatvāt pramāṇaṃ sādhakatamatvā bhāvāditi na vidvāṃścetipādakatvamanuvādakatvaṃ pratyakṣādyanuvādakatva prasaṅgāt kiṃ tvadhigatārthigantṛtvaṃ hedupuruṣa vedāntānāmayo yadyaṃ śrutopi  pratyakṣādyai: pramāṇāntarasya na viṣaya: kṛtasteṣāmanuvādakatā kṛtasya saṃśaya tvam smṛteriva tebhya: pūrvoṣigamasaṃśayenāpekṣiterabhāvāt ata eva puruṣavā kyamapi pramāṇam nahi tadapi śaktaprāmāṇyetyāpattenāthe pratipādyati kintva napekṣitavadvāpāraṃ svayameva utpattimātra eva tadupekṣaṇāt svābhāviko hi  padānāṃ padārthaparatā svābhāvikī ca padāthonāmākāṅkṣādisannidhiyettāvatā mitaretarānvayogyatā tena yathā vede pramāṇāntarānapekṣa: śabda: śabdaśāstra-dravyagandhe uddeśyaprakaraṇam |
ghrādivārthātyaya śabaṃ laukiki ve laukikā vaidikāñca śaba vācyā iti nyāyoktamayāṇi śabdaśakteraviśeṣāt vastugrāmākhyānusaraṇaṃ tu svarūpaviṣayo'vahetodoṃṣvyādyābādhagamyāya pratyakṣe iva svakāraṇamyudeśagamo vipayo'sadbhūtanivavārthe ityeṣa dik |
vistarastvaduyamiduho dṛṣṭavya: |
nanu kāryeye śabdasya prāmāṇyaṃ na svarūpe śududhyavahāre'svanyavyatirekābhyāṃ kākṣeptviṣu padānāṃ śaktyavagamāt kṣato vedāntānāṃ na svarūpaparatveti cet vāntavidaṃ svarūpaparatvāpi vākyasya loke prayogadarśanāt yathā pariṇāmasukhamātre pariṇativirasya ca pravartaniti |
yadyapi pravṛttinivṛtyorūpadeśaḥ śabe hi vākyārthaḥ pariṇāmasukhasamāpte bhavaya pariṇativirasya ca mābhavediti na caimaṇyot surasatvaprāptautyeva svayamabhijñāṣāta puruṣa: pravartate virasatvamanītyeva dveṣādivartate kā tatra śabdasāmarthyemāvini upadeśāpekṣā agre hi śāstramayevadvadati |
aya pravṛttinivṛttyorathisandhānenāsya vākyasya prayogāt tādṛśyemiti cet asti pravṛttinivṛttyathetā kiṃ tu janakatvāt tu pratipādakatvena yasmādutātheviṣaya eva prāmāṇyaṃ yadi tu pravṛttinivṛttyorathisandhānena vākyaprayogāt tatho'satīyamānayorapi śābdatā āśrayabodhotpādanīnā tṛptiyotpādanaṃ ca vākyārtho syātāṃ pratyakṣasya ca kāñcidarthakriyāmabhisandhāyopalabdhite viṣaye pravṛttyādyarthakriyā prameyā syāt janakatvena pravṛttiparatvaṃ vedāntānāmapi vidvāṃte tebhyaḥ svarūpapatītau dhyātvādyāsādipravṛttasya vigataviṣayavikalpaviśuddhātmajñānodaye satyapavargotya bhāvāt na caidamāvaśyakaṃ pratyavṛttinivṛttyarthatvakaḥ pramāṇavyāpāra iti tathāḥ pūrvecchāpratibadudhyo'nutpādepi vastuparicchedyamarthiṇāmvāṅmukhaiḥ paryevasānāt na ca kākṣeptviṣu yathārthe padānāṃ śaktiḥ ananviteṣu vyutpattidarśanāt yathaiva prokṣitrakṣamalaudare madhuni madhukaraḥ piteti vartamānāpadeśe prāgbhūtarūpadārthe udbhaviddhamadhukarapadārtheṣu ye madhupāne kartāraṃ paśyati taṃ madhukaravācyatvena pratyeti anyāthatvi pāramparyeṇa kākṣeptveṣu vākyaprayoktuḥ śududhyavahāre kākṣeptviṣapadārthe madhukarapadasya vyutpattimāvāñditi cet aniścayāt |
vākyaprayoktuḥ kiṃ śududhyavahārāt kākṣeptvatvaye vyutpattirbhavet kiṃvā prasiddhapadavāmānādhikaraṇenopadeśādā svarūpeṇe iti niścaye nāsti idaṃśayamatāyāṃ abhāvātkiṃ ca prayogakariṣyate vyutpattiḥ proktasaṃbandhite anyathānyathānyā na śabdārthe pratyeti tattvaṃ madhukaraśabdavyāniścitārthatvamanvitārthatvaṃ ca puruṣabhedenetyathaiveṃṣavaśamāgatatvāt kriyākāṃkṣānibandhanaḥ padārthīnāmanyonyasaṃbandhaḥ tātparyātapadārthahetuṣu vedāntavākyeṣu bhaviṣyamaharṣīti cet tātparyavaśeṃ kriyayā abhāvaḥ yatra tu nāsti tatrāpadasaṃghātarūpatyā padairabhihitānāṃ padārthānāmeva yogyatāvaśiṣṭatvāmanyonyākāṃkṣānibandhanaḥ saṃbandhaḥ tathā ca kāṣṭhyādidāno cikṣuvanatilako rājā ityatrāpi vākyārtho gamyate eva athavā tatra atuprayujyamānāstiśabdatirodhānibandhano bhaviṣyatīti yadikiṃcidetat prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ |
atra padārthayarmavācakadeva padārthānāmapi saṃgraho labhyate svātantryeṇa dharmāṇāṃ saṃgrahābhāvāt ||
nanu padārthadharmāṇāṃ saṃgrahaparo grantho mahodayaheturiti dyoṣyadate śabdānāmathaipratipādanamantareṇa kāryotpattyabhāvādityāśaṅkhya padārthadharmaparītihetoḥ saṃgrahasya pāramparyeṇa mahodayahetutvaṃ pratipādayannāha ||
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyānāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ (2) padārthānāṃ sādharmyavaidharmyatattvajñānaṃ(3) niḥśreyasahetuḥ ||
dravyaguṇetyādi |
dravya ityukteno yo bhāvastasya sattvaṃ sādharmyaṃ dharmaḥ sādharmyaṃ yathādharmyaṃ dharmo vaidharmyaṃ sādharmyavaidharmyaṃ eva sattvaṃ tatharmyavaidharmyatattvaṃ tasya jñānaṃ niḥśreyasahetuḥ |
viṣayasambhogajaṃ sukhaṃ tāvat vaṇikādīnāṃ duḥkhabahulaṃ svargopapadyamānapi sādharmyaṃ sātiśayaṃ ca tathā ca kasyacit svargīmanaparasya svargīrājyamu atastadveti śatarta pracyutiśaṅkhayā paramapuruṣārthāpādya duḥkhākāntaṃ na niścitaṃ śreyaḥ ātyantiko duḥkhanivṛtirasahavedananikhiladuḥkhāparamuhatvādaparātmanaśca niścitaṃ śreyaḥ tasya kāraṇaṃ dravyāditattvajñānam |
enena tatparyuktaṃ yaduktaṃ bhagavatena viśeṣaguṇanivṛttilakṣaṇā muktirityedupañcānna mithyate iti |
viśeṣaguṇocchede hi sati ātmanaḥ sakṛṣṭeṇāvasthānaṃ nocchedo nityatvāt |
na cāyamapuruṣārthaḥ samya-svatuḥkṣoparamasya paramapuruṣārthatvāt samastasukhāsāvādapuruṣārthetvamiti cet sukhasyāpi kṣayiṣṇutayā bahulāpatyanoktatyā ca sādhanasādhyatayā yatnaparikliṣṭatayā ca sadā duḥkhakrāntasya viṣamiśratvena naśvaro duḥkhapakṣe nikṣepāt |
kaiṣāṃ sādhyasādhanayoraikyamātmaparijñānamapravartakāraṇamityupacārāya dravyādīnāmiti sambandhaḥ |
dravyāṇi ca guṇāśca karmāṇi ca sāmānyaṃ ca viśeṣaśca samavāyaśceti vibhāgaṣaṭkānusāreṇāpi vivṛtaḥ |
uddeśyasya vibhāgaṣaṭkena samānaviṣayatvāt |
krādau dravyasyoddeśaḥ sarveṣāmarthena prādhānyāt |
guṇānāṃ ca karmaviśeṣya bhūyastvād dravyānantaramabhidhānam |
niyamena guṇānuvidhāyitvāt karmaṇāṃ guṇānantaramuddeśaḥ |
kṣaṇonnatatvāt sāmānyasya karmānantaramabhidhānam |
paścapadārthadhṛteḥ samavāyasya sarvaśeṣeṇābhidhāne prāpte viśeṣāṇāṃ madhye kathanam |
kṣamāvasya pṛthaganuddeśaḥ bhāvapadatantyāt tvabhāvāt |
dravyāṇādīni ṣaṭsvanye ṣaṭrau akṣāṇi sādhyasādhyadisādhanasya niḥśreyasabahutve kriyate dravyādijñānasya kṣayitve sādhyasādhanayoḥ svātantryeṇa jñānasādhāvāt ||
tattvairalaskhodanābhivyaktādumodeva ||
nanu yadi tattvajñānaṃ niḥśreyasabahutvasādhi dharmo na kāraṇaṃ tataḥ sucaritoyaḥ pratoṣyadayati: śreyasiddhiḥ sa dharma iti tatra āha |
tattvairalaskhodanābhivyaktādumodeveti |
tatiḥśreyasaṃ dharmodeva bhavati dravyāditattvajñānaṃ tasya kāraṇatvena niḥśreyasasādhanamityabhiprāyaḥ |
tattvato jñāteṣu bāhyadhyātmikeṣu viṣayeṣu doṣadarśanādiraktasya samīhānivṛttāvātmatattvasya tadayoni krameṇayukteḥ tatpratyāgādhavāsanāni ca śrutismṛtyuditānyarthaduṣṭitaprakṣālyanupādānasyātmajñānamabhyasyataḥ prakṛṣṭavaitṛṣṇye'kṣayamaukṣyaṃ iti paripakvāntaḥkāraṇasyātyantikaśarīravimokṣasya bhāvāt |
dṛṣṭe viṣayiṣāmahiṃkṣapaṭākṣādīnāṃ parityāgo viṣayadoṣadarśanapūrvakābhisandhyakṛtānivartakātmaviśeṣaguṇāt praśvāsāt tena śarīrādīnātmanyanikaḥ parityāgo viṣayadoṣadarśanapūrvakābhisandhyakṛtanivartakātmaviśeṣaguṇanimittaḥ vijñāta iti mokṣādhikāre vakṣyāmaḥ |
dharmopi kevalaṃ sākṣān niḥśreyasaṃ karoti yāvadośvarecchayā nānugṛhīte tenedamuktaṃbhāvā iti codanā īśvaracodanā īśvarecchāviśeṣaḥ ṛbhivyaktiḥ kāryārambhaṃ  pratyabhimukhya īśvaracodanayābhivyaktādīśvaracodanābhivyaktāttu īśvarecchāvi śeṣeṇa kāryārambhābhimukhīkṛtādubhayodeva niḥprayase bhavatīti vākyayojanā nadvaiti  cakāraḥ dravyādiśāstrodiṣṭānena ṣaṭ dharmasya niḥprayasahetukatvaṃ samucci noti |
evaṃ ṣaṭpadārthajñānasya muktathopāyatvaṃ pratītya teṣāṃ pratyekaṃ bheda jijñāsāyai paripṛcchati ||
atha ke dravyādayaḥ padārthāḥ kiṃ ca teṣāṃ vācyaṃ  vaidharmyaṃ ceti ||
tatra dravyāṇi pṛthivyamūtejovāyvākāśakāladi gātmamanāṃsi sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyoktāni(1) navaive ti(2) |
taduvyatirekeṇānyasya sattānabhidhānāt(3) ||
atha ke dravyādaya iti |
kāni dravyāṇi ke guṇāḥ kāni karmāṇī tyādi yojanīyaṃ nāṣyathaṃ dharmiṇi jñāte dharmo jñāyate iti tena dharmeṣu pṛthak  praśnaḥ kiṃ ca teṣāmityādi |
avāpi ca' samuccaye |
uttaramāha tatretyādi |
teṣu  dravyādiṣu madhye dravyāṇi pṛthivyādīni sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāya sāmānyasañjñayā  dravyasañjñayā viśeṣasañjñayā pratyekamaṣādhāraṇasañjñayā pṛthivyojastvādidravyo tkāni dvaṣakāreṇa pratipāditāni |
kimenāvantyehaikkidaparāṇyapi santītyāha  navaiveti |
nanu navanāṃ lakṣaṇābhidhāne sāmarthyodaparābhāvāvo jñātavyo vyartho  navaiveti |
na |
navasu lakṣiteṣu kimaparāṇamasattvādbhuta satāmapyanupayogitvānna  lakṣyaṃ ityamiti saṃśayo na nivarteta lakṣaṇasya vyavahāramātrasatyā sarvā nātmanajñātīyavyavacchedamātrasādhanatvena cānyābhāvapratipādanāsāmarthyāt  tadvyavasāyārtha kṛtamu idameva sāmarthyādṛṣṭānāṃ viśeṣasaṅkhyābhidhānaṃ tannā ntare bibhrāga iti nirdeśasya kasyate kathametadavagamya navaiveti ata āha |
nadvātirekeṇyādi |
tebhyo navabhyo vyatirekeṇa sarvetra mahāviṣā sarveśyāpa-dravyagandhe ḍaṭṭruprakāraṇam |
draidhāthaṃ pravṛttenānyasya yajñājabhidhānāt |
tamo nāma drupavdvyāpāramāṇupayakṣūparamāṇuparamāṇuyogābhimānabuddhyādyāntarasamstoti cedanna kaścidvāha |
yadi  tamodravye drupavaddravyasya sparśeṣyābhicārāt sparśavaddravyasya sahata: pratighātagharmatvāttamasi var̤arata: pratighanya: syāt mahānyakāre ca bhūyojakāśove  tadavayavapramāṇi khaṇḍakhaṇḍamavidyamāṇi pratīyeraniti |
tadyuktam |
yathā  pradīpādimateravamavedyamutrayādvudyamanasparśanirviḍābhayadharmānīyamānakhaṇḍādyavidyavyapi bhāvamātiṣātipramāṇavalamārabhyate tathā tama:paramāṇubhirapi tamodravyam |
tasmādanyathayā sabhāvīyate tama:paramāṇava: sparśavantastadrahitā vā na tāhasyapovanta: sparśavatstvakāryasya kaścidanupalambhāt adravyatvāpādābhāvāt sparśavaddravyāntarasambhavā iti cet drupavanto vāyuparamāṇavo 'dravyatvāpādoṣuṇyādrupavatkārye nārabhante iti kiṃ na kalpyate kiṃ vā na  kalpitametadekeṣānīyādeva paramāṇauradroṣapayahācaturatho kāryāṇi jāyante iti |
kāryekaṣamayigamyā: paramāṇava: prathākāryeṣunīyante te na taddravyajanyā: pramā ṇābhāvāditi cet evaṃ tarhi tāmasa: paramāṇavo'pyasparśavanta: kathaṃ tamo dravyārambhedva sparśavatsvasya kāryadravyānārambhakatvenātmābhicāropalambhāt  kāryedarśanāt tadanuguṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ kalpyate na tu kāraṇavaiguṇyena draṣṭakāryaviparyāsa  yujyate iti cet na vayamanyakārasya pratyārthina: kiṃ tvātmakānupapattetaili mabhāvapratīteśca dravyamidaṃ na bhavatīti brūma: |
tarhi bhāvābhāva evāyaṃ pratī yeta na tasya nīlākāreṇa pratibhāsāyogāt sakhyandanepi dūragamanāmopākhyā pino nīlimnaśca pratīte: kiṃ ca gṛhakoṇe pratiyogini saṃyuktaviśeṣaṇatayā  labdhanyapratiyogyukhanābhāvo gṛhyete na svatantra: tamasi ca gṛhyamāṇe nānyasya  yasyamusti na ca pratiyodhamukha: pratyaya: tasmāttāmāṣedham |
na cālokādarśana mātramavetat bahirmukhatayā tama iti kathayati ca kṛṣṇākārapratibhāsanāt |
tasmā drupaviśeṣoyamasvantaṃ tejobhāve sati sarvatra: samāropitastama iti pratīyate |
divā cācche cakṣugolakasya nīlimnavabhāsa iti balavāsu: |
yadā tu niyata deśādhikaraṇo bhāvābhāvastadā dūraṣabhāvāropite nīlimni chāyetyavagama: |
ata eva dauṣau hṛṣṭvā mahatīṃ cchāyāyāṃ chāyetyabhidhāna: tadrucchāyāpi na:  nīlimna: pratīti: |
aprāvṛte ca bhāvayamārgātiśayepi duḥsapāda: taduktam |
naca bhāsābhāsasya tamastvaṃ bṛhadabhaṇatvam |
chāyāyā: kāṣṭhagatatvena ca purāṇau bhramaguṇate ||
dūrāsannadvaṭeṇādiyadarthapratyakṣatvāñceti |
dehānuvartinī chāyā na vastutvādinā bhavediti ||
durūpaṇatvañca kvacicchāyāyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣama: valatimattvayogi gandhatyāgato dṛśye yava yava tejasaubhāvastatva tatra hṛpopalabdhikṛta: |
yacca paratvādyeyogyatvayādiṣṭā: |
tatra cālokābhāvajjanīyahṛtiścaiṣe tamasi cālokānupadeśena cakṣu: sāmarthyaṃ tadanubhaviṣyāt yathālokābhāva eva  svarūpate |
nanvevaṃ tahi dravavirodha: dṛśyaguṇakarmaniyataniyamodābhāvastama iti na virodha: bhāvayai sati tamasa: pratītebhāvastama bandhutvam |
īśvaropi dṛṣṭe puṇyavādātmeva na tu praḍguṇārikaraṇasvatantreguṇātrikaraṇād guṇabhedena bhiyate muktātmabhiścemivāyate ||
guṇāśca rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāthaparimāṇapṛthaktvayogavibhāgaparatvāparatvabuddhisukhadu:khecchādveṣaprayatnāśceti kāṇadoktā: ṣoḍaśa |
ca śabdavasuddhitāstra gurutvadravatvasnehasaṃskārādṛṣṭaśabdā: vegetyevaṃ caturviśatiguṇā: ||
guṇā rūpādaya: kāṇādoktā iti sūtrakāreṇa śabdānukathitā rūparasatyādinā |
ca śabdānānuktā guṇatvena loke prasiddhā gurutvādaya: sma samadiṣṭā: |
evaṃ caturviṃśatireva guṇā: |
ye tu ṣoḍaśottarakāṇabhaṣṭādiṣṭayogādaya: te teṣāmantarbhūtanti |
śeṣaye balavatopi parasya parātmatayā pratyutsāha: ca ca prayavasiteṣa eva |
khanate dharmāyogatino budiraudāmyam |
paradu:khaharṣaṇecchā kāmaṇam |
tattvābhiniveśino buddhidaurgatyam |
śreyamātmanugrahaṇīyatvam ityevamādiḥ |
catuṣṭayaśabdena yathāyathayogaparyañgraha: |
saṃskāra iti sa ca vegasya  bhāvanāyāḥ sthitasthāpakasya cābhidhānam |
nanvevaṃ sahaupikyaṃ na saṃskāratvajātyākaiṇyā vegabhāvanāsthitasthāpakānāmaikatvāt |
evaṃ tahi na caturviṃśa-dravyatvaṃ cetyevamākārakam |
nivṛttamu |
adravyatvajñātyoryathā gharmedharmeyorekatvāt na adravyatvajñātyabhāvāt |
nirguṇo'pi guṇo'pyādhāraṇadharmeyogitvenopacārādudubuddhyaśetiriti vyavahāraḥ ||
utkṣepaṇāpakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇāgamanāni pañcaiva karmāṇi |
gamanagrahaṇādbuddhamanasoścanaścandanodhvairjvalanatiryakpatananamanodrumanādayo gamanaviśeṣā na jātyantarāṇi ||
karmāṇi vibhajate |
utkṣepaṇeti |
kiyanti tāni tatrāha |
pañcaiveti |
nanu bhramaṇādayo'pi santi kathaṃ pañcaivetyavadhāraṇamata āha |
gamanagrahaṇāditi |
gamanagrahaṇād pañcaiva karmāṇi |
anyeṣāmantarbhāva |
bhramaṇarecanaścandanetyādi |
yasmād bhramaṇādayo'pi gamanaviśeṣāḥ gamanaprabhedāḥ na jātyantarāṇi tasmād gamanagrahaṇenaiteṣāmapi grahaṇāt pañcaivetyavadhāraṇaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ ||
sāmānyaṃ dvividhaṃ paramaparaṃ cānuvṛttipratyayakāraṇam |
tatra paraṃ sattā mahāviṣayatvāt sā cānuvṛttereva hetutvāt sāmānyameva |
dravyatvādayaparasattvaviṣayatvāt |
tadvad vyāghraterapi hetutvāt sāmānyaṃ sadviśeṣākhyāmapi labhate ||
sāmānyaṃ kathayati |
sāmānyaṃ dvividhamiti |
dvaividhyameva kathayati |
paramaparaṃ ceti |
covayārthe paramaparamevetyarthaḥ |
tasya rūpaṃ kathayati |
anuvṛttipratyayakāraṇamiti |
anyatantyānugatānāṃ piṇḍānāṃ yataḥ kāraṇādanyonyasvarūpānugamaḥ pratīyate tatsāmānyam |
kiṃ tat paraṃ sāmānyamityāha |
paraṃ sateti|
atra yuktimāha |
mahāviṣayatvāditi |
dravyatvādipekṣayā bahuviṣayatvādityarthaḥ|
sā cānuvṛttereva hetutvāt sāmānyameva dravyatvādikaṃ tu svāśrayasyaśrutereva hetutvena sāmānyameva |
yadyapyevaṃ sāmānyādibhyo vyāvartate tathāpi na tebhyaḥ svārthaṃ vyāvartayituṃ śaknoti teṣāmapi svarūpasattāśabdārthatvena tvāt |
baddhamedhayā cānuvṛttihetutvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tenāmāvādyānuvṛttihetutvepi na doṣaḥ yatpramāṇena pratīyate tatvāhnika vyavahāro lokānāṃ viṣayeṣu tu nāstīti nena pramāṇamanyeva khatīti kecit |
tadayuktam |
pramāṇaikatvataḥ prāg bahutve 'pyabhāvādvaidavattvañca kharaviṣāṇasyeva grāhyatvābhāvādanyānyaismayāpatteśca |
itaḥ pramāṇasya grāhyatve sattāyāḥ pramāṇasādhyatātkṣaṇasya ca grāhyasya pramāṇasyāpi grāhyāntarānusaraṇenānavasthānāpātāt |
atra mate na heyaḥ pramāṇasadṛvyayaḥ saneti kiṃ tu pramāṇaśabdasya yatheyarthaṃ vastusvarūpameva ghaṭā yopi sattāsāmānyamicchanti teṣāmapi ghaṭāderekarūpatvānupapatteḥ niḥsvabhāve sarvavinā śādau ghaṭādaya asambhavāyām sarvaṃ kaiśidyāstu kiṃ sambhoti |
carcācaryate |
pratyekaṃ ghaṭāderekarūpaṇi bhinnāni kathaṃ tairekākārapratītiḥ ekaśabdapravṛtti ścānneṣu sambandhagrahaṇābhāvāt |
atha teṣvekaṃ nimittamasti siddhaṃ naḥ samī hitam |
yathā dṛṣṭaikagopiṇḍasya piṇḍāntare pūrvedṛganukāriṇo buddhirudeti naivaṃ mahīdharādiṣu stheṣyatupalambhamātrasya pūrvoktānukārātprāgbodhastīti kutotra sāmā nyakalpaneti cet kiṃ mahīdharādiṣu nirvikalpakānubhavo nāsti uta sādyāpi na vidyate yadi nirvikalpakānubhavābhāvāttu sāmānyāprāptyākhyānaṃ tarhi gauravamapi pratyākhyeyam teyoḥ śābaleyaśābaleyayoḥ sarveṣāṃ sādṛśyābhāvāt |
atha sādya yāpi svarūpānubhavo nāsti tadasiddhum sarveṣāmapi teṣāmabhāvavitkalpena hṛpaṇa tulyatāpattiprasaṅganāt |
ityādi viśeṣaiḥ |
gopiṇḍeṣu bhūtaditi tajjātīyatā buddhau'pyathamavasāmānyānugamām mahīdharādiṣu tu vilambino stotrādyavasā mānyānugamena jāteranudṛttatvāt yathā maṇikārthenācchurāve muktātatibuddhiḥ |
yatenārthakriyākāritvamapi sarvaṃ pratyuktam |
akṣatorthakriyāyāṃ abhāvādarthakri yārthaṃ ca sattvaṃ tasya sattvādarthakriyāyāścārthakriyāpekṣayā sattvenānavasthāne sarveṣāmasattvāpasaṅgāt |
dravyatvādyāpatteḥ |
dravyatve guṇatve karmatve cārthaṃ sattāpe kṣayāsyāviṣayatvādityarthaḥ |
tathā dravyatvādyapekṣayā pṛthivītvādikamaparyaṃ tada pekṣayā ghaṭatvādikamaparyaṃ guṇatvādyapekṣayā rūpatvādikamaparyaṃ karmatvādyapekṣayā śīghropakṣepādikaṃ vyākhyeyam |
jalabhūpatasya bahibhuṃpatamānasya nadītyanuyadābhāvādṛ dravyatvaṃ nāstīti kecit |
tadyasat |
dravye'pi tayoḥ svabhādhānyaina pratītisambhavāt svabhāvānyamatītireva dravyatvamatītiḥ |
uttveṇāditvapi calanātmakatāmatītirasti dhaiva ca krame'pratītiḥ hṛpādiṣu tu kṛtatvamapratyāntuṭliṅgakṣayakṣambhavādu guṇatvavyāptyabhāvyānaṃ vyaktiprahaṇamiva samaprayahaṇamapi tasya pratītikāraṇaṃ brāhmaṇatvacaitraiva yoniśabdavyañjanānaṃ tathāpi viśuddhabrāhmaṇaśatvanimittakayonyuttimātrabuddhamapi brāhmaṇatvaminindriyāṇanīnācaiṇa bandhiyādivilakṣaṇatayā ca gṛhyate atyantaṃ vyāptiśeṣādṛṣyenānuvṛttatvāt yadā tu mātāpitrostvapaṃśau ca cakṣurādayastadā viśuddhaṃ brāhmaṇatvamavaṣṭambhate tadā brāhmaṇo'sīti pratyakṣeṇaiva pratīyate tathāhi suviditarūparīkṣāśāstro raktajātibhedaṃ pratyakṣataḥ pratyeti nāpra: |
na ca tāvatā raktajātibhedo nāsti na ca tatsattvamapratyakṣaṃ yadyutaṃ strīṇāṃ svabhāvacapalānāṃ viśuddhidurekṣabodheti tadapyasamañjasam|
suśikṣitānāṃ sukarastadavabodhaḥ kathitaśca tāsāṃ bahuvidho raktapoṣāya ityādau tāsāmapyaktānupambhuḥ|
tatu dravyatvādikaṃ svaviṣayasya vijātīyebhyo'pi vyāvṛttihetutvāt(1)dṛḍhaprokṣyā viśeṣapañcakānapi labhate na kevalamanuvṛttihetutvāt sāmānyaśaṅkā labhate vyāvṛtterapi hetutvādviśeṣaśaṅkāmapi labhate ityapiśabdayogaḥ |
kimutaṃ syāt dravyatvādiṣu sāmānyaśabdo mukhyo'nuvṛttihetutvasya sāmānyalakṣaṇasya sambhavāt |
viśeṣaśabdaśca bhāṣye svāśrayo viśiṣyate sarvato vyavacchidyate yena sa viśeṣa iti lakṣaṇatvenopi bhāvāt idaṃ tu lakṣaṇamanyaviśeṣeṣvasti ||
nityadravyavṛttayo'ntyāḥ(2) viśeṣāḥ|
te(3) khastvaityantavyāvṛttihetavaḥ(4)tvānniḥśeṣa eva(5) ||
nityadravyavṛttayo'ntyā viśeṣā iti |
nityadravyeṣveva varttante aṇvo ye te viśeṣā iti |
nityadravyeṣveti dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyānāṃ vyavacchedaḥ dravyaguṇakarmāṇi dravyeṣveva varttante na nityeṣveveti|
sāmānyāni tu na dravye-leva na nityaleva cetanne bhavati buddhiśabdādīnāṃ vyavacchedaḥ teṣāṃ samasta-nityadravyātmabhāvāt |
nanu kiṃ viśeṣaḥ eva kiṃ vā dravyatvādivadrūpyarūpā iti saṃdehaḥ |
te khalviti |
khaluśabdo niścaye nityadravyadharmayo ye viśeṣāste viśeṣā eva niścitaṃ na tu sāmānyānyapi bhavantītyarthaḥ |
atyantaṃ sarvadā dhyākuteṣu svāśrayeṣvetarasāhacaryakeraseva hetutvācca kāraṇatvāditi |
yathā vede teyopariṣṭādṛdrāhaṇadanīyam |
śrutasiddhānāmācāryodhārabhūtānāṃ yaḥ sambandha ihapratyakṣahetuḥ sa samavāyaḥ ||
samavāyasvarūpaṃ nirūpayati |
śrutasiddhānāmiti |
śrutasiddhiḥ pṛthak-siddhiḥ pṛthagavasthitirayorapi sambandhinoḥ parasparavihāreṇa pṛthagāśrayā-śritve vā yayonāsti tāvaśrutasiddhau tayoḥ sambandhaḥ samavāyaḥ yathā tantu-paṭayoḥ |
yadyapi tantavaḥ paṭavyatiriktāśraye samavayanti tathāpyubhayoḥ paraspara-vihāreṇa pṛthagāśrayāśritvaṃ nāsti paṭasya tantuṣevāśrayitvāt |
yatra tu dravyo-rapi sambandhinoḥ parasparavihāreṇa vyatiriktāśrayāśritvaṃ tatra yutasiddhiḥ |
yathā tvagindriyaśarīrayoḥ śarīraṃ hi tvagindriyaparihāreṇa pṛthagāśraye svāśraye samāśritaṃ tenānyayoḥ saṃyogo na samavāyaḥ |
nityānāṃ tu yutasiddhiḥ pṛthaga-sthitiḥ pṛthagavastheyatvā vā yayonāsti tāvayutasiddhau tayoryaḥ sambandhaḥ sa samavāyo yathākāśaguṇadravyatvayoriti |
ayutasiddhayoḥ sambandha ityucyamāne dharmeṣu sakhasya ca yaḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvalakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ sopi samavāyaḥ prāpno-ti tayorātmakāryinayorayutasiddhaprabhāvāt tadarthamācāryodhārabhūtānāmiti|
padaṃ na tvakāśādīnāmayutasambandhinityadravyanirūpyatvenapyutasiddhipadenaiva tasya nivartitatvāt |
evamaprākāśasyākāśapadasya ca vācyavācakabhāvaḥ samavāyaḥ syāt tanivṛttaye-mihaparatyakṣaheturiti vācyavācakabhāve hi tasyācchedāt tadartho jñāyate na tvi-hedamiti |
ācāryodhārabhūtānāmihaparatyakṣaheturiti kṛtsnadeśasambandho na vyava-cchidyate tadarthamayutasiddhānāmiti |
atha kecidayutasiddhipadaṃ vikalpayanti kiṃ yuto na siddho āhosvidyuto viddho yadi yuto na viddho kasyayoḥsambandho dharmiṇorbhāvāt |
athāpyanto khiddho tathāpi ka: sambandho'pṛthaksiddhasvādeva bhinnayorhi sambandho yathā kuṇḍabadarayoriti |
tadapare na pṛthagni |
na hyayamapṛthakṣo yuto na khiddho na niyamāneti asato: sambāyānambhūpagamāt |
nāpyāsyāmaye: ayuto siddhuriti ekātmakatve dvaikameva vastu vyācopyam parasparātakatvyābhāvalakṣaṇādanupraveśapatāyā: |
na ca tadevaṃ vastu paramārthata: parasparavilakṣaṇena dvayeṇa tathorākārayo: pratibhāsanāt vilakṣaṇākāradvayupadarśanasyaiva bhedalakṣaṇatvāt |
anyathā bhedābhedavyavasthānupapatte: |
tasmāt svarūpabhedepyutākhiddhi: kiṃ sppyatikhiddhānāmiti parasparāpekṣārūpa pṛthagaprayonātmikānātmityartha: |
tathā ca sati sambandho nānupapanna: svarūpabhedasya sambhavādinacoktu parasparāpekṣatvasya dahanārtha:piṇḍayoriva hinā sambandhanāsambhavāt |
idānīṃ viśeṣo vaktirucyate: prakṛtyādaya:piṇḍena saha sambadyante iha tu svakāraṇasamaye'nupajāyamānameva saba sambadyate yathā kṣitikriyā chevenetyalam |
nanu kimaye paṃcava padārtho ḍitraṣa: nāpare tebhyo'nve bhāvāt tadanyeṣāmaprabhāvācca navyabhāvasya saṃve: savepramāṇairanupalabhyamānatvācchūnyaviṣayatvat |
ṣaṣṭhāṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ viśiṣṭātmakaviṣayatvam |
vyāhataṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ yathā guṇāśrayo dravyam |
sāmānyavānaguṇa: saṃyogābhimānyormadhyo na kāraṇaṃ guṇa: |
ekadravyamaguṇaṃ saṃyogābhimānayoranapekṣakāraṇaṃ karma |
anubhūnimātrayakāraṇaṃ sāmānyam |
atyantavyāvṛttiheturiviśeṣa: |
ayutikhiddhayorāśrayāśrayibhāva: samavāya iti ||
sarve dharmevinā dharmiṇāstuddhya: kṛta: ||
anudravyaiṣu dharmeṣu dharmo na śakyante vaktumato dharmeṇāmadeśe prakṛmiṇatuṃ bahutvaṃ pradarśayati |
evamiti |
sarve pūrvoktena yanthena dharmairavinā dharmīn parityajya dharmiṇāstuddhya: kṛto dharmiṇāṃ saṅgrāmeṇa saṃkṣepenaiva kṛtamidānīṃ dharmo uddiśyante iti bhāva: |
yadyapi pūrve dravyādīnāṃ vibhāga: kṛtastathāpyuddeśa: kṛta ityuktaṃ vibhāgasya nāmadheyasaṃkīrtanamātreṇaiva uttarṣatvāt ||ṣathaṇānāmapi padārthāśāsatrasiddhatvāt(1)bhidheyatvaceytvāni ||
yadyapi dharmaḥ padapadārthobhyo na vyatiricyate hi, na te yathānyonyā-pekṣayā dharmo yadi taccha up-nītiḥ |
tathāpi saiṣāṃ dharmidharmatvayā parijñānārthaṃ pṛthaguddeśaṃ karoti |
padamabhidhīni |
śaktitvaṃ svadṛdṛṣṭatvaṃ śakyaprāpi sādhayatye yasya vastuno yatsvarūpaṃ tadeva tathāvidhatvum |
abhidheyatvamāpi cabhidhānā-tipādanayogyatvaṃ tattu vastunaḥ svarūpmeva,2) bhāvasvarūpamevāvasthābhedena ceya-tvamabhidheyatvaṃ cetyāde ||
āśritatatvaṃ cvānyatra(2) niruddhyobhayaḥ ||
āśritatvaṃ ca paratantratvenopalabhyaḥ na samavāyapratyakṣā iti: samavāye tadyabhāvāt ida cāśritatvaṃ vastuviśeṣu paramāṇuṣu ākāśakāladigātmamanaḥsu nāstītyāha |
anyatra nityadravyebhya iti |
ye tu dharmāt vyatiriktānicchanti teṣāmekatvāt samastavastuvyāptivyastītipakṣaprahelakatvamidaṃ kalpite dadvyā-diṣu dhanādyeṣyat aśritatvaṃ pratibaddhu micchate tadā tatkālpatvanaivaiṣeṣāṃ sattāyāḥ svadṛpatāvāyacca vaditi pratyayopapatteḥ yeṣāṃ tu bhāvasvarūpamevāśritatvaṃ na teṣāṃ vyathā dhanā svarūpatvādupunipattyagahetutvābhāvāt nāśritatvamanathaikaṃ niḥsvarūpaṃ dhanāyāḥ samavāyābhāvādityapyanupapadyate ||
dravyādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ(4) samavāyitvasamavetatvaṃ ca ||
dravyādīnāṃ viśeṣāntānāṃ ṣādhamyaṃ sādhayati |
samavāyitvaṃ samavāyala-kṣaṇā vṛttiḥ |
anekatvaṃ parasparādibhinnatvamitaretaravyāvṛttaṃ svarūpameva |
dravyā-dīnāminyukte samavāyopi gṛhyeta taddharme pañcānāmityuktam |
pañcānāmityukte ca kaipāmiti na jñāyate tadarthaṃ dravyādīnāmiti ||
guṇādīnāṃ pañcānāmapi nirguṇatvaniṣkriyatve ||
guṇādīnāṃ samavāyāntānāṃ ṣādhamyaṃmāha |
guṇādīnāmiti |
nirguṇatvaṃ guṇābhāvaviśiṣṭatvaṃ niṣkriyatvaṃ kriyābhāvaviśiṣṭatvaṃ yathā bhāṣe|abhāvasya viśeṣaṇaṃsvāviśiṣṭaprayayajananāveśamatropi tathā doṣanibandhumadhyaṃ dhūnulamiti |
bhāvabhāvayorasambandhāt ayamabhāvo viśeṣaṇamiti cet nāsti bhāvadeya viśiṣṭaprayayaḥ tadarthatvāt sambandhamapi kalpayiṣyāmaḥ |
yadi sambaddhameva viśeṣaṇaṃ manyate ||
dravyādīnāṃ cayāṇāmapi(1) sattāsambandhaḥ rāmaḥnyāviśeṣabatvaṃ svasamayārtheśabdābhidheyatvaṃ dharmodharmekartṛtvaṃ ca ||
dravyādīnāṃ cayāṇāṃ sattāsambandhaḥ sattayā sāmānyena sambandhaḥ samavāyamukho dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ dhāraṇyam |
yathā caiteṣu(2) sattāsambandhastathopapāditam |
idaṃ tviha nirūpyate |
ki sattāsambandhaḥ sato'sato vā sataścetu prāpta sattāsambandhāt sattvenāsaṅge iti vyāgho sattā yathāsattaḥ sambandhaḥ kharaviṣāṇādiṣvapi sattā syāt nityeṣu tāvanyathāpattamāvānnupapannaḥ anityeṣu prāgabhāva eva sattākāryasāsamarthāt |
na ca kharaviṣāṇādiṣvatiprasaṅgaḥ tadutpatto kasyacitsāmagrīmabhāvāt |
anyadapi sādhamyaṃ dravyādīnāṃ cayāṇāṃ ayamiti |
sāmānyaviśeṣabatvaṃ ceti |
anuvṛttivyāvṛttihetutvātu sāmānyaviśeṣā dravyatvādayasteṣāṃ svaha sambandho dravyādīnāṃ sa va samavāya eva |
svasamayatheśabdābhidheyatvaṃ ceti |
vaiśeṣikaiḥ svayaṃ vyavahāraya yaḥ saṃketaḥ kṛto'stin śāstre 1śabdādu dravyaguṇakarmāṇi pratipattavyāni |
tena dravyādīni trīṇi nirūpaṇavacanīyaśabdainocyante |
dharmodharmakartṛtvaṃ ceti |
dharmodharmayorādhānimittatvaṃ cayāṇāṃ yathā hi pṛthivīdeva dīyamānāyāsnupamāṇā ca dharmādharmayoḥ kāraṇam |
yaśca: saṃyogo draveḥ kāraṇaṃ yathā kapilāsyayā narāsyaccharīrasya |
evaṃ kreāyumayakārya yathā saumyamarma cātiḍajagupsamanaṃ ca evamanyadapyuhyam |
dharmādharmakartṛtvamitityākhyānameva dharmādharmajananaṃ prati teṣāṃ nijā śaktirucyate |
nanu jātirapi trayoḥ kāraṇaṃ na tasyāḥ svāśrayavyaktyedamo ya paritāṣyatvāt ||
kāryatvānityatve kāraṇavattāveva ||kāryetvānityatve kāraṇatāvacchede |
yeṣāṃ dravyādīnāmanityanikāraṇamasti teṣāṃ kāryeṣamanityatvaṃ ca dharmo na saṃśayāmityarthaḥ |
svakāraṇo samavāyaḥ prāgabhāvaḥ ghanādasamayoga vā kāryatvamityukte |
tadyuktam |
pracchede sadyasā-bhātu tasmāt kāraṇādhīnaḥ svātmalābhaḥ kāryatvamiti lakṣaṇaṃ vyāpakatvāt |
prakṛtvaṃśabhāvepalakṣitā vastunaḥ satvevānityatvamiti kecit |
tadyuktam |
aniteḥ śranitya iti kriyāśīrṣavaṃ lokaḥ pratyeti na tu khalādivadguṇāt |
utpattivināśayogitvamityapare: |
tadvyāptāsam |
prāgabhāve utpattistvabhāvāt tasyā-thanityatvena loke prasiddhipaiḥ tasmāt svadhvaṃsavināśa yatkāraṇatvamiti |
yatho-ktam |
kaṃcityatve vināśārthāya kriyāsāmānyamucyate iti |
yadyapi vināśo vastukāle nāsti tathāpi pramāṇāntarasiddhadhvaṃsabodho bhavatyeva viśeṣaṇam anityo ghaṭa iti pratyukterityāt tathā loke vināśi śarīramaśruvā nityayā iti ||
kāryatvaṃ cānyatra pārimāṇḍaltyādibhyaḥ(1) ||
kāryatvaṃ cānyatra pārimāṇḍaltyādibhya iti |
pārimāṇḍaltyamiti para-māṇuparimāṇamuṃ ādiśabdād dryaṇukaparimāṇamu ākāśakālādigātmanāṃ vibhutva-matyantamahattvamanaḥ:parimāṇaṃ paratvāparatve dviṣṭhaprakṣamantyākṣayaviparimāṇaṃ cetyādi grāhyam |
etāni parityajyāpareṣāṃ dravyādīnāṃ cayāṇāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ samavāyya-samavāyikāraṇatvaṃ yadyapi dravyasya nāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ na ca samavāyikāraṇatvaṃ guṇakarmaṇostathāpi nimittakāraṇatvamalaṃkāratayedaṃ sādharmyamuktam ||
dravyatvitvaṃ cānyatra nityadravyebhyaḥ ||
dravyatvitvaṃ cānyatra nityadravyebhya iti |
nanvaśritatvaṃ ghaṭaṇāmityuktaṃ tenedaṃ punaruktaṃ na punaruktaṃ dravyopalakṣinatyāśritatvasyāp vihitatvāditi kathyate |
tadyuktam |
sāmānyādīnāmapi dravyopalakṣitatyāśritatvasya sambha-vādevaṃ dravyādiṣayasādharmyakathanaṃ syāt tasmādidamaṃ vyākhyeyam anyatra nitya-dravyebhya iti dravyalakṣaṇamupalakṣaṇaṃ tadanyeṣāntyā viśeṣāstepi grahyante |
nitya-dravyāṇi tadanyeśca viśeṣāṇaṃ parityajya dravye yattraśitatvaṃ dravyādīnāṃ cayāṇāṃ sādharmyaṃ nāpareṣāmitiyaiḥ ||dravyānye bādhyambaidharmyekāryāt |
sāmānyādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ(1) svātmasattvaṃ buddhilakṣaṇatvasakāryatvasakāraṇatvamasāmānyaviśeṣavattvaṃ nityatvamarthaśabdānabhidheyatvaṃ ceti ||
samapti sāmānyādīnāṃ bādhyammevāha |
sāmānyādīnāmiti |
svātmave sattvaṃ svarūpaṃ yatsāmānyādīnāṃ tadeva teṣāṃ sattvaṃ na sattāyogaḥ śabdavṛ |
etena sāmānyādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ sāmānyarahitatvaṃ (2) sādharmyamuktamityarthaḥ |
kāpyetadanyathakartumaśakyatvāt sāmānye satā(3) nātyantamagrahadvāru |
viśeṣeṇvapi sāmānyavaduktosaṃśayasyāpi sambhavāt nirṇaṃśārthe viśeṣānusaraṇīyanavasthaiva sambhāvepi satāmyaṇāte tadutpatye samavāyāpyupagamādaniṣṭāpattireva dūṣaṇaṃ gotvādimvaparajātimattvena vyāpsya satāthavyavasthasya tadviṣutena nivṛttirnivṛddhiḥ kṣuttattvahe sāmānyādiṣu sattvadityanugamaḥ svadrupasattvādharmyeṇa satāthāropāt tahiṃ mithyāpratyayo'to ko nāmāha neti bhramastamāvedekānugamo driṇyeva svadrupasattvaṃ tu na pṛthā svadrupasya yathārthatvāt dravyādimvapi satāthāropakṛta evāstu pratyayānugamaḥ naivam sati mukhye 'pyāropāśaṃkāmbhavāt na cevaṃ sāmānyādimvapi mukhyā bādhakasambhavāt dravyādiṣu ca tadabhāvāt |
buddhilakṣaṇatvamiti(4) |
buddhireva lakṣaṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ yeṣāṃ te buddhilakṣaṇāḥ vipratipannasāmānyādivadukte buddhireva lakṣaṇaṃ nānyat dravyādivadukte tvanyadapi tatkāryaṃ pramāṇaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
kṣīre'nuvtaṃmāno dugdhā lakṣyante pratīyante iti buddhilakṣaṇāḥ |
tadayuktam |
dravyāderapi svabuddhilakṣaṇatvātedaṃ vaidharmyamuktaṃ syāt |
akāryatvaṃ kāraṇānapekṣatvamabhāvatvaṃ taddha sāmānye nāvadyakaṃ |
pravṛddhasthāne vyaktikāle cāvasthitimāśritya kāraṇābhāve'nātmabhūtasahakāriṇā prayojanenajaryatvakārānuyuktenena pratyavekṣaṇe vyāptivat gṛhyate samavāyasyāpyakāryatvaṃ pravopadasaṅgrahābhāvavakrame yadi hi paṭasya samavāyaḥ paṭātpūrve sambhavati asati sambandhini kathamasau sambandhaḥ syāt |
atha paṭena sahotpadyate tadā paṭasyānādhāratvaṃ prā-prāpti|
atha eṣañcānuvṛtti tathāpi paṭasyānāśrayatvameva na ca kāryatvamanāśrayyaṃ  yuktam tasmādakutaka: samavāya: |
viśeṣaṇāṃ cākāryatve śaktutve cati dravyaguṇakarmānyatvāt sāmānyasamavāyavatsiddham |
akāraṇatve samavāyyasamavāyikāraṇatvābhāva: na tu nimittakāraṇatvapratiṣedho buddhinimittatvāpyupapadyamānāt |
asāmānyaviśeṣavatsamavajātirahitatvamitarayo: |
sāmānyeṣu sāmānyaṃ nāma nāparaṃ sāmānyamasti |
ajñāpi sāmānyaprāgbhāveṣvabhāvānām viśeṣasamavāyeṣvu sāmānyamāve kaścita eva nyāyya: kāyyaṃ tarhiṃ sāmānyeṣu pratyayānuvṛtti: sāmānya sāmānyamiti |
anekatvattisamavāyopādhivaśāt viśeṣavyekaśabdapravṛttiratyantāśrutinivṛddhajanakatvasya sarvaiva sambhavāt|
nityatve vināśahetutve  naṣṭapi sāmānyasya vyāghṛttyādivināśayogyatvaniyādiṣaṇā bhramo bhrama: pravṛttena niṣpādiṣyatyevaṃca vyāptiviruddhaścoyate samavāyasya tu sarvaiva kāryopalambhādakṛtakatvājñānamupete |
arthaśabdānityatvaṃ ceti svasamayārtheśabdānityatvaṃ ceteṣāṃ sādhakam |
va: ṣaṣṭhucaye ||
pṛthivyādīnāṃ navānāmapi dravyatvayoga: svātmanyārambhakatvaṃ guṇavattvaṃ kāryakāraṇavirodhitvamantyaviśeṣavattvam ||
idānīṃ dravyāṇāmeva sādhamyaṃ nirūpayati |
pṛthivyādīnāmiti |
pṛthivyādīnāmeva dravyatvena sāmānyena yoga: sambandha: sa kriyāphala āha |
navānāmapīti |
kaṇiśabdo'piśabdārthe |
etena dravyapadārtheṣveteṣyoga bhedalakṣaṇabhūtam |
dravyaśabdasya pravṛttinimittaṃ ca cintitam |
atra kaścidvadati dravyatvayogo dravyatvasamavāya: sa ca paśupadārthaśyetyātmakaṃ dravyalakṣaṇamiti |
apara: samādhatte |
pravṛtti sarveṣāmiṣṭa: samavāyastathāpi dravyatvopalakṣaṇabhedād dravyasya lakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ hi kalpitabhedavyāpyakāśasya śrotramātrenārthakriyābheda iti draṣaṭavyametadvākyortha: yathākāśaṃ śrotraṃ naiva yogo dravyasya lakṣaṇaṃ kintu dravyatvameva tatsāmarthyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ na sphoṭiti yogasūtreṇa liṅgasya tharthivyāditvakṣayane tathā caivaṃ prayoga: pṛthivyādikamitarebhyo bhidyate dravyatvāt yeṣāmi-tasmaiva medo nāsti teṣāṃ dravyatvamapi nāsti yathā rūpādaunāmiti |
tasmādaśaṅkāṃcamayatvāt ca |
anyadapi dravyāṇāṃ bādhyatvemāha |
svātmanyātmakatvamiti |
svasamavetakṣaṇeyanakatvamityarthaḥ |
guṇavattvaṃ guṇo: vaha sambandhaḥ |
etadupayuktaṃ guṇādibhyo dravyāṇāṃ vaidharmyemanyathā'sambhavāt |
kāryaikāraṇāvirodhitvam |
guṇo hi kvacikāraṇaiṇa vināśyate yathā'dyaḥ śabdo dvitīyaśabdena kvacikāraṇena vināśyate yathāntyaḥ śabda upāntyaśabdena kvāpi kāryeṣu vināśyate yathottarayogena dravyāṇi tu na kāryeṇa vināśyante nāpi kāraṇeneti kāryakāraṇāvirodhīni nityānāṃ kāraṇāvināśyo'sāvadeva kāraṇenāvināśaḥ anityadravyāṇāṃ kāraṇavināśyoḥ sambhavati kāryeṇa na vināśaḥ kiṃ tvanyeneti vivekaḥ |
tathānyaviśeṣavattvamanyaviśeṣayogitvamityarthaḥ ||
anāśritatvanityatve cānyatrāvadhidravyebhyaḥ ||
anāśritatvaṃ kṛtidravyasamavetatvaṃ nityatvaṃ vināśarahitatvaṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ bādhyatve tatu kiṃ sarveṣāṃ bādhyatvemityata āha |
avadhyadidravyebhyo'nyebhiḥ |
ava-dhavidravyāṇi parityajyānyadiśeṣavattvānāśritatvanityatvānyanyatra ghaṭantītyarthaḥ|
na kevalaṃ pūrvoktāḥ pṛthivyādīnāṃ yakṣamāḥ kiṃ tvanāśritatvanityatve ceti vārthaḥ ||
pṛthivyudakatejovātanabhasāmanekatvāparajātimattve ||
pṛthivyādīnāṃ dravyāṇāmeva parasparasādharmye vaidharmye ca pratipādayannāha |
pṛthivyudakatejovātanabhasāmiti |
anekatvaṃ pratyekaṃ vyaktibhedaḥ |
apa-rajātimattvamiti pṛthivītvādijātisambandhitvam ||
kṣitijalajyotiranilamanasāṃ kriyāvattvamūrtatvaparatvāparatvavegavattvāni ||
kṣitijalajyotiranilamanasāṃ kriyāvattvamūrtatvaparatvāparatvavegavattvānīti |
kriyāvattvamupekṣaṇādikriyāyogaḥ |
mūrtattvamavacchinnaparimāṇayogi-ttvam |
paratvāparatvavegavattvāni paratvāparatvavegasamavāyaḥ |
saṃyogasaṃyogā-spīyassamayassamoreva parāparatyavahārahetutvāt paratvāparatve na sta iti kaucit na bhinnavidrussamanviyo: satyapi saṃyuktasaṃyogādyapekṣassamayassavatrāpi satyapi ca tadṛśaḥ śarīrāpekṣayā bahiṣkṛṣṭaviprakṛṣṭadūrābhyāde parāparatvapratyayāvāt |
ekasyāṃ diśyavasthitayoḥ piṇḍayossatyapratyaya iti cet cakti tārhi saṃyogādyapekṣassamayaskāpyanyaṃ viṣayāntaraṃ viṣayavailakṣaṇyamantareṇa vilakṣaṇāyā buddherutpādāt |
vegādi guṇāntaraṃ na kriyāvattātimāca manyante vaibhāṣīyabhāvāt kriyākaraṇatāmātrūpādarśanamito vaipāśyavahāra iti ced vyavahāramātradṛṣṭa kriyākāṇāṃ nirantarotpādyapravatāṃ pratyekamantarāpūrvonāpurvādasya pratyakṣaṇapariṇateḥ vaipāśyasya ca bhāvāt vyakta ca loke kriyādhvaṃsormedādvagatiḥ vegena gacchatīti pratīteḥ ||
ākāśakāladigātmanāṃ sarvaṃgatatvaṃ parasparasaṃsargaṃ sarvaisaṃyogisamānadeśatvaṃ ca(1) ||
ākāśakāladigātmanāṃ sarvaṃgatatvamityādi |
sarvaśabdenātra prakṛtāpekṣayāntarāṇītāni mūrtadravyāṇi parāmṛśyante |
sarvaṃgatatvaṃ sarvadeśaiḥ saha saṃyogaḥ ākāśādīnāṃ na tu sarvaira gamyaṃ teṣāṃ niṣkriyatvāt |
parasparasaṃsargamityanānacatuṣṭayaparimāṇayogitvam |
sarvaisaṃyogisamānadeśatvaṃ sarvaiṣāṃ saṃyogināṃ mūrtadravyāṇāmākāśaḥ samāne deśe yatra ādhāra ityarthaḥ |
evaṃ digādiṣvapi vyākhyeyam |
yadyapyākāśādikaṃ sarvaiṣāṃ saṃyogināmādhāro na bhavatyādhārasyāvenāvasthānāt |
tathāpi sarvaisaṃyogādhāratvāt sarvaisaṃyogināmādhāra ityucyate upacārāt |
atra eva sarvaṃgatatvamityanenānuvādaktaṃ |
tatra hi sarvai: saha saṃyogoktityuktam |
iha tu sarvaiṣāmādhāra ityucyate ||
pṛthivyādīnāṃ pañcānāmapi bhūtatvendriyaprakṛtitvāhaṃkenidriyaparāhaviśeṣaguṇavattvāni ||
pṛthivyādīnāmākāśāntānāmitaretaravyāvṛtyā dhāryante kathyante |
pṛthivyādīnāmiti |
bhūtatvaṃ bhūtaśabdavācyatvaṃ ekatvamitamantareṇānekaiḥ pṛthivyādivyaktaśavdapravṛttivyaktavat yathā devadattendriyatvaviśeṣakatvasāmānyayo-yogadvaicanādirabaṃdhavaśeḥ baddhamitaḥ tathā pṛthivītvādisāmānyavaśāt pṛthivyādiṣu cakṣuṣu bhūtaśabdaḥ baddhamitaḥ ākāśe tu vyaktiniyamana yatha bhūtaṃ bhūtamiti tacchabdānuviddhaḥ pratyakṣatvachabdavācyanopādhikata yathā devanādike'pra itipratyayaḥ|
indriyaprakṛtitvamindriyatvasāmānyaṃ na bhūtasamavāyīndriyāṇi āgrāṇakāritvāt grāhyakāritvaṃ hi mauliko dharmo yathā pradīpasyeti kecit|
tadayuktam|
vyavahitānupalabdheḥ pradīpendriyasāgrāhyakāri kuḍyādyavahitamapyathe gṛhṇīyādapratibandhāt|
yogyatāsāmānyavahitārthagrahaṇamiti cet indriyasya tāvadyogyatā viṣayagrahaṇasāmarthyamastyeva tadānīmavyavahitārthagrahaṇāt viṣayasyāpi yogyatā mahattvānitakadravyatvarūpaviśeṣātmikā vyavadhāne'pi na nivartate ākāśāvasthānamapi tadavasthameva|
atha mataṃ āvaraṇabhāvo'pyathapratītikāraṇa saṃśayāsāva iva etannakarmīpi āvaraṇaṃ satyāvaraṇabhāvo nivṛta iti pratīteranutpatteḥ kāraṇabhāvāditi|
naitatsāram|
āvaraṇasya svapratidvandvaprāṇipratibandhabhāvopalabdheḥ dvārādikaṃ hi yattto jalasya khācirasya ca taijasaiḥ pratibadhyati(1) na tu svasyabhāvamātraṃ nivartayati tathā khati kuḍyametadanumānaṃ prāpraprakāśakaṃ cakṣuravyavahitārthaprakāśakatvāt pradīpavat bāhyendriyatvāt tvagindriyavat|
nanvevaṃ tarhi viprakṛṣṭopi grāhyaṃ syāt raśmeretatnibandhāt anadvṛtahṛdyathayogāt cākṣuṣā raśmayo dūre gatvā dhanvamaye gṛhṇanti|
ata eva saddhaturuktatvāt kimindriyasya(2) bhautikatvaṃ na siddhyati pradīpasyeva raśmidvāreṇa tadupapatteḥ yatha ca raśmayo bhūyobhiḥ svātapaveḥ saṃhatīprayatinā tadavayaveṣveva saha santiṣṭhante|
tatrāśeṣaviśeṣātmandatvasyārthasya grahaṇāt smṛtyā(3) grahaṇaṃ yatha tvacaprasamāveṇa śabdanyastatra dhāmānyamāvaviśiṣṭasya dharmiṇo grahaṇādasmṛtaṃ grahaṇam|
yadu gacchati tatsvakriyāhetuvyatiriktāpekṣaṃ krameṇa prāpnoti tatkārya śākhācandramaso'pyujjvakālopalabdhiriti cetu indriyavṛtte(4)rāśuśuktitvāt phalāśayagata(5)vyatiṣedhavat kṣamāgrahaṇanimitto'yaṃ kṣamo na tu vāstavo yogapadyam|
nanu prāṇipade ghrānarālocamitigrahaṇaṃ na syāt nānyathā tadupapanne indriyasya svavyathāsātīndriyatvāt tadudrāghātādhṛtau khātaranirantarapratyayo ki tu parokṣasambandhaghātāghātṛtau yatha śarīrasambaddhasyārthasya grahaṇaṃ tatha nirantarapramitirutpadyaḥ yatha tu ṣaṭsaṃkhyādṛsya grahaṇaṃ tatha dāntara iti |
bāhyekaikendriyagrāhyaviśeṣaguṇavattvānīti |
bāhyekaikendriyeṇa cakṣurādinā grāhyā ye viśeṣaguṇāḥ rūpādayastadvattvena pṛthivyādīnāmiti |
ataḥ kāraṇagrāhyatvamapyeṣāṃ guṇānāmasti tataścaikaikendriyagrāhyatvamasiddhaṃ tadyathe bāhyagrahaṇam |
ekaikenadreṇa svarūpakathanīyam ||
caturṇāṃ dravyātmakatvasya bhāvatve ||
caturṇāṃ dravyātmakatvasya bhāvatve |
caturṇāṃ pṛthivyudakānalānilānāṃ dravyātmakatve dravyaṃ prati samavāyikāraṇabhāvaḥ sa ca nijā śaktireva |
svarūpavatvaṃ svarūpasamavāyaḥ ||
trayāṇāṃ pratyakṣatvarūpavatvadravatvāni (1) ||
trayāṇāṃ pratyakṣatvarūpavatvadravatvāni |
trayāṇāṃ kṣityudakatejasāṃ pratyakṣatvamindriyajajñānapratibhāsamānatā na tu mahattvādikāraṇayogaḥ rūpavattvamindriyasya punarutpattiprabandhāt |
nanvātmanāpi pratyakṣatvamasti satyaṃ bāhyendriyāpekṣayā trayāṇāmityuktam |
tathā rūpavattvaṃ rūpasamavāyaḥ |
dravatvaṃ dravatvaṃ nāma guṇāntaram ||
dvayorgurutvaṃ rasavattvaṃ ca ||
dvayorgurutvam |
dvayoḥ pṛthivyudakayorgurutvaṃ nāma guṇāntaram |
tasya bhāvāt pṛthivyambuke ca guruśabdaniveśaḥ |
rasavattvaṃ ca rasasamavāyaḥ na kevalaṃ tayo(2)rgurutvaṃ rasavattvaṃ ceti cārthaḥ ||
bhūtātmanāṃ vaiśeṣikaguṇavattvam ||
bhūtātmanāṃ vaiśeṣikaguṇavattvam |
bhūtānāṃ pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśātmanāṃ vaiśeṣikaguṇayogaḥ |
viśeṣo vyavacchedaḥ viśeṣāya svāśrayasyāntarebhyo vyavacchedāya prabhavantīti vaiśeṣikā rūpādayastadyogo bhūtātmanām ||cityudbakātmanāṃ caturdeśaguṇāvastham |
cityudbakātmanāṃ caturdeśaguṇāvastham |
citiudbakasyātmanāṃ caturdeśaguṇayoga: |
ākāśātmanāṃ dvaṇikaikadeśavṛttiviśeṣaguṇāva-stham |
ākāśātmanāṃ ca dvaṇikaikadeśavṛttiviśeṣaguṇe: yaha yoga: vidyate ityāha |
ākāśātmanāmiti |
viśeṣaguṇā: pṛthivyādīnāmapi santi tarhidva-cyayomekadeśavṛttinayeṇaṃ ye ca te ākāśātmanāmadhyātmabhūtayo viśeṣaguṇā: teṣāmānutaravināśitvaṃ ca svarūpamantīti dvaṇikaikadeśottena krṛtam |
dikkālayo: pañcaguṇāvasthaṃ sarvotpattimatāṃ nimi-ttakāraṇatvaṃ ca |
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgā: pañcaiva guṇā dikṣi kāle ca vartante ityāha |
dikkālayoriti |
na kevalamanayo: pañcaguṇāvasthaṃ sādhyarthe sarvotpa-ttimatāṃ nimittakāraṇatvaṃ ca sādhyarthe |
nanu dikkālā sarveṣāmutpattimatāṃ nimittamiti kuta etat pratyetavya yadi śabdaviśeṣiṇākāśasyāpi kāraṇatvaṃ syāt atha tadbhupadeśāt bodhyanaikāntiko guhe jāto gehe jāta ityanimi-tepi darśanāt |
acodyate |
asti tāvat tattvoddiṣṭapratiniyamāt padārthavyutpa-ttidvāraviśeṣaniyamāc kālaviśeṣaniyamācca sarveṣāmutpatte: yadi deśakālaviśe-ṣāviva na kāraṇaṃ yacca kaccana hetava: kāye kasmuraviśeṣāt sarvadā sarvatra kāra-ṇābhāvāt kāryānutpattiriti cet yacca deśe kāle ca kāraṇāni bhavanti tatra teṣāṃ janakatvaṃ nānyatraitatprayotavyaṃ viśiṣṭadeśakālayorahetutvaṃ kāryajananāya tayo: kāraṇairupakārodbhavāt |
idameva ca deśasya kālasya ca nimittatvaṃ yadekatra kāryotpattiranyatrānutpattiriti |
dvititejaśornaimittikadravatvayoga: |
dvititejaśornaimittikadravatvayoga: |
naimittikadravajāte naimittikaṃ ca taddravatvaṃ ceti naimittikadravatvaṃ tena yaha dvititejaśoryoga:kṣapidastejasya ca durbalaujasāderaniṣedho'nena bibhyatānām gurutvabalavyādhikṣayeva durbalaṃ dahyamāneṣu suvarṇādiṣu kṣayuktasamavāyād pratīyate iti cetra pāthivadravyasyātyantamaniṣedho'nena bhasmībhāvopalabdherasya ca tadabhāvāt |
ata eva suvarṇādikamapi pārthivameveti kathañcidanugrāhye'pi pratyuktaḥ pārthivatve sati kṣapidādidravyatvanavahiṃsavego'nena dravatvoṣṇaṭṭaprabhṛtīnām |
yadyapyedamuktaṃ pārthivaṃ suvarṇādikaṃ kṣapidahiṃsradravatvabhāve sati gurutvādhikaraṇatvādvā'nugrāhyam |
tadayuktam |
kiṃ tatra gurutvasyopalabdhiḥ tadguṇatvāduttīrṇaguṇatve'pi vṛttīdikṣapi snehastvādayaprāptyāvṛttinimittāditisaṃśayasyānivṛtteḥ yadyapi sādhuvāntaraṃ parapakṣapramānatvāditi tadapyanudūraprabandhenāpyupanetṛkṣāpaṇaṃ diṅmātramatrātirūpadiṣṭam ||
ṛtenaiva nyāyena sarveṣu padārtheṣu svadūṣapi sādhutve svayaṃ śābdamu viṣayeyāditastyādyanuvekṣamye vācyamiti śithyānāha |
evamiti ||
evaṃ sarvatra vācyaṃ viparyayādvaidharmyaṃ (1) ca vācyamiti dravyāsaṅkaraḥ (2) ||
adṛṣṭeṣu padārtheṣu na teṣāṃ lakṣaṇāni pravartante nirviṣayatvāt alabdheṣu ca tattvamatītyabhāvaḥ kāraṇābhāvāt ataḥ padārthevyutpādanāya pravṛttasya śāstrasyopeyayā pravṛttiḥ uddeśo lakṣaṇaṃ ca parīkṣāyāstu niyamaḥ |
yathābhihite lakṣaṇe prayojānāntaravyatiṣāpu tattvaniścayo na bhavati tatra paraprakṣavyudāsārthaṃ parīkṣāvidhirucyate |
yatra tu lakṣaṇābhidhānasāmarthyādeva tattvaniścayaḥ syāt tatrāpi vyarthoṃ nāśrīte yo'pi hi vividhayā śāstrasya pravṛttimicchati tasyāpi prayojanādīnāṃ nāsti parīkṣā tatkasya hetorilaṃbaṇamātrādeva te pratīyante iti |
evaṃ vedapraṇīteṣvanudīrghavyākhyāsyasya pravṛttinai cintyā |
nāmapradheyena padārthonāmabhidhānamuddeśaḥ uddiṣṭasya svarūpajñānopayogalakṣaṇo dharmo lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣitasya yathālakṣaṇaṃ vicāraḥ parīkṣā |
uddiṣṭaviṣāyasya na vidyāntastadguṇalakṣaṇeneva saṃgṛhītatvāt tathāhi pṛthagucyate padānvayeti niyamārthaṃ viśeṣala-dravyamadhye pṛthivīnirūpaṇam |
kaṇapracayaśca vibhakteṣu padārtheṣu teṣāṃ viśeṣalakṣaṇāni bhavanti anyathā tāni nirviśeṣāṇi syuḥ tava dravyāṇi dravyaguṇakarmasamudiṣṭāni pṛthivyādyaja iti viśekāni vakṣyati teṣāṃ viśeṣalakṣaṇārthaṃ prakramāmārabhyate ||
ihedānīmavaiśeṣikagranthe vaiśamyaṃsūcyate ||
ihedānīmiti |
pūrve dravyādīnāṃ parasparāpekṣayā vaidharmyuktam |
iha  vahnmabhye prakaraṇe saptasvekaikasya dravyasya vyāvartako dharmaḥ kathyate |
ekaikasya  iti śaṅkapratyādipratyāntabahuvrīhyāgamadvayanāśau uddeśyakrameṇa pṛthivyāḥ prathamaṃ  vaidharmyamāha ||
pṛthivītvābhisambandhāt pṛthivī |
rūparasagandhasparśavattvāparimāṇadravyatvasnehogavibhāgaparatvāparatvagurutvadravatvasaṃskāravatī |
ete ca guṇāviśeṣādhikāre  rūpādayo guṇaviśeṣāḥ siddhāḥ |
cāturvidhyavacanāt ṣaṭtva  saṃkhyādyaḥ |
patanopadeśādgurutvam |
atraiḥ dravatvānyavacanād dravatvam |
uttarakṣamevacanāt saṃskāraḥ |
tritāveva gandhaḥ |
rūpanaikaprakāraṃ śuklādi |
rasaḥ  ṣaṅvidho madhurādiḥ |
gandho dvividhaḥ surabhirasurabhiśca |
sparśo'ṣṭā śrutiṣṇāśītatve sati pākajaḥ |
sā tu dvividhā |
nityā cānityā ca |
paramāṇulakṣaṇā nityā |
kāryalakṣaṇā tvanityā |
sā ca  sthairyāyavayavasaṃniveśaviśiṣṭāparajātibahutvopetā  ayatnāhananāhanaikopakārakarī ca |
trividhaṃ cāsyāḥ  kāryam |
śarīrendriyaviṣayasañjñakam |
śarīraṃ dvividham |
yonijamayonijaṃ ca |
tatrāyonijamanapekṣya yo-ṛbhaurilataṃ devarṣīṇāṃ pratītaṃ dharmaviśeṣasahitebhyo'ṇubhyo jāyate |
buddhajantugaṇaṃ yātanāśarīrāṇyadharmaviśeṣasahitebhyo'ṇubhyo jāyante |
śukraśoṇitavadvipātajaṃ yonijam |
tadbuddhiviṣayaṃ jarāyujamaṇḍajaṃ ca |
mānuṣapaśumragāṇāṃ jarāyujam |
pakṣisarīsṛpapāśāmaṇḍajam |
indriyaṃ gandhavyañjakaṃ sarveprāṇināṃ jalāvacanabhibhūteḥ pārthivāvayavairārabdhaṃ ghrāṇam |
viṣayastu dravyakodikasameṇā rūpyastrividho dṛtyāghrāṇasyāvralambaṇāḥ |
tatra bhūpradeśāḥ prākāṣṭhapuṣkādayo bhūdravyākārāḥ |
pāṣāṇā upalamaṇivajrādayaḥ |
sthāvarāstṛṇauṣadhivanaspataya iti ||
pṛthivītvābhisambandhyāt pṛthivī |
ye hi pṛthivīṃ svahastena jighranti śrutāstebrāhmaṇāhastu pṛthivīti na vyavaharanti te prati viṣayasambandhyātmicāriṇa vyavahārasādhanārthamasādhāraṇo yameḥ kathyate |
pṛthivītvābhisambandhyāt pṛthivīti |
iyaṃ pṛthivīti vyavahartavyā pṛthivītvābhisambandhyāt yat punaḥ pṛthivīti na vyavahriyate na tat pṛthivītvenābhisambaddhaṃ yathākāśikam |
na ceyaṃ pṛthivītvena nābhisambaddhā tasmāt pṛthivīti vyavahartavyeti |
yo vā pṛthivīti loke bhṛṇoti na jānāti ca tasyāḥ svarūpaṃ kroḍuguptiṃ taṃ prati tasyāḥ svarūpajñātīyavyāvṛttasvarūpapratipādanārthamasādhāraṇo yameḥ kathyate yā loke pṛthivīti vyapadiśyate sā pṛthivī pṛthivītvābhisambandhyāt |
yathāhaṃdruyacodakāḥ samānāsamānajātīyavyāvṛttyarthāśca lakṣaṇārthāḥ |
yatenekṣādi pratyuktaṃ pratidravyeṣu padārtho na lakṣaṇīyāḥ aprasiddhā na tatrāmarthakyatvāt |
svarūpaṇāsamānasyāpi vyavahāraviśeṣapratipādanārtha sāmānyena prasiddhasya viśeṣalakṣaṇārthaṃ ca lakṣaṇāśruteḥ |
nanvevaṃ śatyanavasthā lakṣaṇalakṣaṇārtha-dravyamuccai pṛthivīti guṇabhāvāt |
vyāghrāntaro jaghanīyatvāditi cenna apratītau jaghanyo'tyantāsac khavec vāpratītyabhāvāt tathāhi śirasā pādena gavāmanuvardhanta hidvīpau: na punaretāvabhyañcataḥ kṣamīkṣante yastu sarve'pīkṣāmupaho na sa pratyudeṣyaḥ |
sattva vātamūkādivavadanaiścikārāt |
gandhapṛthaktvacaritatvapūrvakāṇupacayatvamapi pṛthivyā itarebhyo vaidharmyamiti pratipādyamanrāha |
rūparasagandheti |
nañca dradrāvantaraṃ manupuṣtyaprayogāt pratyekaṃ rūpādīnāṃ pṛthivyā saha sambandho lakṣyate|
rūpakāravyāpyate guṇāḥ pṛthivyāmabhimatā ityāha |
gate ceti |
guṇānāṃ vinirdeśo dravyeṇa vṛttaiḥ sā pratipādyate 'nenābhivyaktaye 'sminniti guṇavinirdeśyādhikāre dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ tasmin rūparasagandhasparśauḥ pṛthivyāṃ siddhāḥ duścakāreṇa pratipāditāḥ rūparasagandhasparśavatī pṛthivīti |
cāturpadatvāt 'aṇa saṃkhyādvayaḥ|
saṃkhyā parimāṇāni pṛthaktvaṃ saṃyogavibhāgau paratvāparatve karma ca kṛtidravya(1)kamavāyāddraguṇāneti cāturpadatvādupacaryaṃ pṛthivyāṃ saṃkhyādayaḥ kṣama siddhāḥ |
yadi te rūpidravyeṣu na santi tatsamavāye teṣāṃ pratyakṣatvaṃ duścakāreṇa noktaṃ vyāhatiyeḥ |
patanāpadeśād gurutvamiti |
saṃyogapratipakṣābhāve gurutvāt patanamityupadeśāt duścakāreṇa patanasambandhinyā pṛthivyā gurutvamastītyarthāt kathitaṃ vyādhikaraṇasyākaraṇatvāt|
adbhiḥ sāmānyavacanād dravatvam |
śapijatumathacchidrānāṃ pāṣāṇānāmanivaśepopād dravatvamadbhiḥ sāmānyamiti vacanāt pṛthivyāṃ naimittikaṃ dravatvamastītyuktam|
mṛducchidraśabdena vikalpyāmidhānam(2) |
uttarakarmavacanāt saṃskāra iti |
naivadānādāmiṣoḥ karme tatsamekāritātva saṃskārāt tathottaraguṇaṃ ceti duścakāreṇa iho pārthivadravye karmahetuḥ saṃskāra ityavaśeṣatā pṛthivyāṃ vego'stīti jñāpitam (3) ṛvidyamānajñāhetutvāt|
yathā caika yava saṃskāra āpatanāt tathopapādayiṣyāmaḥ(4) |
vitāgandhaḥ |
ayamasyāthaḥ kevala yathāpramasādhāraṇadharma iti |
sugandhi vastile dugandhiḥ samīraṇa iti pratyayād dravyāntareṇa gandho'stīti cenna|
pārthivadravyasamavāyena tadaguṇopalabdheḥ kāryameva niścaya iti cen tadabhāve udgatasaṃsāt yathāpi rūpaṃ cayāṇāṃ tathāpyagantasmedopekṣayā tadeva pṛthivyā yathā vaiśeṣyamāha |rūpamevaṃprakārāviti |
kṛṣṇāpi citrādavetyuktavyānīyam |
śuklapītādayanekāvidayaṃ kurvaṃ citrādeva nānyatratyarthaḥ |
ekasyāṃ pṛṣṭhaujāto nānāhūpāṇi vyaktibhedena samarpayanti |
kumbhivekasyāmapi vyākulanekaprakārahūpasamāveśaḥ yatra nānāvidhahūpaprapañcatyabhirapayaveraṇuvyāpyate |
kathametaditi ceducyate |
yathāprapedesthayathāsthadaktayāghraghaṭakahūparuṇabiti hūmamātraṣyam karṣameveṣu ca na śuklameva hūpaminna nāpi śyāmammeva kintu śyāmaśuklaharitādīni na ca teṣāmekaṃ hūpamevāste nāparathītyasti niyamaḥ pratyekamanyacca sarveṣāmapi sāmapredarśanāt |
na ca parasparaṃ virodhena savoṣāmapi nārambhatta evetyapi yuktam |
citrahūpasyāghadhavitraḥ pratītehūpasya duṣṭasya pratyakṣatvābhāvācca |
na cāpyavahupāṇi samuditānyacca citraliṅgā pratīyante tenaivāvayavī pratyakṣa iti kalpanāyāmanyatrāpi tathābhāvaprasaṅgenāvayaviruktedāpatteḥ tasmāt sarvameva tairāvabhyate tadvāraṇyasārṇaṃ vicitrakāraṇasvabhāvānugamācchayāmaśuklaharitātmakameva syācitramiti ca vyapadiśyate |
virodhādekasminnekasyabhāvāsambhavāditi cet tathā ca prāñcāduktamevātaḥ |
ekaṃ ca citraṃ cetyetattu citrataraṃ tata iti |
ko virodho nīlādīnāṃ na tāddantettaramābālarukau bhāvasyamāvānumātṛ anyonyasaṃśrayāpatteśca svahūpānyatvaṃ virodha iti cet satyamasatyeva tathāpi citrātmano hūpasya nāpṛthaktā vicitrakāraṇasāmarthyopāvirbhastasya sarvelokaprasiddhena pratyakṣeṇaivopapāditatvāt kācitye pāśve paṭasyaiva tadāśrayasya citrahūpasya prahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ tasyaikatvāditi cet anyathāśririkāmyāṃ samadhigatasāmarthyasvābhyasanānāhūpadarśanatvāpi citrahūpagrahaṇaihetutvāt tasya ca pāśvaitaratre pramābāt |
nanvevaṃ tarhi nānāhūpadṛṣṭukaurāsthye dravye na citrahūpagrahaṇaṃ tadavayavahūpagrahaṇābhāvāt ko nāmāha na tatheti |
na hi paramasukṣmasya vastuno hūpaṃ vivicya gṛhyate yasya tu vivicya gṛhyate tasyātathātvahūpāṇāpi gṛhyante |
yastvekayāpakṣāni bahūni citrahūpāṇīti manyate tasya nīlapītādyamāvaśye dvitatṛkṣaye hūpānyanivekasyāpravaddhūpasyānārambhakatvāt |
atha sarve tatrobhāvyāmekaṃ citraṃ kūpamārabhyate taddanyatrāpi tathā syādaviśeṣāt citravādavyāsitaṃ citradravyaṃ śaṅkudhudvyākhyācitradvyatvāt itaradravyavat tadṛtaṃ kūpamekamavayavidravyatvāt itaravayavidravyahūpavat |
rasaḥ pāṇḍiva ityatrāpi pūrvebhaṅgāsyānam |
prakṛtyasyadravyatve pṛthivīnirūpaṇam |
caitanyigrahaṇaṃ tattvāramyamaiṇa pṛthivyā eva svarūpakathanamityāśaṅkāmṛṇāha |
gandho dravivya iti |
tadeva dravyatvaṃ darśayati |
surabhisurabhiṃjñeti |
asurabhiriti surabhigandhaviruddhaṃ pratidravyādisambandhe pratikulasaṃvedanīyaṃ gandhāntaraṃ na tu tadabhāvamātraṃ vidhirūpeṇa khātiprayatnayā ca saṃvedanāt |
upacayeṇaiyastu gandho'nudbhūtaturīyaparimāṇabheda eveti pṛthagnocyate |
athavā saumyasurabhireva surabhigandhādanyo'surabhiriti vyutpādanāt |
yathāsura: svago'sya vaidhamryaṃ tathā darśayati |
svago'sya iti |
pākaja: sparśa: pṛthivyā vaidhamrye tasya svarūpakathanamanumābhau iti |
yadyapi sparśavat pākajo hṛṇarakhādavyasya vaidhamryaṃ tathāpi hṛṇarakhaco: pākajatvānabhidhānam anyathāpi tayorvaidhamryeya sambhavāttṛtīyamājñatipādanasyaiva vivakṣitatvāt ato'yamānupākajeṣu stabdhādiṣu sparśasya pākajatvamanumānāt stabdhādiṣu sparśa: pākaja: pādhivasparśatvādvṛ ghaṭādisparśavat ghaṭādisparśasyāpi pākajatvamekendriyagrāhyatve sati sadaguṇatvāt tadgatadravatvat |
atrāntaramevanirūpaṇomāha |
nityā cānityā ceti |
prakārāntaramāvedayannāha vacanena |
kā nityā kā cānityetyāha |
paramāṇulakṣaṇā nityā kāryalakṣaṇā tvanityeti |
ubhayatrāpi lakṣaṇaśabda: svarūpārtha: |
paramāṇusvabhāvāyā: pṛthivyā: sattve kiṃ pramāṇamanumānam aṇuparimāṇakāraṇatvaṃ kāryadravyāntaṃ parimāṇakāraṇatvasyānyatrāparimāṇakāraṇatvasvāt |
yadvedaṃ vidyāntaṃ yata: paramāṇunoktiṃ sa paramāṇu: |
ata eva nityo dravyatve satyanavayavatvādākāśavat athārthaṃ khāyave na sarve paramāṇu: kāryaparimāṇopekṣayā tadavayavaparimāṇasya lauke utpadyamānasmatīte: śabda tasyāvayava: sa paramāṇurbhaviṣyati atha khāpi na bhavati avayavāntarasadbhāvāt śava tarhyavayasya tatsthāvayavināmatyantataratvādibhāve na syāt sarveṣāmanantakāraṇajanyatvāviśeṣeṇa parimāṇaprakṣepādūrdhvamaikya: kā''śaṅkhāsaṅkhyāprakṛtasāmyasyokavambhavāt |
asti tāvadarthaṃ parimāṇabheda: tasmādyaṇuparimāṇaṃ kāryadravyatithayamiti siddho nitya: paramāṇu: sa caiko vārambhaka: ekasya nityasyakāryamakṣatve kāryasya sattotpattiḥ syādutpattiṇāmāvāt |
kāryavināśitve ca pradhvaṃset |
āśrayavināśyatvāśrayavaibhāgasya ca vināśahetorabhāvāt |
vyāghātavyāptamakṣatvaprayuktam |
iha mahattvādyadravyatvayoranyato dravyatvāpaṇidyātmaparimāṇasya kāryedravyatvobhaya sāmarthyadarśanāt |
vyaṇuke kāryedravyatvobhaya janyate mahatparimāṇatvād ghaṭavat |
pūrva vyāghātamekasya kārambhakatve pratibandho dravyatvena paramāṇutvāmārabhyate yadṛdu drāṇukamiti khidduḥ |
drāṇukaivehumirārabhyate ityapi niyamo na draṣṭavyaḥ tasyānuparimāṇātmato kāraṇavahutvavināśatve'pyatvatāśāratveṣayatvād bahu tvaniyamaḥ |
kadācit tribhirārabhyate iti vyaṇukatrityucyate kadācidbahubhirārabhyate kadācidaṇubhiriti yatheṣṭaṃ kalpanā |
na ca kāryasya vyagretā yathā yathā kāraṇasaṃkhyābāhulyaṃ tathā tathā mahatparimāṇatāratamyopasamāna na caivaṃ yati drāṇukānāmeva ghaṭārambhakatvaprasaktiḥ ghaṭasya mahat utpattiratamādimātraprayojanena tathaivārambhakattvānān tadaiva drāṇukādiprakrameṇa kriyate kāryelakṣaṇā pṛthivī sā cānityā kāraṇavibhāgavyaprayavināśasya ca hetoḥ sambhavāt |
kāryelakṣaṇāyāḥ pṛthivyā anityatvena saha yamontaraṃ samuccinoti |
khā iti |
svoṣṭe niśaṅkatvam |
ādiśabdān prāṇiśaktatvādiparigrahaḥ |
akṣapratyakṣānāṃ khadviśeṣo'pratyakṣavaiśeṣikamatānabhijñaiḥ sthaulyādyapratyakṣavaiśeṣyācca tairviśiṣṭā aparajātibahutvopetā gotvādijātiprayastyutattvayoḥ |
paramāṇavādiṣaprajātyapratīcapradṛṣṭatvaśād tathā tathā teṣāṃ vyūho yathā yathā tadārambhebhyaparajātayo jāyante |
nanvanukāritā sarveṣāmaṇūnāṃ sphaṭiḥ kāryelakṣaṇā pṛthvī kāmaprekṣiyāṃ puruṣasya janayati yeneyamadraṣṭena kriyate ityata āha |

alpaṣlpatirekāvyāptiriktakāraṇabhāvasya śukrayoṇitasyābhāve kṣaye śarīrasyotpattirityata āha |
yarmaviśeṣavahitebhya iti |
viśiṣyate iti viśeṣaḥ dharme yatha viśeṣaḥ dharmaviśeṣaḥ prakṛṣṭo dharmastattvahitebhyo'pyupyabhya iti |
ayamabhisandhiḥ śarīrasyaive paramāṇuva yatha kāraṇaṃ na śukrayoṇitavaditi tasyāḥ kriyāvibhāgādinyāgnyo tathāvināśe vastvutpannapākjaiḥ paramāṇubhirārambhānna ca śukrayoṇitaparamāṇūnāṃ kāścidviśeṣaḥ pāśrvasthādiśeṣāt |

yathaṃ caidrurmaviśeṣānugrahetibhyaḥ paramāṇvo'yonijaśarīrotpattinirṇupapadyā |
nanu dṛṣṭastāvattaveva śarīrotpattau śukrayoṇitayoḥ pūrvakālatāniyamaḥ:(2) tena yathā śabdendrajanaḥ ayugapattattvadravyāsvātantraryamajjanayāhaṅkārapramāṇāntaravirodhādanupapannastaddvayonijaśarīrotpyagamo'pi vedya śukrādi nirapekṣyāpi śalbhādiśarīrasya darśanān viśiṣṭadeśyāntarya śarīrasya śukrādipūrvanivāpateti hetu svaye tathāpi na niyamasiddhiḥ kimadṛṣṭaviśeṣābhāvādṛṣṭaṃdādiśarīrasya śukrādipūrvatā kiṃ vā viśiṣṭadeśyānamātranubandhṛteti khandehāt |

pituḥ śukraṃ mātuḥ yoṇitaṃ tayoḥ saṃdvipātānnaraṃ ṣaṭarānalakhandhyā'ṅkurayoṇitāsmyakeṣu paramāṇuṣu pūrvarūpādivināśe sati samānaguṇāntarotpattau krayukādiprakrameṇa kralaśarīrotpattistattvāntaḥkaraṇapraveśo na tu śukrayoṇitāpekṣāyāṃ śarīrātpattyānantaryaṃ |
tatramātubābāraso mātrā saṃkrāmati catuḥpathānac punarjeṭhātanalaṣaḍbhyān kajalārambhakaparamāṇuṣu kriyābhibhāgādinyāyena kajalaśarīre'pi bahu bahutvabahupakaje: kajalārambhakaparamāṇubhiradravyadhādravyātkriyeṇāhāraparamāṇubhi: ṣaha sambhūya śarīrottpattamārabhyate ityeṣā kalpanā śarīre pratyahaṃ dṛśyā |

drāṇamiti tasya vṛttā |
kātmā jighrati gandhamupādatte uneneti kṛtvā tatsyadravye gandhopalabdhireva pramāṇam kriyāyāḥ karaṇasādhyatvāt cakṣurādivyāṇāre ca tasyā kṣutvādātu pāṇivatvepi hṛpādiṣu madhye gandhasvevāvityajñakatve pramāṇam |
kuṅkumagandhāmityajñakṛtavavat yathā vṛtte svagandhasahitameva kuṅkumagandhasabhivyanakti tathā ghrāṇamapi svagandhasahitamevendriyam kṣito na svagandhasya grāhakaṃ tenaiva tatprāpyaṇāt yathā ghrāṇasya tathā rasanacakṣustvagindriyāṇāmapi yathābhāvena dṛṣṭāntabalena hṛparakṣasyaśeṣabhūtānāmevendriyatvājñānamātraṃ svaguṇasahaśayam |
yogyaṃ tu śabdaguṇamindriyam |
atastanaiva śabdopalambhaḥ |
śarīrendriyārtho viṣayasya svahṛviśeṣe tuśabdena darśayatu bhedaṃ darśayati |
viṣayastiti |
jñāṇādiprakaṣapārthyasya iti sādhāraṇyahṛpānuvādaḥ |
ghṛtvābhāṇasyāvaralakṣaṇaṃ iti |
ghṛtvābhāṇasyāvarādisvabhāve ityarthaḥ |
teṣāṃ madhye pūrve svahṛpeṇa nirdhārayamāha |
tatreti |
tatra bhūmadevyāḥ sthalanimnādayaḥ prāktarukṣādayaḥ sarve te mṛtsnākārāḥ mṛnmayedāḥ ityarthaḥ |
pāṣāṇabhedamāha |
pāṣāṇā iti |
upalāḥ śilāḥ |
maṇayaḥ sūryakāntādayaḥ |
vajro'śanirhīrakaśca |
tṛṇamūlapādāḥ |
oṣadhayaḥ phalapākāntāḥ godhūmādayaḥ |
ye vanaspatiphalāste vṛkṣāḥ kovidārakapūtayaḥ |
latā prasiddhaiva |
kṣavatnantītyavatānā nāma viṭapāḥ kaitakīvījapūrādayaḥ |
ye vinā puṣpaṃ phalanti te vanaspataya auḍmbarādayaḥ |
natu svecchāyonaceṣṭāviśeṣaḥ sthāvaratve tatu mṛtvābhāṇyorupyasti satvaṃ tayo hṛpāntarasyāpi sambhavādanena hṛpeṇābhidhānaṃ na kṛtam ||
apūtvādiṣaḍbhyodāpaḥ |
kūpasarassaryaghṛdbhavatvahaṃsaprāṇaparisāraṇaprathaktvasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvagurutvasaṃskāravatyaḥ |
pūrvavadeṣāṃ siddhiḥ |
śuklaguru-śrītā svaprakāśakhaṇḍārthaiḥ|
tattaijñaptirūpaiva vāgvihitaṃ ca dravyatvam|
vākyaṃ pūrvevad dvividhāḥ|
nityānitya-bhāvāt|
vākyaṃ tu kāryaṃ trividhaṃ yatīrendriyavi-ṣayavastvakam|
tatra śarīrandriyānijaseva vakṣamāṇokti-pārthivādayavodhapuṣmākhyopabhogakṣamathaiva|
indriyaṃ da-śendriyāṇāṃ svavyañjakaṃ vijñānānabhimate jelāvayavaidātmyaṃ rasajam|
viṣayastu kharitvasudrāhimakara-kādiḥ|
atho lakṣaṇamāha|
athutpattipradhvaṃsyātmādhāraḥ|
atrāpi vyavahārasātharyaṃ samānāsamānajātīyavyavacchedo vā lakṣaṇārthaḥ pūrvavat|
idaṃ tviha prakaṭavyam|
svayaṃ pratyakṣādipramāṇapadārthabhedaḥ parasya lakṣaṇena pratipādyate'pratipanna-sthāpratipādakatvāt|
bhedasya padārthonāmanyonyāmāvalakṣaṇaḥ sa ca yathābhāvo yatra cābhāvastadubhayagrahaṇena gṛhyate anyathā tatsvarūpamatiniyamena niṣedhānu-papatteḥ gauraśvo na bhavatīti|
tatra kiṃ vaḍūlopodrāhubhayogeṇānudetyādyāvaprakaraṇaṃ parasparaviviktayorvo vaḍūloṇahayo sāvadyayośca na bhavatīti pratītyasambhava eva parasparaviviktayorahamādibhāvapratītāvavirodhatāyathātvaṃ vijijñāsāyāmevaṃ satyabhā-vaprahārabhāvaprahāro ca vijijñāsaṇaṃ abhāva eva viveko yataḥ|
acecchati|
bhinna-yoretatsamāve na tvitaretarabhāvo bhedaḥ pradeśavadvastunaḥ prātyātmikaṃ svarūpaṃ ca yadvā bhedaḥ taddvāpadārthonānayorjātindravyādikaścaiva pratīyamānaṃ pratyekaṃ vilakṣaṇameva saṃvedyate tathāhi prayāgo nāśvadyamānāt pravartate gośabdaṃ ca na smarati tadāśvajaniśca svena svenātmanā gṛhyamāṇena tatsvarūpaniyamenānyonyā-bhāvapratītinaiyamikā|
na caivaṃ yadi vācyaṃ svarūpameva pratyakṣu kimanyonyā-bhāvaneti tattvāpi pratiṣedhaviṣayasya saṃvedanāt|
na kevalamutpannamapi vaidharmyaṃlehayaheva cārair yaduktaṃ pūrṇātvacampatirevayo vaiyatharyamiti pratipādyamtrāha |
hūmraveti |
yatra dravyānantaraṃ madhupūṣpādayaḥ kāraṇīyāḥ |
pūrvavedeśāṃ sthitiḥ |
yathā pūrvaṃ pṛthivyāṃ dravakārakhavanādeśāṃ hṛpādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ sthitiḥ pratipattistathāpyasvapītyarthaḥ |
tathā ca pūrvaṃ hṛparasasparśendya āno dravāḥ sthitāśceti |
saṃvyādipātipādakaṃ tu sādhāraṇameva yuktam |
vaiṣamyenirdiṣṭaguṇāvadher pṛthivyādisādhāraṇānāṃ rūpādīnāmaviśānatayābhivyākhyānāttasmevenaiṣāmasādhāraṇatvaṃ pratipādyate |
śuklatvādi |
śuklameva hṛdayagaṃ madhura eva rasaḥ bhīta eva sparśaḥ |
apuṣu hṛpāntaragatītirāśayahṛgramedāt kathametaditi cet tāsrāmvedāhṛtya kriyate vividhānāṃ yathasthitamācakṣatītiḥ punarnirvacitānāmāśrayahṛpānuvidhānāt |
tānu na madhuro raso guḍādivadugnibhāvanāditi cetra kaṭukaṣāyatiktalavaṇāttuvilakṣaṇasya rasasya saṃvedanāt guḍādivadugnibhāvanaṃ tu mādhuryatiśayabhāvāt |
nirviśeṣa eva leho'pāṃ vaiṣamyamiti dhvanati |
leho'mbhaso'pi |
nanvayaṃ pṛthivyāmapi vartate yathā caurai taile sarpiṣi ca na sarvatra pāṣāṇoṣṭakāṣṭhakanthanādisarambheṣāt yatu kvacit caurādiṣu dravanaṃ tatsyutakṣaṇavāyuduḥkhagatasyaiva yathā saṃkṣidguktadravatvasya cauratailayoḥ udakadharmatvaṃ tu lehasya sarvatra tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhānāt tathā cānupadeśapramāṇānāṃ takṣaṇukādīnāṃ sthiyatā jātusapradeśapramāṇānāṃ ca hṛpatā tatrāpi khatate pariskhiyamānamūlānāṃ sthiyatvaṃ sadgurihaṇāṃ ca tatrāstīti |
sāṃsiddhikaṃ ca dravatvamiti |
na kevalaṃ vedaiḥ svabhāvasiddhaṃ ca dravatvamambhaso'tyarthaḥ |
cauratailayostvāśrayasannikarṣeṇa tadupalambhaḥ kvacithoyattvopalambhāt |
pṛthivyā ivāpāmapyabantarbhedena draviṣyamityāha |
tāśceti |
paramāṇusvabhāvā āpo nityāḥ |
kāryasvabhāvāstvanityāḥ |
kārye ca vividham |
yatrāpi pūrvavadanugṛhaḥ |
yathā pṛthivyāḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayasaṃjñātaṃ kāryaṃ vividhyamevamāpāmapīti |
tatra śarīrasayonijameva |
pārthivaṃ śarīraṃ yonijamayonijaṃ ca āpyaṃ śarīramayonijameveti viśeṣa ityarthaḥ |
natu mānuṣaṃ śarīraṃ tābhyarthādhivaṃ gandhaguṇopalabdherārthaṃ tu krārṣṇītyāha |
vaṅgaṇāloke iti |
idaṃ śarīramāpāmāgamātmatvenaityam |
dravyakṣatsamāva-tvādṛśaṃ tattādṛśyaṃ śarīraṃ jalabudbudaprāyaṃ viṣṇupratyavahārayogyaṃ kaṇṭhapuṣpamopamamaye vyādityāha |
pārthivāvayavopapuṣṭatvādupayogasamaye'pi |
pārthivānāmavayavānāmupuṣṭamātraṃ saṃyogaviśeṣadātmakaṃ śarīramupayogāya samaye syāt ātmaśarīretyato pārthivāvayavā nimittakāraṇaṃ teṣāṃ saṃyogādātmāvayavānāṃ dravyatve pratibandhe viśiṣṭadehave śarīramucyate na jalabudbudaprāyamityarthaḥ |
ye tu  paśumṛtasamavāyikāraṇaṃ śarīramityācakṣate teṣāmagatyaṃ śarīraṃ syāt kāraṇagatyasyekasthānātmakatvāt cidahaṃkārasyāpi ca prāpnoti kāraṇeṣu nānāhaṃkārasambhavāt na caivaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tasmāt paśumṛtaprakṛtikaṃ bhūjalānṛkṛtikamaṇḍata  eva na syāt yata eva bhūjalānilaprakṛtikamapi na syāt bhūvāyvākāśaprakṛtikatve rūpaparasamanvaye ca syāt |
ajalānilākāśaprakṛtikatve cāgnyamartyaṃ cetyādi yathāsambhavaṃ yojanīyam |
na ca paśumṛtasamavāyikāraṇatve śarīratve'kṣatvaṃ prāpnoti svabhāvabhedena bhedopapatteḥ |
mānavaṃ śarīraṃ pṛthivyātmakaṃ  gandhavaktvāt paramāṇunavaparyantadhiveṣu utkaṭādiyabhīṣṭalambhaḥ kṣayamaneti cet saṃyuktasamavāyādityuttaram |
indriyaṃ rasajñajakaṃ ṣaveṣāṇināmiti |
ṣaveṣāṇināṃ  rasajñajakaṃ pratīndriyaṃ tajjalāvayavasārasyaṃ tathāpi kasmānnedaṃ rasajñajakaṃ  syānnānyaduktadravyamityāha |
vijñānajanimṛteriti |
vijñānebhiḥ pārthivāvayavaiyai utpattimṛtā āptihetusāmagrī ātmāvayavāste'tiriktadravyavilakṣaṇā(1)mārabdhamata idaṃ viśiṣṭotpādakadravyajakamindriyaṃ na dravyāntaraṃ tasyevamutpatyabhāvādityarthaḥ |
etena niyamadarśanādeva kalpyate rasanendriyamudbhave rasopalabdhireva pramāṇaṃ kriyāyāḥ kāraṇasādhyatvāt ātmatvaṃ kṛpādiṣu madhye rasajñajakatvāt sukhayogiṇāṃ lālādidravyavat |
viṣayanirūpaṇāthaimāha |
viṣayastvani |
ṣaritsamudro himaṃ karako ghanopalamityādi(2)viśeyo bhogyatvena  proktamopāsāyanatvāt ||
tejastvābhidhatsvandhāt tejaḥ |
rūpasparśaṃsthayāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvadravatvasaṃskāra-vatra! pūrvavadevāṃ siddhi: |
tatra śuklaṃ bhāsvaraṃ ca rūpam |
uṣṇā eva sparśa: |
tadapi dvividhamaṇukāryabhāvāt |
kāryaṃ ca dharīrādivijayam |
dharīrasthaujijamevādityaloke pārthivāvayavopasargācchoṣabhogāsamartham |
indriyaṃ sarvaprāṇināṃ rūpacakṣukamanyāvayavānabhibhūtestejomayavairārabdhaṃ cakṣu: |
viṣayaścakṣuṣāṃ caturviṃśam |
bhautaṃ divyānudbhūtaṃ mākāśajaṃ ca |
tatra bhautaṃ kāṣṭheṣṭanaprabhavasturthejalanasthamārcaṃ paṃcadaśahan svedanādisamarthaṃ divyamadityādi dauradviṣadādi bhaktasyāhārasya rasādipariṇāmārthamudbhūtaṃ ākāśajaṃ ca suvarṇādi |
tatra saṃśuktasaṃghātādasvādupalabdhiriti ||
tejastvāmisambandhyāt teja iti |
vyākhyānaṃ pūrvavat |
tejastvamiva rūpācakṣādyaṇuprayogopi tasya vaiyarthyamiti darśayati |
rūpetyādi |
pūrvavat teṣa: khiddhiriti |
yathā sūkṣmāsthavacanādūpādīnāṃ pṛthivyāṃ siddhistathā tejasyapītyartha: |
tathā ca duktam |
tejopi rūpasparśavat |
saṃkhyādiguṇanipādakaṃ tu sādhāraṇameva duktam |
prākṛṣyasya rūpaṃ tadrūpeyati |
śuklaṃ bhāsvaraṃ ceti |
śuklaṃ rūpaṃ pṛthivyudakayorapyasti kiṃ tu na bhāsvaraṃ rūpaṃ svarūpaprakāśakaṃ śuklaṃ rūpaṃ tejasyeveti viśeṣyate |
granthasya lohitaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vā rūpaṃ kvacit pratīyate tadaprayogāśikṣutvaṃ nirākṛtyasva sarvatra śuklatāmātramatīte: yathā pradīpabhāsamaṇḍalasya vairacandrādālokya ca |
uṣṇā eva sparśa iti |
pṛthivyudakasahatāmṛduṇḍaśītānuṣṇāśītasparśoṃ |
uṣṇa eva tejasa: sparśa iti vaiyarthyam |
pṛthivyudakavattejasopi dvividhamaṇuśbdena samāsagrāha |
tadyogi |
aṇu-bhāṣyāt kāryamabhāvaṃ tejo'pi dvividhamiti |
kārye ca śarīrādisambhūtaṃ |
śarīranirdṛṣṭaṃ viṣaya iti cyaṃ tejasya eva kāryam |
śarīramayonijamevādityāloke |
nu dahanātmakatvātejastaṃ tadātmakaṃ śarīraṃ bahiruṣṇaprāyaṃ viṣipraṣyavahāropayuktvāccopabhogyaṃ kalpate tathāhi |
pārthivāvayavo'ṇupramājuti |
pārthivāvayavapracayānā nimittabhūtānāmaṇuprabhānāṃ dvayeṇāviśeṣāt tejo'tmakavā upabhogyatvaṃ viśiṣṭameva śarīrasādharmye na bahiruṣṇaprāya mityabhiprāya: |
indriyaṃ dṛkaśrotrajñakṣamiti |
vaścetrāṇināṃ kṛṣṇasyañjakaṃ yadindriyaṃ tatu tejo'dvayevorucyate |
idameva kuto dṛkaśrotrajñakṣamindriyaṃ syāt nānyatejodravyamityacetpratiḥ |
atyāvaśyakatvānimitteriti |
ṭe pārthivādakāśapradeśapratibaddhasāmarthyortejo'vayavāstettaracyaṃ cakṣuḥ ataḥ idaṃ viśiṣṭatvādādupādhikyañjakamindriyaṃ nānyattu tādṛśaṃ taddhatyavate ityamāduprameva kāraṇaṃ kārye'pyevaṃ eva pramāṇam |
tejastvaṃ tu tasya rūpādiṣu madhye niyamena rūpasyāmivyañjakatvāt pradīpavat |
idaṃ tvanugraśādnugṛhītarūparyameva |
tena na svārthaṃ drahati nāpyupalabhyate |
viṣayasañjakaṃ caturvidham |
viṣaya iti sañjñā yasya tadviṣayasañjakaṃ tejaḥkāryaṃ caturvidham |
cāturvidhyameva darśayati |
bhoṣabhiṣyādi |
tatheti nirdhāraṇārthaḥ |
bhūtau bhave bhaumaṃ kāṣṭhacayasambhavaṃ kāṣṭhasambhavaṃ yadindhanaṃ tatsapād prabhavatyucyate nirapekṣasyānutpatteḥ kāṣṭhamahānuṣupalakṣaṇārtho tṛṇagulmādīnāmapi kāraṇatvāt |
abje jvalanaṃ kriyāviśeṣastatsvabhāvakaṃ taddharmakaṃ pacatyedanādisamaye pacanaṃ pūrvarūpāvilakṣaṇaṃ guṇāntarotpādanaṃ svedanaṃ snehadravanāśanaṃ cchādiśabdādvisphoṭādijvalanajalavarṣaṃ dahanaṃ tapta ghamaśītaniyathetrikriyāpracayonam |
dityamahniṣyacaraṃ cauraṃ vidyudādimayaṃ tejo'vicyandaṃ cchāpa ityevaṃ prastoti vyutpatyā tatcauraṃ vidyudādi cchādiśabdādutkocā avabodhya: |
bhuktasyāhārasya rasādipariṇāmārthemudaye udare pravaṃ tejo bhuktasyāhārasya rasapalaghātumāvena pariṇāmaprayojakam |
ākāśaṃ ca suvarṇādau |
ākāśaḥ sthānaviśeṣaḥ tasmin suvarṇīrajatādau tejasaṃ dravyaṃ jāyate |
suvarṇādīnāṃ tejastve tāvadāgamaḥ pramāṇam |
nyāyaśāstrabhihitaḥ |
kārya tahiṃ gandharasayoguṇayorabhoktṛsamarthasya ca guṇasyopalabdhirata āha |
tatheti |
mogināmadṛśyatvena bhayavasā pārthivānāṃ pārthivāvayavānāmaṇuprabhāmād-nṛdṛtahṛtpadme piṇḍībhāvayogyaṃ suvarṇādikamārambhate tacca yāthāthyadṛṣṭayabhāvetā ime rasādayo gṛhyante|
itiśabdaḥ samāptau|
