anyatheva ghaṭasya kṛtakatvasya canyacchabdasya tatra yadi nāma ghaṭasya kṛtakatvamanityatvena vyāptaṃ kṣaṇikatāvatā śabdagatatāpi tathā bhaviṣyatīti vyāmuhanto darśitayorapi liṅgasya pakṣadharmatvāvinābhāvayo: sādhyapratītyabhāve bati nigamanena tatsāditi sarvenāśā sāmānyena pravṛttaṃ vyāptigrāhakaṃ pramāṇamanusṛtyāya śabde 'nityatvaṃ pratipādyate yasmādayaṃ kṛtakaṃ tadnityamiti sāmānyena pratītaṃ na viśeṣata: tasmāt kṛtakatvenānitya: śabda iti |
etasminnu pakṣe ca prakaraṇasamakālanayamāpādṛḍhatvābhāva: pakṣavacanenaivopasaṃhṛte ṛtanācipakṣatvāśaṅkayo:(1) pakṣavacaṇatvāt |
apare tu tasmāditi caihvīmāvedṛ(2) parāmraśanti yasmādayaṃ kṛtakaṃ tadānityaṃ dṛṣṭam yasmāt kṛtaka: śabdo yasmācca pratipakṣabādhayorasambhavaktasmāt kṛtakatvāda-guṇayacce buddhādhikāre nirṇīyaniddhupaṇam |
nitya: śabda iti |
upabaṃharati tasmāditi |
yasmāt pañcamevānumāneṣu khalayasya sādhanasāmagryeṣya pratītau sādhyamatītau: pañcaivasyānti nāparaṃ kiṃcidupekṣate tasmāt pañcāvayavairevā'nyatrāpikena vākyena svanirūktavyāptyeva pratipādanaṃ kriyate iti kṛtvā yatpūrvatrāpyarthaṃ vākyaṃ paraśeṣānumānamiti viddhaṃ vyavasthitam ||
ye tu pratyakṣamevaikaṃ pramāṇamicchanto nānumānaṃ pramāṇamiti vadanti te idaṃpraṣṭavyā. kimekameva pratyakṣasvalakṣaṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ yatsvarūpe pratīyate kiṃ vā sarvameva |
na tāvadekameva pramāṇasya aparasya tatpucchasāmagrīkasyāprāmāṇyakāraṇābhāvāt |
athānītamanāgata ca puruṣāntaradṛṣṭaṃ sarvameva pratyakṣasvalakṣaṇaṃ pramāṇam |
kathamida niścīyate pratīyamānapramāṇyasyaktiścānīpratyakṣadini cet  anumānaṃ svabhāvānumānasya prāmāṇyam |
evamanumānapramāṇatvabhāve vikalpavācyam kasya pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ pratipādyate na tāvadu svātmanaiva pratipādakatvāt parasyaiva sa kiṃ pratipadya: pratipādyate vipratipanno vā na pratipanna: pratipannasya pratipādanavaiyarthyāt vipratipannaścennaivasākṣātkaraṇatā vipratipattiśca na pratyakṣeṇa gamyate vacanaliṅgaitānumīyate cet viddhaṃ kāryānumānasya prāmāṇyam |
anumānaṃ pramāṇamiti kena pramāṇena sādhyate pratyakṣaṃ vidhiviṣaya na kasyacitpratiṣedhe prabhavati anupalabdhyā gamyate cet taddhaitupalabdhiliṅgakamanumānaṃ syāt |
tathā coktaṃ baugatai: |
pramāṇetarasāmānyasthateranyavidho gatau: |
pramāṇāntaravṛddhāya: pratiṣedhācca kasya cid ||
iti |
pramāṇatadabhāvasāmānyavyavasthāpanāt parabuddhuriyamāt kasyacidvaśeṣya pratiṣedhācca pratyakṣāt pramāṇāntarasya svabhāvakāryānupalabdhiliṅgatrayānumānasya sadbhāva iti vārtikakāre iti ||
viśeṣadarśanajasambadhāraṇajñānaṃ saṃśayavirodhī nirṇaya: |
etadeva pratyakṣamanumānaṃ vā(1) |
yadu viśeṣadarśanāt saṃśayavirādhyutpadyate(2) sa pratyakṣanirṇaya: |
yathā(3) sthāṇupuruṣayorubhvaitāsāmānyavādūraśyālocanā-dvinaiveṣyapratyakṣopabhayaviśeṣānusmaraṇāt kiṃmayaṃ syātnu: puruṣo veti saṃśayotpattau ghira:pāṣāṇādidarśanāt puruṣa evāyamityavadhāraṇājñānaṃ pratyakṣaniścaya: |
viṣāṇamātradarśanādgu gaurnaveyaṃ veti saṃśayotpattau sāsnāmātradarśanādgu gaurevāyamityavadhāraṇājñānamanumānanirṇaya iti ||
nirṇeye kecittu pratyakṣānumānānyathāṃ pramāṇāntaramicchanti tān pratyāha |
viśeṣadarśanajamityādi |
yatha viśeṣānupalambhāt saṃśaya: saṃśāta: saca viśeṣadarśanājjāyamānamavadhāraṇājñānaṃ nirṇaya: |
sa ca saṃśayavirodhī tasminnutpādyamāne saṃśayasyocchedāt |
yathāha maṇḍano vipramaviveke |
niścite na khalu syāṇādyutprekṣena viśeyate iti saṃśīyate ityartha: |
yadyapi sarvatraiva niścayātmakaṃ jñānaṃ nirṇaya: tathāpi saṃśayottarakālamabhivyena prasiddhi(1)prāptatvāt saṃśayavirodhotpattau |
svārthaṃ saṃgrahavākyaṃ vivṛṇoti nirṇayasya pratyakṣānumānayorantarbhāva darśayati |
pratyakṣavetyādi |
pratyakṣaviṣaye yadavadhāraṇātmakaṃ jñānaṃ sa pratyakṣanirṇaya: |
yadyanumānaviṣaye 'vadhāraṇājñānaṃ so'numānanirṇaya iti uparitanena granthasanderśeṇa kathayati ||
liṅgadarśanecchānusmaraṇāpekṣādātmamanaso: saṃyogāviśeṣāt padārthāvādarapratyayajanitāśca saṃskārād dṛṣṭaśrutānubhūtārtheṣu śeṣānuvyavasāye(2)cchānusmaraṇadvaihetukautaviṣayā smṛtiriti ||
smṛtilakṣaṇaṃ vidyamānatve |
liṅgadarśane cchādyapekṣā |
liṅgadarśanaṃ cecchānusmaraṇaṃ ca ādiśabdena nyāyasutroktāni(3) praṇidhānādīni saṃgṛhyante tānyapyetatprāṇātmamanaso: saṃyogāviśeṣāditi(4) smṛtikāraṇakathanam |
ātmamana:-guṇapratyaye buddhyadhikāraṃ sphuṭanirūpaṇam |
kṣayopaśamya ca liṅgadravyanādisaṅkāritaye viśeṣaḥ kṣetrādāsmāt smaraṇānupapatteḥ liṅgadravyenaivatu saṃskāro'pi sphuṭoneti manaḥkāraṇamityāhuḥ |
paṭṭupratyayāsādasmātpratyayajanitāt saṃskārastvāyate |
paṭṭupratyayaḥ sphuṭatarapratyayastasmāt saṃskāro jñāyate |
tena gacchakṛguṇasaṃyaśaṅgānāt kroḍatvu paṭṭupratyayotpāde 'pi yahguṇeyathaḥ saṃskāro na bhavati |
yathā khādyātu pakṣite 'nuvāke tena gṛhītasyāhṛtyā punaḥ punarmaṃhaṇāvaṇo'pyabhyāsaḥ saṃskārakāraṇam tasmin satyanuvākasya yahgadarśanāt |
kroḍatvātpannāsvatotare vastuni bahudrupalabdhe kālāntare sphutidravyanādādasaṅgamamapi saṃskāranimittam |
dṛṣṭamutānumateṣviti viṣayaprajñānena kṛtam |
dṛṣṭe'kṣiti pratyakṣīkṛteṣu śruteṣviti śabdāvagateṣvanumateṣvanumiteṣvityarthaḥ |
śeṣānubhayavaśāyekṣākṛtānugraheturiti kāryanirūpaṇam |
śiṣyate pariśiṣyate iti śeṣaḥ anu paścādbhavacitiḥ vyavasāyaḥ śeṣaścāsāvanuvyavasāyaśceti śeṣānuvyavasāyaḥ prathamopajātaliṅgajñānāpekṣayā tadanantaro'pyanumeyajñānaṃ tasya heturyogyatāsmaraṇam sukhasādhanatvasyutiricchāhetuḥ prathamaprastutirdvitīyapadānusmaraṇahetuḥ duḥkhasādhakasmaraṇaṃ dveṣahetuḥ tadityave sphuterākāraḥ |
tatra cārthasyātītatvaṃ pūrvonubhūtatvaṃ pratīyate ityatītaviṣayā sphutiḥ |
ata eva na pramāṇaṃ tasyāḥ pūrvonubhavaviṣayatvopadarśanāthaṃ niviṣṭattvayā arthepāricchede pūrvonubhavadhārakatvāt |
anumānajñānaṃ tūtpanno parāmṛśa svaviṣaye svatantraveva sphutiriva tatsyāt pūrvonubhavānubhavatvenātrapratītyabhāvāt |
yathāhuḥ tattvaṭīkāyāṃ sarvaistattvabuddhayo guravaḥ ||
pūrvavijñānaviṣayaṃ vijñānaṃ sphutiriṣyate |
pūrvajñānādinā tasyāḥ prāmāṇyaṃ nāpagamyate ||
iti |
yathā vedamāhuḥ kārikāyām ||
tatra yat pūrvavijñānaṃ tasya prāmāṇyamiṣyate |
tadupasthānamātreṇa sphuteśca caritārthatā ||
iti |
ye tvanadhejñatvāt sphuteraprāmāṇyamāhuḥ teṣāmatītānāgataviṣayasyānmānasyāprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi dūṣaṇam ||āmnāyavidhātṛṇāmṛṣīṇāmatītānāgatavartamāneṣvatīndriyeṣvartheṣu dharmādiṣu pratyakṣanibaddheṣvapratyakṣanibaddheṣu cātmanamanasoḥ saṃyogādyu dharmaviśeṣācca yat prātibhaṃ yathārthanivedanaṃ jñānamutpadyate tadārṣamityācakṣate|
tat tu prastāreṇa devayonīnāṃ kadācideva laukikānāṃ yathā kanyakā bhavīti kṣo me bhrātā''ganteti hṛdayaṃ me kathayatīti||
ārṣe vyākhyeye|
āmnāyavidhātṛṇāmiti|
āmnāyo vedastasya vidhātāraḥ kartāro ye caturmukhastvāmanotīndranāmatītai balimāneṣvatīndriyeṣu yathārthamatikṣālampratiṣu pratyakṣanibaddheṣvāmanampratipāditeṣvapratyakṣanibaddheṣvāmanampratipāditeṣu cātmanamanasoḥ saṃyogācca prātibhaṃ jñānaṃ yathārthanivedanaṃ yathāsvarūpavedanaṃ saṃpratipattyarahitaṃ jñānamutpadyate tadārṣamityācakṣate vidvāṃsaḥ|
indriyapratihatyābhāve yatprātibhānaṃ sā pratibhā pratibhaiva prātibhamityucyate tacca bhavadbhiraḥ|
tasyotpattiranupapatrā kāraṇābhāvādityanupayoge sati idamuktaṃ dharmaviśeṣāditi|
viśiṣyate iti viśeṣo dharme eva viśeṣo dharmaviśeṣaḥ vidyātapaḥsamādhayaḥ prakṛṣṭā dharmastasmādyu pratibhodayaḥ|
tatu prastāreṇa bāhulyena devayonau bhavati kadācideva laukikānāmapi yathā kanyakā bhavīti kṣo me bhrātā''ganteti hṛdayaṃ me kathayatīti|
na cedaṃ saṃśayaḥ utprekṣādisaṃśayobhāvāt na ca viparyayaḥ saṃvādataḥ pramāṇameva||
siddhadarśane na jñānāntaraṃ kasmāt prayatnupūrvakamajanapādulepacūrṇagulikādisiddhānāṃ hṛtpadmahṛṣīṇāṃ sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭeṣvartheṣu yadṛśunaṃ tatsvata evā śraya divyāntarikṣabhaumānāṃ prāṇināṃ pṛhattvacandrajvālādinimittaṃ dharmārthasaṃvipākkadarśanamiṣṭaṃ taduguṇaganaye buddhyadhikāre khiddṛśonānaso'nukhukhāvidṛṣṭaprayukh|
etadumānameva|
atha khiṅgānamevaṃ dharmādiṣu darśanasiddhaṃ tadapi pratyakṣārthadhorāsyatārasminnatarbhūtanityevaṃ buddhiriti|
khiddṛśonamapi vicārāntaratiti kecidicchanti tannirucyathe'gāha|
khid‌darśona na jñānāntaram‌|
etadevopapādyati|
kalpādisthānidvā|
prayatnapūrvekṣamajjanapaṭhanādibhirvidvānāṃ dṛśyānāṃ darśanayogyānāṃ svarūpavattā padārthānāṃ dṛṣṭāro ye te vidvā ucyante teṣāṃ dṛśyadṛṣṭṛṇāmajjanādibhirvidvānāṃ cakṣoṣu vyavahiteṣu viprakṛṣṭeṣu pradarśanabhindriyādīnāmacakṣastvalyakāraṇeva|
atha divyānatiriktomānāṃ prāṇinā yahancakṣacakṣurādibhinna dharmoyameviṣākarasene khid‌jñānamipragṛ tasyanumānameva yahacchārīrāṃśā nihṛtvāt‌|
atha khiṅgānamevaṃ dharmādiṣu darśanasiddhaṃ tatpratyakṣārthadhorāsyatārasminnatarbhūtanityevaṃ|
yadi dharmādidarśonasindriyajaṃ tadā pratyakṣam‌|
athendriyānapekṣa tadānumityarthaḥ|
upadeheti|
evaṃ buddhiriti|
evamanantarottena kṣaṃyeṣu|
buddhiriti|
buddhivyokhyātetyarthaḥ|
itiśabdaḥ parisamāptiṃ sūcayati||
anupalabdhārthaṃ sukhamū|
svagātyasipretaviṣayasāndriye satī'pyapalabdhīndriyārthabandhakapīḍahasaṃmīkṣaṇekṣādatmanaśyau: saṃyogāhād‌(2)tugrahābhipyakṛtyaṣādiprasādajanakasutyavate tat‌ sukhamū|
ato'teṣu viṣayeṣu(4) stijamū|
anāgateṣu saṅkalpajamū|
yat‌ niduṣāmatastu viṣayānusmaraṇīcchāsaṃṅkalpayāvibhedati tadvidvāṃśamasantoṣameviśoṣanibhittamiti||
buddhyarthatvāt‌ sukhaṃ buddhyanantaraṃ vyācaṣṭe|
anupalabdhārthaṃ sukhamiti|
anugrahate u'nenetyanugrahaḥ anugrahalakṣaṇamanugrahasvabhāvamityarthaḥ|sukhaṃ hānukūlyasyānubhavastathā svaviṣayamanubhaveṃ kurvain puruṣāntaramanugṛhṇāti |
tatraiva vyākhyau |
pragādīti |
prakṛtyandanavanitādayo ye prasiddhatā viṣayāsteṣā sākṣātye vati sannikarṣe sanogopalabdhīndriyārthasannikarṣodbhūtāvapekṣāpratyakṣate tatu sukham |
vandiddhite ucyabhimate |
ye viṣayāntaravyāvṛttasya duḥkhānupādānadviśeṣalakṣye: kāraṇatvaṃ gamyate divuttasya sukhābhāvādviṣayavikalpesyāpi kāraṇatvāvagamaḥ |
yathodīcyādipadaiva svasthatodigaviśeṣaḥ |
madhupahastiprabhṛtayanātipraśādaśanakatvati kāṣṭhopavaṇīnam |
anugrahaḥ sukhaviṣaya evaidatvam |
abhiṣvaṅgo'nurāśo nayanādiprasādaśca vaiṣayyam |
ādiśabdānmanaḥprasādasya grahaṇam |
etaiṣāṃ sukhaṃ janakam |
sukhenātmanaina svānubhavo janyate sa evātmano'nugrahaḥ sukhe copajāte sukhādīnāṃ pravṛttatā syāt |
sukhasādhaneṣvanurāgaḥ sukhānuvṛttiḥ |
atīteṣu smṛtijam atīteṣu sukhasādhaneṣvanubhūteṣu sukhaṃ puno'tsmaraṇādbhavati anāgate'smin me bhaviṣyatīti yadulpādyamāne |
yattu viduṣāmātmajñānavatāmakṣatvaṃ viṣayānusmaraṇānukūlyeṣadati viṣaye ubhati cānusmaraṇe ubhati ca yadulpe cāvirbhavati tadvidyāprasaktopaśamaviśeṣanimittamiti |
vidyā cātmajñānam yayā jitendriyatvam ghattobho dehasthitihetumātrābhiniveśātmabhimānāṅkurasya dharmaviśeṣaḥ prakṛṣṭo dharmo nivṛttikalaṅkayuḥ etaccatuṣṭayanimittam |
ye tu duḥkhābhāvameva sukhamāhusteṣāmānandātmanānubhavavirodho hitamādhyami ahite hāsyamīti pravatidveṣayorupapattiśca ||
upaghātalakṣaṇaṃ duḥkham |
viṣāyunamipretaviṣayakṣātriṣye satyaniśopalabdhaundriyārthasannikarṣodbhūtamaudayaikṣādātmamanasoḥ saṃyogā(1)yadutpadyapacānādainyanimittamucyate tadduḥduḥkham |
atīteṣu (2) kṣudhaivyādhaśokādiṣu smṛtijam |
anāgateṣu bhaddhūṣyajamiti ||
sukhaprasaṅgoktatyā tadanantaraṃ duḥkhaṃ vyākhyeya |
upaghātalakṣaṇaṃ duḥkham |
upahanyate 'nenetyupaghātaḥ upaghātalakṣaṇamupaghātātmakamabhāvam |
duḥkhamupaghātaṃ prati-kṛtavyabhicāreṇa svātmaviṣayamanubhavaṃ kurvedātmānamupalabhanti |
etaddvṛṇoti |
anigrṛhalakṣyītyādinā |
amaro'sahiṣṇutā dveṣa iti yāvat upaghāto|
duḥkhānubhavo dveṣya vicchāyatā teṣāṃ nimittam |
duḥkhe sati tadanubhavalakṣaṇaṃ ātmīyaghātaḥ syāt |
ato'niṣu ṣaṇodiṣu spṛtijam anāmateṣu bahuṣvajanimiti pūrvaṃ vyākhyānam ||
svārthe parārthe vā'prāptaprārthanecchā |
sā cātmamanasoḥ saṃyogāt sukhādyupekṣāt svarūpapekṣādvetyucyate |
prayatnasvatidharmotharmehetuḥ |
kāmo'bhilāṣaḥ rāgaḥ sakṛtvaḥ kāruṇyaṃ vairāgyam upadhā bhāva ityevamādya icchābhedāḥ |
maithunecchā kāmaḥ |
abhyavahārecchāmilāṣaḥ |
punaḥ punarviṣayānurañjanecchā rāgaḥ |
anāsannakriyecchā sakṛtvaḥ |
svārthamanapecya paraduḥkhaprahāṇecchā kāruṇyam |
doṣadarśanādviṣayatyāgecchā vairāgyam |
paravañcanecchā upadhā |
antarnigūḍhecchā bhāvaḥ |
cikīrṣājihīrṣetyādikriyābhedādicchābhedā bhavanti ||
svārthe parārthe vā'prāptaprārthanecchā |
aprāptasya vastunaḥ svārthe prati yā prārthanā idaṃ me bhūyāditi parārthe vā prārthanā asyaidaṃ bhavatviti secchā |
sā cātmamanasoḥ saṃyogāt sukhādyupekṣāt svarūpapekṣādvetyucyate |
anāgate sukhādyupekṣe vastunīcchā upajāyate tadutpannau ca tadviṣayasārthaṃ sukhamanāgatamapi buddhividdhyatyādibhirinakāraṇam |
eṭāha nyāyavārttikakāraḥ |
phalasya prayojakatvāditi |
atikrānte sukhahetāvapicchotpatteḥ smṛtiḥ kāraṇam |
prayatnasvatidharmodharmehetuḥ |
upādānecchātatvasaṃgrahaḥ prayatno bhavati smaraṇecchātaḥ smaraṇam vihiteṣu jyotiṣṭomādiṣu phalecchayā pravṛttasya dharmo jāyate |
pratiṣiddheṣurāgādi pravṛttyātmake: |
kāmādayo'pi santi te kasyācetrāṃ yatra āhu |
kāma ityādi |
kāmādaya icchā prabhedā: na tattvāntaramiti yaduktaṃ tadeva dṛṣṭayeti |
neṣṭhanecchā kāma iti |
nirūpaṇadaḥ kāmaśabdo neṣṭanecchāyāmeva pravartate anyatra tasya padāntarasamabhivyāhārāt pravṛttiḥ |
yathā svagekāmo yajeta iti |
abhyavahārecchā abhilāṣaḥ abhyavahāre bhojane tacchecchā abhilāṣaḥ |
punaḥ punarviṣayānusandhānecchā rāgaḥ punaḥ punarviṣayāṇāṃ bhogecchā rāga ityarthaḥ |
anāsaktikayecchā saṅkalpaḥ anāgatasyārthasya karaṇecchā saṅkalpaḥ |
svārthamanuddiśya paraduḥkhaghātecchā kāruṇyam svārthamuddiśya kimapyacikīrṣanyāya yā paraduḥkhaghāte apanayane icchā sā kāruṇyam |
deṣṭadarśanādduḥkhahetutvāttatrāpte viṣayāṇāṃ parityāge icchā vairāgyam |
paradvastunecchā paramātārthecchā upekṣā |
anatrnaipṛṇyecchā liṅgapariṇāmikā cecchā sā bhāvaḥ |
cikīrṣā jihīrṣā ityādikriyābhedādicchābhedā bhavanti |
karaṇecchā cikīrṣā haraṇecchā jihīrṣā gamanecchā jigamiṣetyevamādaya icchābhedāḥ kriyābhedānuvanti ||
prajvalanātmako(1) dveṣaḥ |
yasmin sati prajvalitamivātmānaṃ manyate sa dveṣaḥ |
sa cātmamanasoḥ saṃyogād duḥkhāpekṣāt svarūpekṣādvotpadyate |
prathamasyatithamoganahetuḥ |
krodho dvaiṣaḥ manyuramarṣaṇe iti dveṣabhedāḥ ||
prajvalanātmako dveṣaḥ |
kṣatadveṣaṇeti |
yasminnu kṣatītyādinā |
tadyuktam |
sa cātmamanasoḥ saṃyogād duḥkhāpekṣāt svarūpekṣād vā utpadyate |
(2)kṣatite duḥkhahetau tajjaduḥkhamutpitau dveṣaḥ |
prathamasyāyamasphutihetuḥ ghanamahaṃ hanmīti prathamaḥ dveṣāt vedārthavimukhatādiṣu dveṣādayaḥ tadaye'parādhanayantu dveṣādayaḥ |
sphuṭarūpi dveṣādupajāyate yo yaṃ dveṣṭi sa taṃ ghātaṃ smarati |
krodho droho manyuramarṣaṇa iti dveṣabhedāḥ |
śarīrendriyādivikāraheturbādhātmabhāvo dveṣaḥ krodhaḥ |
aśaktitavikāravicchedānubandhāpakārāvadhāno dveṣoguṇābhyuccayai dvayaprayatnaśūnyatvanirūpaṇam |
drohaḥ |
capakṛttasya pratyupakārābhāve'dhyātmanirguṇo dveṣo manyuḥ |
parapaṇadveṣo'sūyā |
svaguṇāparibhavasamuttho dveṣo'marṣaḥ ||
prayatnaḥ saṃrambha utsāha iti paryāyāḥ |
sa dvividho jīvanapūrvakaḥ icchādveṣapūrvakaśca |
tatra jīvanapūrvakaḥ suptasya prāṇāpāna(1)santānaprerakaḥ prabodhakāle cāntaḥkaraṇasyendriyāntaraprāptihetuḥ |
asya jīvanapūrvakasyātmasanadoḥ saṃyogādrusodhasāpekṣādvṛtvastiḥ |
itarastu hitārhitaprāptiparihārasamarthasya vyāpārasya hetuḥ śarīravidhārakaśca |
sa cātmasanadoḥ saṃyogādicchāpekṣādvṛ dveṣāpekṣādvṛtvacate ||
prayatnaḥ saṃrambha utsāha iti paryāyāḥ |
sa dvividho jīvanapūrvaka ityādi |
sadehasyātmano viṣayasānakarmaprayayahetasya manasā saha saṃyogaḥ sambandho jīvanam tatpūrvakaḥ prayatnaḥ kāmarthakriyāṃ karotītyata āha |
tatra jīvanapūrvaka iti |
suptasya prāṇāpānakriyā prayatnakāryā kriyatvāt |
na ca tadānīmicchādveṣau prayatnahetū sambhavataḥ tasmājjīvanapūrvaka eva prayatnaḥ prāṇāpānaprerako gamyate na kevale jīvanapūrvaka eva prayatnaḥ prāṇāpānaprerakaḥ kiṃ tu prabodhakāle 'ntaḥkaraṇasyendriyāntaraprāptihetutvam viśeṣopalambhānumitāntaḥkaraṇendriyasaṃyogaḥ prayatnapūrvakāntaḥkaraṇakriyājanyo'ntaḥkaraṇendriyasaṃyogatvāt jāgradantaḥkaraṇendrayasaṃyogavaditi prayatnapūrvakatvāsiddhiḥ |
asya jīvanapūrvakasyātmasanadoḥ saṃyogādrusodhasāpekṣādvṛttiḥ abhoryasāpekṣa cātmasanadoḥ saṃyogo jīvanaṃ tasmādṛtsotpattirityarthaḥ |
itarastu icchādveṣapūrvakasya hitārhitaprāptiparihārasamarthasya vyāyāmasya vyāpārasya hetuḥ śarīravidhārakaśca gurutve satyapatanaḥ śarīrasyocchrāyakaiḥ prayatno vidhārakaḥ(2) sa cātmasanadoḥ saṃyogādicchādṛṣāpekṣādvṛtvacate hitasādhanopādāneṣu prayatnaḥ icchāpūrvako duḥkhasādhanaparityāge prayatno dveṣapūrvakaḥ||
gurutve jalabhūmyoḥ patanakarmakāraṇam |
apratyakṣaṃ patanakarmānumeyaṃ saṃyogāprayatnasvaṃskāravirodhi |
asya cābādhiparamāṇurūpādivaddravyānityatvaniyamaḥ(3) ||gurutvaṃ jalabhūmyorityāśrayakathanam |
patanaikīkāraṇamiti tasya kāryanirūpaṇam apratyakṣamiti svabhāvopalakṣaṇam |
na kenacidindriyeṇa gurutvaṃ gṛhyate ityarthaḥ |
ye tu tvagindriyagrāhyaṃ gurutvamāhuḥ |
teṣāmaya: sthitasya dravyasya sparśopalambhavadgurutvopalambhaprasaṅgaḥ tvagindriyagrāhyopalambhe svabhinnakarmotpattirekaṇāpyasaṃbhavabhāvāt |
anuparisthitasya gurutve pratīyate tadyuktādīnāmadhobhāgamanānumānāt atīndriya caivaṃ kathamasya pratītirityata āha |
patanaikīkāraṇamiti |
yaddravyavidravasya patanaṃ tena yatkāryaikasamavetasamavāyikāraṇaṃ tadeva hi no gurutvam |
etenaitat pratyuktaṃ pṛdkṣaprayeraḥ |
avayavidrutatvakāryasthācanativiśeṣasyānupalambhādavayavini gurutvābhāva iti avayavinaḥ patanābhāvaprasaṅgāt |
athāvayavānāṃ gurutvādeva tasya patanaṃ tadvāvayavānāmapi svāvayavaigurutvāt patanamiti sarveṣa kāryaṃ taducchedaḥ |
atha vyadhikaraṇebhyaḥ svāvayavagurutvebhyo'vayavānāṃ patanāsambhavāt teṣu gurutvaṃ kalpyate tadā avayavinyapi kalpanīyam nyāyasya samānatvāt |
yat punaravayavigurutvasya kāryātireko na gṛhyate tadavayavāvayavigurutvabhedasyāpyāntaratvāt |
yathā mahati dravye unmīyamāne tatpatitasūkṣmadravyāntaragurutvakāryagrahaṇam |
vaiśeṣikapravacanaskāravirodhi |
gurutvasya saṃyogena prayatnena vegākhyena ca saṃskāreṇa bahu virodho vidyate taiḥ pratibaddhasya svakāryākaraṇāt |
tathā ca ṭrolākuṭṭasya saṃyogena pratibadhnyādayatanaṃ prayatnena pratibadhnyādayatanaṃ ca śarīrasya vegena pratibadhnyādayatanaṃ bahiḥ bibhratasya śarayabhāvakāle |
asya cājādyutpattau prāgutpattitvānityatvaniyamaḥ |
yathā'paramāṇuṣvādayo nityāstathā pāṣāṇādyaparamāṇuṣvapi gurutvam yathā cā'ṇudikāryadravye kāraṇaguṇapūrvaprakrameṇa dvyādayo jāyante āśrayavināśācca vinaśyanti tathā gurutvamapi ||
dravatvaṃ spandanaikīkāraṇam |
tridravyavṛtti |
tattu  dvividham sāṃsiddhikaṃ naimittikaṃ ca |
sāṃsiddhikamapāṃ viśeṣaguṇaḥ |
naimittikaṃ pṛthivītejasoḥ sāmānyaguṇaḥ |
sāṃsiddhikasya gurutvavaccityānityatvaniyamatvayaḥ |
ghṛṅgātadrthānāt sāṃsiddhikamaṇukamiti cenna |
himyate tejasāsaṃyuktānāmākhyānāṃ paramāṇūnāṃ parasparaṃ saṃyogo dravyārambhakaḥ ṣaḍūtāvadyaḥ tena paramāṇudravatvapratibandhāt kāryaṃ dvimakarakādyaṃ dravatvānutpattiḥ|
naimittikaṃ ca pṛthivītejasoranityasaṃyogajam|
katham ṣaḍavidhetubhyūrcchiṣṭādīnāṃ kāraṇeṣu paramāṇuṣvanityasaṃyogādguṇāpekṣāt kasmotpattau tajjanyo vibhāgebhyo dravyārambhakasaṃyogavināśāt kāryadravyanivṛttāvanityasaṃyogādyapekṣāt svatantreṣu paramāṇuṣu dravatvamutpadyate tatakṣeṣu bhaugināmapṛthagbhāvādātmāṇusaṃyogāt kasmotpattau tajjanyaḥ saṃyogebhyo draṇukādiprakrameṇa kāryadravyamutpadyate tasmiṃśca rūpādutpattisamakālaṃ kāraṇaguṇaprakrameṇa dravatvamutpadyataiti||
dravatvaṃ spandanakarmakāraṇam mate spandanakarmakāraṇaṃ taddravatvamityarthaḥ|
svidyadvuni pṛthivyudakajvalanatrayāṇītyarthaḥ|
nanu dvividhamiti|
gurutvavekadravyaṃ tu dvividhamiti sūtraśabdārthaḥ|
naimittikaṃ sāṃsiddhikaṃ ca|
nimitte ca bahiḥsaṃyogāstasyedaṃ kāryamiti naimittikam|
sāṃsiddhikaṃ ca svabhāvasiddhaṃ bahiryogānapekṣamiti bhāvatu|
sāṃsiddhikamapi viśeṣaguṇo'ntyabhāvāt naimittikaṃ pṛthivītejasoḥ|
ṣāmānyaguṇaḥ sādhāraṇatvāt|
sāṃsiddhikasya dravatvasya gurutvavadanityatvanityatvavibhāgamya|
yathā nityadravyasamavete gurutvaṃ nityam anityadravyasamavete ca kārye kāraṇaguṇapūrvakamāśrayavināśādinaśyatīti tathā sāṃsiddhikaṃ dravatvamapi|
atra codyati|
ṣaḍūtāvadyo't sāṃsiddhikadravatvamutkramite cet ākāśya dvimakarakāderdravyasya ṣaḍūtāvadyo't kāṭhinyadarśanādapāṃ svabhāvasiddhaṃ dravatvamityuktam|
samādhate|
divyeneti|
ṣarvaṃ codakaṃ svabhāvasiddhadravyatvayogatambhāveṇa svabhāvasiddhameva dravatvaṃ nādhatiriktam yatra tu dvimakarakādau kārye dravatvānutpattistatra divyena tejasā sambaddhānāmākhyāparamāṇūnāṃ parasparasaṃyogo dravyārambhakaḥ ṣaḍūtāvadyo yastena dvimakarakārabhma-kāryaṃ paramāṇūnāṃ dravyatvapratibandhāt tejaḥsaṃyogena paramāṇūnāṃ dravyatvaṃ pratibaddhamiti anyathāpyadravyatva laghutvasya bahiḥsaṃyogena dravyatvapratibandhadvāreṇānumiteṣu laghutvasyāpyatvamapi himakarakādidravyakālāntarīya dravībhāvadarśanenānumānam jalamanaṃ tu himakarakādermānindriyogrāhya yadilaghutvaṃ kaṭhinadravyasya tadbahiḥsaṃyogādanagatam yathā suvarṇādīnām himakarakādivilayanamapi vilayanameva |
tasmādihāpi draṣṭasāmagryo bahiḥsaṃyoga eva nimittamāyodyate |
śābdikaṃ dravyatvaṃ vyākhyāya naimittikaṃ vyācaṣṭe |
naimittikaṃ pṛṣṭhajotejaḥsaṃyogajanitam |
kathamityucena vṛttaḥ ṣaṭpadamudayati |
saṃbherityādinā |
saṃbhervastupūrṇacchadrānāṃ pāṣāṇānāṃ kāraṇeṣu paramāṇuṣvaniṣpaṃyogāt kriyotpattau satyāṃ krameṇodhvo vibhāgebhyaḥ saṃbheravārambhakasaṃyogavināśāt saṃbheradidravyanivṛttau satyāṃ svatantreṣu paramāṇuṣu bahiḥsaṃyogād dravyatvamutpadyate tadanantaramutpannadravatveṣu paramāṇuṣu bhogināmanugrāhyavādātmaparamāṇusaṃyogāt kriyotpattau satyāṃ krameṇodhvaṃ: paramāṇūnāṃ parasparasaṃyogebhyo dvyaṇukādikrameṇa kāryadravye jāte drapādyutpattikāle eva kāraṇadravyebhyo dravyatvamutpadyate |
himakarakādivilayane'pyevameva(1) nyāyaḥ ||
dvedhā'yaṃ viśeṣaguṇaḥ |
saṃgrahadhvajādihetuḥ |
asyāpi gurutvavadanityānityatvaniyamatayaḥ ||
dvedhā'yaṃ viśeṣaguṇaḥ saṃgrahadhvajādihetuḥ |
saṃgrahaḥ parasparamṛdūnāṃ cakṣādīnāṃ piṇḍībhāvaprāptihetuḥ saṃyogaviśeṣaḥ pṛthvā kāryasyoddūtonādikṛtā śuddhiḥ śrādiśabdānudravatvaṃ ca teṣāṃ hetuḥ |
dvaidhasyāpi gurutvavadanityānityatvaniyamatayaḥ gurutvaṃ ca paramāṇuṣu nityaṃ kārye ca kāraṇaguṇapūrvakamāyathavināśādināśi tathā dvedhā'pi ||
saṃskārastrividho vego bhāvanā sthitisthāpaka(2)śca |
tatra vego mūrtimatsu pañcasu dravyeṣu nimittaviśeṣāpekṣāt karmayogajāyate(3) niyatadikkriyāprabandhahetuḥguṇapañcake saṃskāranirūpaṇam |
sparśāvṛddhyantayogāviśeṣavirodhī kriyikāsaṃskāraguṇo'pūrvakarmaṇotpadyate |
bhāvanāśabdakartṛtvātpuruṣo dṛṣṭaśrutānubhūtebhyaśca smṛtipratyabhijñānahetubhūtavati jñānamadṛṣṭakhādivirodhī|
padmavyākhyāmāhapratyayajaḥ padupratyayāpekṣā|
dātmanaścauḥ saṃyogā|
drākṣartha'rthe paduḥ saṃskārātiśayo  jāyate|
yathā dākṣiṇātyasaumyadeśanāditi|
vidyāpitṛpuṇyāyāmādiṣṭaśrayasmāneṣu tasminnevārthe pūrvepūrvasaṃskāramapekṣamāṇāduttarottarasmāt pratyayādātmanaścauḥ saṃyogāt saṃskārātiśayo jāyate|
prayatnena manaḥprabṛddhi sthāpayitvā'pūrvamartha didṛkṣamāṇasya vidutsphuraṇavadvarśanenavadādarapratyayaḥ tasmādevamāṇādātmanaścauḥ saṃyogāt saṃskārātiśayo jāyate|
yathā daivahūde rājataśaikharyapadmadarśanāditi |
sthitisthāpakastu sparśavaddṛḍhadravyeṣu vartamāno ghanāvayavasanniveśāviśiṣṭeṣu kālāntarāvasthāyiṣu svāśrayamanyathākṛtaṃ yathāvasthitaṃ sthāpayati|
sthāvarajaṅgamavikāreṣu dhanuḥśākhāgrahastāntāsthisūtravastrādiṣu bhugnasaṃvartiteṣu sthitisthāpakasya kāryaṃ dṛlokyate|
nityānityatvaniṣpattyorapyāpi gurutvavat ||
saṃskārastvindriyo vege bhāvanāsthitisthāpakaśceti|
tatra vege prakṣematsu paśudravyeṣu nimittaviśeṣāpekṣāt karmaṇo jāyate|
paśuṣu dravyeṣu pṛthivyaptejovāyumanassu karma vega karoti nānyatra svapramāṇāt nodanābhighātādinimittaviśeṣāpekṣaṃ na kevalaṃ mandagati|
vegābhāvāt|
niyatadikkriyāprabandhahetuḥ|yadvṛttāmimukhyena kriyayā vego janyate tadvṛttāmimukhenaiva kriyāsantānasya heturityarthaḥ|
sparśendriyati|
viśiṣṭena sparśendudravyasaṃyogamātrāntanirviḍā-vayavavṛttinā vego vinaśyate yaḥ svayaviśiṣṭaḥ mandastu vegaḥ sparśendudravya-saṃyogamātreṇa vinaśyati yathātidūra gatasyerṇadistaminavāṣpṛnibaddhasya|
kṛcchiditi|
vāyulyena tāvadvegaḥ kameja|
kṛcchidūgrāvadravacārasthe jalāśaye'pi kāraṇavegeyo'pi jāyate|
bhāvanetyādi|
bhāvanādhairyakastu saṃskāra ātma-guṇaḥ|
dṛṣṭaśutānubhūteṣviti|
dṛṣṭaśutānubhūteṣvartheṣu smṛteḥ pratyabhijñānasya ca heturiti tasya kāryekathanam|
dṛṣṭaśutānubhūteṣviti viṣayoparāgo'payego boddha-vyastatrāpi sphutidarśanāt|
jñāneti|
pratipakṣajñānena saṃskāro vinaśyate|
dvītā-divyasanāpadaśca prabodhotivismaraṇāt|
madenāpi saṃskārasya vināśaḥ surāma-nasya pradeśaviśeṣāt|
maraṇādiduḥkhādapi saṃskāro vinaśyati janmāntarānu-bhūtasmaraṇābhāvāt|
ādiśabdena sukhādiparigrahaḥ|
vegāvasthasya kṛpitasya vā pūrvavṛttasmṛtyabhāvāt|
paṭumyāseti|
paṭutvamyāsadubyāsabhāvātpathādāvṛtanpathādau saṃskāro jāyate|
paṭumyāsayordvādātmyamanasoḥ saṃyogaviśeṣādātmaguṇe'ye paṭuḥ saṃskāro jāyate|
prayeti|
uddeśa dākṣiṇātyasyātyantānubhūmekālsvādātmaguṇa-kṣetoryaḥ tadṛśebhāt tasya paṭuḥ saṃskāro jāyate kālāntare smṛtyutpādakasvasuni-jananāt|
abhyāsavātyayarjaṃ saṃskāraṃ darśayati|
vidyāśilpetyādi|
vidyāśā-strāmasādikā śilpaṃ patrabhaṅgādikriyā vyāyāma cāyuryādiyamaḥ tairutpatsyamā-neṣu sattviśeṣeṣu prabṛddhote|
pūrvetyādi|
yataḥ suciramanuvartate sphuṭataraṃ ca smaraṇaṃ karoti na hyādāvanubhava eva saṃskāraviśeṣamāyeta prathamaṃ taduṣṭesmaraṇā-bhāvāt nāpyantara eva hetuḥ pūrvottarāvasthayostasmāt pūrvasaṃskārāpekṣo-ttarottarānubhavāhitārthikādhikasaṃskārotattikrameṇopacayasyasaṃskāropacādantyānu-bhavāt tadutpattiḥ||
idaṃ liṅgaṃ nirūpyate|
vidyāmānasyamānāyāṃ kiṃ tadyogyaṃ vākyena pratipādyate kiṃ vā sphoṭe'the kṛtaḥ saṃśyeśo vipratipatteḥ|
yadyo vadanti sphoṭo'rthe pratipādyateti|
apare tvāhurvaikarthaṃ pratyāyakamiti|
ato yuktaḥ saṃśayaḥ kiṃ tāvadgrāhyaṃ sphoṭo'rthapratyāyaka iti|
yadi hi varṇota-ritarastaṃ padaṃ padāntaritastaṃ ca vākyaṃ tadyarthapratyaya eva na syāditi|
tathāhina varṇāḥ pratyekamarthopākhyānaṃ sphuraṇamātraṃ bodhayanti śabdabodhaikayogyatvāt anudāttādyadhya teṣāṃ na sambhavati yantrasthaghoṣasāmye tulyabodhasambhavāt |
nityasvārthayoginā sāsti samudāya iti cettu tathāpi na teṣāṃ pratītiranubhūyate apratīyamānānāṃ ca pratyakṣatve sarvadā'pratītiprasaṅgaḥ |
nahi pratītya apratīyamānānāṃ ṣaḍbaṃśā apratīyamānānā ca kācitsiddhiḥ |
pūrvairagatā varṇāḥ sphuratyāhaṅkāraḥ pratītihetavaḥ iti cettu yadi hi sphuraṇāpi kramamājino tadā nāsti varṇo'yamityamu tṛtīyavarṇohaṅkārakāle prathamavarṇocchūtivilopāt yugapadutpādatvu sphurīṇāmanā-śaṅkanīya eva jñānayogapadyamatiśayāt |
atha prathamapratyakṣavarṇajñānaṃ tadanu saṃskāraḥ tadanu tṛtīyavarṇajñānamu tena prāktanena saṃskāreṇānyo viśiṣṭaḥ saṃskāro janyate ityevaṃ krameṇānte nikhilavarṇaviṣayaḥ saṃskāro jāto nikhilavarṇaviṣayamekādeva sphūrtiṃ yugapadu karotītyānīyate tadā kramo hīyeta kramo hi paurvāparyaṃ tacca deśaniyamyaṃ vā kālaniyamyaṃ vā syāt ubhayamapi natṛ-guṇu cāvakāśaṃ labhate teṣāṃ sarvagatatvādityatvācca |
buddhikramaniyamyamastu varṇānāṃ kramo bhavetu ca caikasyāṃ sphūrtibuddhau padavarṇamānānāṃ pratyastamita ityakṣamāṇāmeva pratipādakatvamu |
ataśca khare ratho vane carma madhau daurnetyā-diṣvapyekapratyayo na syādarthānāmabhedāt kramasya pratītyanuttvācca |
asti cāyaṃ pratītibhedaḥ sarvalokanupayuktamānatvahetunirastaṃ nimilāntaramācikṣatīti sphoṭasiddhiḥ |
nanu sphoṭo'pi nāmaviśeṣya ko'rtha pratipādyati sarvadā'pyupalabdhi-prasaṅgāt |
abhivyaktiśca na tasya varṇebhyaḥ sambhavati uttena nyāyena teṣāme-kaikataḥ samuditānāṃ cābādhasambhavāt tasmāt sphoṭādapi durlabhā arthapratītiḥ |
atra vadanti prathamedānupātino vāgvedinaḥ svatantraḥ pratyekameva tadvṛttispho-ṭasya sphoṭamasphuṭamabhivyañjayantaḥ pūrve viṣayasaṃskārā'vicchedalābhādante spho-ṭamābhāsayante |
tathā cānte pratyastamitanikhilavarṇavibhāgo'khaṇḍaikamanavayavaśra-traikaṃ viṣṇupraṇetṛtvamanubhūyate yadi hi varṇo eva padaṃ na tadekabuddhiviṣayo-'mīti anālambanā buddhiḥ paryavasyāti śabdārtho pratipattāmahe iti ca vyapadeśo na ghaṭeta tatpāruṣeyātiriktaḥ ko'pi sambhavatyeko yasmādarthaḥ sphu-ṭībhavatīti |
yartha prāpte pratiṣedhite |
guṇaratnākaragatā: kāyasthakulanindakāḥ pāṣaṇḍudāya ityādiṣu padeṣu drāgnaimiṣeṣu kramamājino varṇāḥ eva pratīyantena svapne varṇeṣvatiriktasya kasyacidarthasya saṃvedanamasti |
yadi hi satya pūrve varṇoccārātmanā saṃvidetastasyānte svapnupadvedanaṃ pūrvoccārasya mithyātvamavabodhate raktacākṣuṣeva guktikārthacitto na cedaṃ pratipattirasti nāyaṃ varṇo: kiṃ tu sphoṭa iti |
yā ceyamekārthe'samaṃjasī buddhi: sāpi nāthāntarasyabhāvayati kiṃ tu varṇānusmayavadanubhavamātramavalambate śabdādarthe pratipadyāmahe iti varṇopaghradrāyamaveśarībhatya loka: prayuṅkte |
na ca pratyekamapratīyamāna: pramāṇāntarata: śakyo nirdhārayitumupāyābhāvāt |
athopratītyanyathānupapattittadūṣaṇa iti cenna kimatīyamāna: sphoṭo'thepratibhāse hetu: samadhigato bhavadbhi: pratīyamāno vā apratīyamānasya hetutve khadyotaprabhoto'pahatu: pratītisya satya nāstotyuktam athopratyayo varṇānāmeva sadbhāvapratibālamanugṛhṇāti tataivāmeva vara cakṣurādīnānusāriṇo'rthapratipādane kaścidanugrāhya āśrīyatāṃ na punaraprayamānasya gaganakusumasurabhereva kalpanā yuktā |
na caidaṃ bāhyaṃ varṇonā pratipādakatve kramabhede kramabhede vyavadhāne ca pratotisvarūpa iti |
na hi te viparītakramā: kramabhedānubhūtito deśakālasvabhahitāstadyogyā: kāraṇaṃ kāryabhedyā hi śaktayo bhāvānāṃ yathā tebhya: kāryaṃ dṛśyate tathaiva teṣāṃ śaktaya: kathyante |
athopadiśyanti santa: |
yāvanto yādṛśā ye ca yadarthapratipādane |
varṇā: prajñāsamāyogaste tathaivāvabodhakā: ||
iti |
sarvaikātvānnityatvācca varṇānāṃ kramabhāva: |
ata eva nadīdīrghitvādivadyobheda: kramabhedāt |
varṇeṣu kramo nāsti sa krayamevākramaṃ khyāditi caitra teṣāmuccaritānāmadhyāpayatonāṃ deśakālakṛtasya paurvāparyasya sambhavāt |
yatraivamuktaṃ pratyekya: samuditānāṃ ca na śābadayomiti tadapi na parasya matamālocitam |
yadyapi varṇo anavasthāyinastathāpi tadbuddhyā: kṣaṇabhāvina: saṃskārā: saṃpraya padārthadhiyamātmavante |
yadvā pūrvepūrve saṃskārasaṃskṛyottarottarasaṃvidottyā varṇai: pratyakṣa: yathā cākṣepasaṃskārā: saṃpraya smaraṇaṃ janayanti tathopapāditaṃ dvitve |
atha manyase varṇovidyāt saṃskārārthadhīyatītirayuktā saṃskārā hi yadviṣayodvalambasubhāvinastanmanastadvyāpeva sphuṭimānāvahubhīyate na kāryāntaram |
yathāha maṇḍana: sphoṭavidbhi: |saṃskāraḥ khalu yaddṛṣṭe'nudbhūtamapyābhāsitāḥ |
phalaṃ tatraiva janayatyatīte'pi kṣīṇe kalpyate ||
iti |
tatprathamacodanīyam |
yataḥ padārthamatītyanuguṇatayā pratyakṣamanumeyeṣvadhīyamānā varṇairviṣayāḥ saṃskārāḥ smṛtihetusaṃskāravilakṣaṇāścāntara pratyādhīyante tathābhūtānāmeva teṣāṃ kāryaṇādhigamāt vastu vā bhāvanādrūpāḥ saṃskārāstathāpi teṣāmarthanipātanavaśamaśrayamupapadyate taddvārabhāvietvāt |
ye hi sphoṭaṃ kalpayanti tena sphoṭasyāpreṃtipādanaśaktirapi kalpyate'neti kalpanāgauravam upayābdasya saṃskārasya sāmarthyamātrakalpanāyāṃ tu lāghavamastīetyetadeva kalpayitumucitam |
yathoktaṃ nyāyavārtike: |
yadyapi smṛtihetutvaṃ saṃskārasya vyavasthitam |
kāryāntare'pi sāmarthyaṃ na tasya pratihanyate ||
iti |
tataiva varṇebhya eva saṃskāradvāreṇārthapratyayasambhavādayuktā sphoṭakalpaneti |
ādarātyantayoḥ saṃskāraṃ darśayati |
prathamanetyādinā |
ādaraḥ prathamatiprayatnasāmarthyavaimukhyamayaṃ dṛṣṭamicchati prativastusparśānavadhairyavadvaiyardhaṃ tadavasthatvaprastāvopakṣamaṇādāstvamanadhaiḥ saṃyogāt saṃskāratiyogo jāyate cirakālānikṛṣṭe'pi tasyānucchedāt |
acodāharaṇam |
yathā devahūde ityādi |
devahṛde caitramāsasya citrānakṣatrasaṃyuktāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāmardharātre rājatāni vaiḍūryāṇi ca padmāni dṛśyante iti bālīmavayasya tasyāṃ tithau didrakṣayā sthilitānāṃ śabdayaugamānu'dherāpi prathamātiprayatnapūrvakaṃ manaḥ sthāpayitvā sthitānāmātyateṣu padmeṣu kṣaṇamātradarśanādāpyātyantapragāḍha saṃskāratiyogaḥ kālāntare'pi sphuṭatasmṛtihetukupajāyate |
sthitisthāpakaḥ kathayati |
sthitisthāpakastvanyaḥ |
atyantadṛḍhavyuttamobhavanātkhāt saṃskārāt svayaṃvedṛḍhavyutttitvena sthitisthāpakasya viśeṣamākhyātuṃ tṛṇaśabdaḥ |
de yanā niviḍā ṛcayavaśabdaviśeṣāḥ tairviṣayeṣṭeṣu sarveṣatsu dṛṣṭeṣu vartamānaḥ sthitisthāpakaḥ svāśrayamanyayathākṛtamavanāmitaṃ yathāvatsthāpayati pūrvavadvṛttaṃ karoti |
ye pratyatetīnupalabhmāt sthitisthāpakasyābhāvamicchanti tān prati tasya kāryeṇa bahudhā darśayedvāha |
vyāvartyānumavikā ityādi |
suptāḥ kubjīkṛtāḥ svaśarīraināḥ pūrvavasthāṃ prāptitāḥ suptāścate vaṃśalitāmbheti bhūmyadhalitā: teṣu sthitisthāpakasya kārye lakṣyate |
jighṛkṣaṃ syāt dhanu:śākhādidravyanāminavighnate'ṣu yat pūrvoktavyāghrādihetorāghasya karmaṇa: ekārthasamavetamasamavāyikāraṇaṃ sa sthitisthāpaka: saṃskārākhyatvāptambhāt |
kānye tu bhūmyadhalitetyatti vṛkṣavalkādityatti atyetaṃ viśeṣaṇamiti manyamānā: bhūgāni yāni vṛṣādīni salitāni teṣviti vyācakṣate |
tasya nityānityatvanirṇayo gurutvavat |
yathā gurutvaṃ paramāṇuṣu nityaṃ kāryeṣvanityaṃ kāraṇaguṇapūrvakaṃ ca tathā sthitisthāpakopītyartha: |
dharma: puruṣaguṇa: |
kartṛ: kriyāhetutve mokṣahetu: ṛtostriyo'ntyajukhasaṃvijñānavirodhī puruṣānta:karaṇāsaṃyogāviśuddhāmiṣandhija: varṇāśramiṇāṃ pratiniyatakarmadhanaliṃgastu: |
tasya tu sādhanāni śrutismṛtivihitāni varṇāśramiṇāṃ sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvenāvasthitāni dravyaguṇakarmāṇi |
tatra sāmānyāni sarve śraddhā ahiṃsā bhūtahitatvaṃ satyavacanamasteyaṃ brahmacaryamanupadhā krodhavarjanamabhiṣecanaṃ śucirdravyasaṃcayaṃ viśiṣṭadevatāmastikyaparāveśā pramādaśca |
brāhmaṇasyaitayaivasthānāni tathyā adhyayanadānāni brāhmaṇasyaviśiṣṭāni pratigrahādhyāpanayājanāni svarṇavihitāsya saṃskārā: |
kṣatriyasya samyakṛprajāpālanamadhaṃsādhunigraho yuddhe'nivartanaṃ svakīyāsya saṃskārā: |
vaiśyasya krayavikrayakṛṣipaśupālanāni svakīyāsya saṃskārā: |
śūdrasya pūrvevarṇopāratantryamantrakādya kriyā: |
āśramiṇāṃ tu brahmacāriṇo gurukulanivāsina: svaśāstravihitāni guruśuśrūṣāgnīkṣanabhaikṣācaraṇāni madhumāṃsadivāsvaprāśunābhya-grāmyamanye dharmanindupakṣam |
jhanādivarjaṃ ca |
vicāraśatakātakasya hṛtavārasya gṛhasthasya prācīnayāyāvaracūmpājirdevatharbhūtamanuṣyadevapitṛbrahmasthānānāṃ pañcānāṃ mahāpañcānāṃ sāyaṃprātarṛṣṭtānam ekāgnividhānena pākayajñasaṃsthānāṃ ca nityānāṃ yaktau viśvamānāyāmananyaṣeyādīnāṃ ca haviryajñasaṃsthānāmagniṣṭomādīnāṃ somayajñasaṃsthānāṃ ca |
ṛtvantareṣu brahmacaryamapatyotpādanaṃ ca |
brahmacāriṇo gṛhasthasya vā prāgnāgnīnotasya vanavāso valkalājinabheṣajasmaśrunakharoma dhāraṇaṃ ca |
vanyakṛtāti thiśeṣa bhojanāni vānaprasthasya |
trayāṇāmanyatamasya addhāvataḥ sarvabhūtebhyo nityamabhayaṃ dattvā saṃnyasya svāni karmāṇi yamaniyameṣvapramattasya ṣaḍpadārthaprasaṃkhyānāyogaprabhāvanaṃ pravrajitasyeti |
dṛṣṭe prayojanamanudiṣṭaye tāni sādhanāni bhāvaprasādaṃ cāpekṣātmakamanasoḥ saṃyogādbharmotpattiriti ||
dharmaḥ puruṣaiḥ |
yo dharmaḥ sa puruṣasya śuṇo na kramaviṣayapravṛttimindriyaiḥ |
kratuḥ priyahitamevāvihetuḥ |
priyaṃ sukhaṃ hitaṃ sukhasādhanaṃ sokto najñānāmātmavi śeṣaguṇānāmanyatamo'kleṣṭadastṛtāṃ hetuḥ |
kratuḥ priyādīnāmeva yo hetuḥ sa dharma iti vyākhyeyam |
na tu kratureva yaḥ priyādihetuḥ sa dharme iti vyākhyā |
puṃseṣa kṛtasya yāddaśya pitṛgāmitṛguptakāyavaśāt |
iṣṭikāmena kārīṣaiḥ kṛtāyāṃ tadva nyasyāpi samopadeśabalena |
iṣṭiphalasambandhadarśanāt svargakāmo yajetetyā divākye yogena svargaṃ kuryāditi karmebhyaḥ śreyaḥ sādhanatvaṃ prayuñjate |
yasya tu puḥ ya yena puruṣaṃ saṃyunakti sa dharmaḥ tatsādhanayāgādikameva dharmo na puruṣaguṇaḥ tathāhi yo yāgamanutiṣṭhati taṃ dhārmikamityācakṣate |
etadayuktam |
laṇikasya karmaṇaḥ kālāntarabhāviphaladāyakatvādavaśyabhāvi|
ṛṇo'ddhaṃne|
kṣāṇikaṃ karma kālāntarabhāvi ca svakāryaṃ vicichādya kāraṇamut kāryamānutpādi yugaṃ ca yāgādeḥ kāraṇatvaṃ tadetadanyāyyānupapannamayā phalotpattyanuguṇaṃ kiṃcidapi kā|
gantra-tāvasthāyi karmasāmarthyaṃ kalpyate yadvāreṇa karmeṇāṃ yutā phalādhāyanena nirve|
di tacca pramāṇāntaragocaratvādṛdurbodhamiti vyavadiśyate yathoktam|
phalāya vihitaṃ karma dāṇikaṃ cirabhāvine|
tatsiddhirnānyayetyevamadhūrvamapi kalpyate||
ṛṇo'dyate|
na karmasāmarthyaṃ dāṇike karmaṇi dhruvamiti śaktimati vinaṣṭe nirāśrayasya sāmarthyasyāvasthānamasaṃbhavāt|
svagodikaṃ ca phalaṃ tadānīmanāgātameva na śaktirāśrayo bhavitumarhati|
yadi tvatpurājānantarameva svargo bhavati ṛṣuvekalpanāvaiyarthyo'pi tadupayogasya durnivāraḥ|
viśiṣṭaśarīrendriyādivirahādananubhavaścet tarhi cayaṃ tadānīmanupajāta eva svargo'pyekakṣamāvasthāt ṛnupayogyamapi duḥkhakhaṇḍamastīti ṛnugṛhītavyameva|
tasmād phalajāyamānudeśaṃ|
na cākāśādisamavetādṛṣṭādātmagāmiphalasaṃbhavaḥ|
vastugate ca kāryamanārthakaṃ nopapadyate tasmādātmasamavetasyaiva tasyotpattisthitinupūrveyā tathā iti ca tatkarmasāmarthyaṃ syāt anyathāsāmarthyasyānyathāsamavāyāt athānyasyāpyanyasamavetā śaktiriṣyate tasyāḥ kāyonumeyatvāditi cet|
yathoktam|
śaktiḥ kāryānumeyā hi pratūrvevāpalabhyate|
taduttero'pyatetasyā svāśrayānyāśrayāpi ca||
iti|
tadayuktam|
vinaṣṭe śaktimati tatsiddhetasya śaktimātrasya kāryajanakatvānupapattādeva tanaitadapi pratyuktam|
yadyuktaṃ maṇḍanena vidhiviveke tadāhitatvāt tasya śaktiriti yāganāhitatvādapūrve yāgasya kārye syāt tu śaktirutpekṣetāt karmeṇaḥ phalānutpatteḥ tasyādicaravinaṣṭe karmaṇi vai śaktāvasthāyisvaṅkāriṇo'pūrvodeva phalasyotpatterupapatteśca trayaḥ śābdanam|
kāraṇatvasṛttistu yāgāderapūrvajananadvāreṇa na sākṣāditi pramāṇānurodhādanapayoge'pi|
tathā iti yuktaṃ sarvaṃ|
eṣapūraṇa iti|
yo'pi yāgādau dharmasyadeśaḥ so'pyapūrvevāyanatayāguṇadharmaprakṣepanihnavaśca |
prautiśākhtrena tu svaprokṭaprayogaḥ |
svaprokṣādhyāye hi bauddhitattva jyotirguptasya  nirantaraṃ prautiśākhyanatathārthavādena stuteranyatrāṭṭau ca praunigrahaṇe buddhi prayogāt  tadabhāve bākyogāt prautinibandhanaḥ svaprokṭaḥ |
yasya yathālakṣaṇayā prautiśā khyane prayogaḥ prautiśāstribhyāne ipti tasmāt prautiśākhyanamnatītyutpatteḥ samprayā bhidhānaśaktikalpanā'pekṣaṇīyā |
evaṃ dharmaśabdasyā'pi lakṣaṇayā tatsādhane prayogaḥ  ikābhidhānādevekṣaṇayatītibuddherubhayābhidhānaśaktikalpanā'navakṣāpātiti tāṃ trikāṇāṃ prakriyā |
atīndriyaḥ kevalavidindriyaprāyogibhirnaiva gṛhyate ityatī ndriya dharmaḥ |
anyanukhukhavijñānaviṣayo |
dharmehetāvat kṣaṇotpādādyukhaṃ vi nāśo na ca nirhetuko vināśaḥ kṣatthavidyudale anyatastato vināśe vā'sya niya mena prakṣayotpattikāle yābadakṣasthānaṃ syāt |
phalaṃ ca dharmasya kṣasthavidyudanekakṣaṇa rahasyopabhogyaṃ tasya yadi prathamopabhogādanu nāśaḥ kṣaṇāntare phalāna tyādaḥ |
na caikasya niḥśeṣasya bhāṣaśe|
nāśaḥ saṃbhāvyate tasmādravyavaśeṣa |
ghaṭyaguṇiṃśātena dharmo vinaśyate|
ye tu nityaṃ dharmamāhusteṣāṃ prāthayānupapattiranau cītyamevabhāvāt |
puruṣāntaḥkaraṇena |
ātmaviśeṣaguṇatvāt sukhādivat |
viśuddheti|
viśuddhe'piśabdiḥ ṭaṅkamodarahitaḥ |
īṃkalpaviśeṣaḥ tasvādayo jā yate|
vadhopakriyādvati|
vadho ghātaścādviṣayavidyudrāḥ |
ācāryapraṇo brahmacāri gṛhasthānāpraśyamatamaḥ|
teṣāṃ dharmaḥ pratiśiṣṭaṃ pratyācārya cā'ptikṛtya vihiteḥ śāstravai jainyate ityarthaḥ |
atīndriyasya dharmasya śāstrādi kṛtaḥ pratyetavyāni tatsāha |
tasya hetavaḥ |
viśiṣṭenānuśayenā'cāryeṇāgrahaṇaṃ yadyat dhava na tu liṅgitvā gṛhyate  iti śutivedaḥ sphutisaṃvādiśāstraṃ sādhyaṃ vihitāni pratipāditāni vaṇo'śra miṇāṃ dharmānyaviśeṣabhāvenā'sthitāni dṛṣṭaguṇakarmāṇi dhāmānyāni sarveṣā dhanāni |
tava sarveṣāṃ varṇo'śramiṇāṃ ca śāstrānyanuṣṭheyāni dharmasādhanāni  kathyante |
catve śraddhā dharme manaḥprasādaḥ |
ahiṃsā bhūtānāmanabhidrohaścekṣaṇaḥ |
pratividdhasyāmidrohasya nivṛtteradharmo na bhavati na tu dharmo jāyate |
anabhi drohaścekṣalpastu vihitatvāt syādeva dharmasādhanam |
pūrvahitatvaṃ bhūtānāmanugrahaḥ  satyavacanaṃ yathārthavacanam |
asteyamayācitamadekaṃ parasvagrahaṇaṃ kṣamā na kartavya-nimittasaṅkalpaḥ na tu paraskhādānanivṛttimātramātrakuṇḍapaṃ |
brahmacaryeṣu strīviṣāparivarjanaṃ |
śatadarpaṃ saṃkalpakuṇḍapaṃ |
anupapadyā māvaśuddhiviśuddhamabhiṣāṭheṣa kṛtānāṃ karmaṇāṃ dharmasādhanatvāt |
krodhavarjanaṃ krodhaparityāgaḥ |
ko'pi saṃkalpātmaka eva |
abhiṣecanaṃ snānaṃ |
śucidṛṣṭaṃ ceṣṭavena śucīnāṃ tilādidravyāṇāṃ kuśispatraiṇi niyamena daivaṃ dharmasādhanaṃ |
vidhipūrvaistātrikasprayoktamatānāṃ devatānāṃ maktirityarthaḥ |
upavāsaḥ yakṣodaśyādibhojananivṛttisaṃkalpaḥ |
agramādo nityanaimittikānāṃ karmaṇāmavaśyambhāvena kāraṇaṃ |
etāni sarvametaiva samānāni dharmasādhanāni |
icchayā yāgahomānuṣṭhānaṃ |
adhyayanaṃ vedapāṭhaḥ |
dānaṃ svadravyasya parasmātsādhisaṃkalpaviśeṣaḥ |
śuddhadravyāpi dānamastyeva tena yajñādiṣu yaddānaṃ tadābhimukhyenaiva caityādīnāṃ vidhiṣu dharmasādhanamuktaṃ |
brāhmaṇasya viśiṣṭānyavācārarāṇi dharmasādhanāni pratipādayati |
pratigrahastyāpanagrāhaṇāni |
pratigraho viśiṣṭadravyagrahaṇaṃ |
adhyāpanaṃ tu prasiddhameva |
yājanamānvikyamadhu |
etāni brāhmaṇasya dharmasādhanāni tasyāmīmāṃsaiṣaṇatrayorjitānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ dharmopayogitārā |
svakarṇavihitātmaśuṣyatvāri granthaskārā vaidikakarmānuṣṭhānenopayatāpādanadvāreṇa brāhmaṇasya dharmasādhanaṃ |
kṣatriyasya viśiṣṭāni dharmasādhanāni |
kṣamyakaṃ prajāpālanaṃ nyāyapusonāṃ prajānāṃ parirakṣaṇaṃ |
akṣamuṣṭinyavaḥ duṣṭānāṃ yathāśāstraṃ śāsanaṃ |
yuddhasvanivartanaṃ yuddheṣu vijayaprāptiḥ prāṇatyaktvā agrapalāyanaparaḥ |
svakṣetrasya saṃskāraḥ |
vaiśyasya kṛṣivaṇikpaśupupālanāni |
mūlyaṃ dattvā parasmād dravyagrahaṇaṃ krayaḥ |
mūlyamādāya parasya svadravyadānaṃ vikrayaḥ |
kṛṣiḥ parikarṣitāyāṃ(1) bhūmau bījasya vapanaṃ ropaṇaṃ ca paśupālanaṃ gojāvikādiparirakṣaṇaṃ |
etāni vaiśyasya dharmasādhanāni |
tasyāmīmāṃsaiṣaṇāyorjitānāṃ dhanānāṃ dharme'ṅtvāt |
śūdrasya pūrveṣu varṇeṣu pāratantryamaryādikāsya kriyā dharmasādhanaṃ |
ātmamiṇāṃ tu dharmasādhanamucyate |
brahmacāriṇo gurukulanivāsina iti |
upanīya yaḥ śiṣyaṃ sāṅgaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca vedamadhyāpayati sa guruḥ tasya kule gṛhe vasanaśīlasya brahmacāriṇa' svadhāstravihitāni brahmacāriṇāmadhikṛtya śāstreṇa vihitāni(2) |
guruśuśrūṣā guroḥ paricaryā guruśuśrūṣā ca agninamaskṛtyaṃ ca śaucaṃ ca teṣāmāca-guhūmanye dharmaniṣkṛṣaṇam |
sthāni guhūṣugumphaṣeṣvagyoḥ kāraṇayevācaraṇam |
kamanīcācaraṇamṛ pratyahamenau  hemaḥ kucyanatyācaraṇāmanyathe vanādinyanyajñādaraṇamiti vivekaḥ |
gṛhasthasya  dharmasādhanam kathayati |
vidyātattvātmakasyeti |
yo vedādhyayanārtho gurudīrnaṃ  hatamayatīte vede viṣayajijñāsādya sa vidyātrahmacārakaḥ tasya gṛhasthārasya kṛtapalī parigrahasya gṛhasthasya śālīnayāyāvarudyupāttite'pretanujydevapitṛbrāhma ṇyānāṃ pañcānāṃ mahāyajñānāṃ sāye prātaranuṣṭhānam |
yāvatā dhānyena kuṭumbāpārtha  kumpīpākè(1)śa parigṛhyate cyaṅgamekāhaṃ vā vartane bhavati tāvanmātrasya parigra svaya mānīyamānasya śraddhayā dīyamānasya vaḥ parigrahaḥ sā śālīnā vṛttiḥ parasmādayāci yaṅgamekamādāya yanyatsūkṣmaṃ niṣkāmyaṃ vā yāyāvaravṛttiḥ tāsyāmupārjite ṣṭaye pañcānāṃ mahāyajñānāṃ prātaraparāhṇe vānuṣṭhānaṃ gṛhasthasya dharmasādhanam |
pratiśraye  balipadānaṃ bhūtayajñaḥ |
atithipūjanaṃ manuṣyayajñaḥ |
haumo devayajñaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ pitṛ yajñaḥ |
brahmayajño vedapāṭhaḥ |
pañcāniciśrayānena pākayajñasaṃsthānāmiti |
caturguṇam  yajñādipariti śrībhāṣakāraḥ tasya vidvāṃsaṃ vivāhakāle parigrahaḥ tena pākayajña saṃsthānāṃ pākayajñaṣiśeṣāṇāmahṛtāpravargīyecyāṣṭakagrahyādīnāṃ nityānātmasaṃskṛ raṇīyānāṃ sati sāmarthye udguṇam |
kumbhyādīyādīnāmiti |
caturguṇaṃ dharmasā dhanam |
kumbhyādheyaśabdenānyāyāṣanasthāmidhāyattam yadguṇāṣṭaguṇaṃ vakṣyate kriyate |
haviryajñasaṃsthayā haviryajñaviśeṣā dākṣāyaṇamāsavāturmāsyāganayaṇādikā iṣṭayaḥ(2)  kathyante |
agnihomādīti agnihomeṣṭyayagoṣḍapaurṇamāseyātirācāgniṣṭomāḥ kham  somayajñaviśeṣāḥ somasaṃsathetyabhidhīyante |
kratvantareṣviti |
kratumukājñādanya kāle brahmacarye vratacaryāṇāṃ strīviṣāparivarjanaṃ dharmasādhanam |
cāṇḍālotpādanakarṣi  dharmasādhanam putreṇa lokāñjayatīti śruteḥ |
vanasthasya dharmasādhanaṃ katha yati |
brahmacāriṇo gṛhasthasya veti |
yadaharvāhsya śraddhā bhavati tadaharvāhyaṃ  pravrajediti śravaṇāt yati śraddhodaye brahmacāriṇo vanavāso bhavati gṛhasthasya  vā tasya śāntapraśratamānavatto valkalājinadīkṣamuṇḍakharomyāraṇyam vanyasya  phalamūlasya bhojanaṃ hutaśeṣabhojanam atithiśeṣabhojanaṃ ca dharmasādhanam |
yaḥ prājāpatyāmivṛddhiṃ niṣkṛṣya varṣeṣu dakṣiṇāṃ dattvātmanyagniṃ samāropya puro mārgaṃnikṣipya prakṣipecca na tasya homo na tasyānikṣiparigrahaḥ|
yastu bahvalpāḥ havyahāninā balaṃ pracchannaḥ tasya kulabhṛṣomājanamatithibhojanaṃ ca dharmasādhanam|
yatiryame nihnupayati|
yathānāmiti|
yasyānayamaparigrahe'pi niyamo nāsti śraddhopagame kṣati brahmacaryeṣu yatirbhavati gṛhastho vā bhavati vānaprastho letyenābhiprāyeṇoktaṃ nyāyānāmanyatamasyeti|
śraddhābalaḥ vidyamaśābdabalaḥ sarvametanyo nityamamaye dattvā bhūtāni mayā na jātu hiṃsitavyānīti caturohabdakulpaṃ gṛhītvā sthāni kameṇāpi kāmyāni saṃnyasya parityajya yamaniyamameṣvamanasya yahiṃsādayastādayo yamāḥ|
yathāha bhagavān patañjaliḥ|
ahiṃsāsatyāsteyabrahmacaryāparigrahā yamāḥ iti|
tapaḥ svādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāḥ|
yathāha ca yama bhagavān|
śaucasantoṣatapaḥsvādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāḥ iti|
nyāyamādhyākārastu pratyāsannaṃ nimittaṃ dharmasādhananimayamāha|
teṣvapramattasya sārvakālikapramattaḥ|
ṣaṭpadārthairasaṃkhyānāt ṣaṇṇāṃ padārthānāṃ tattvajñānādaupasātmakajñānotpāda-nakṣamatvasya kṣamāviśeṣasya pradānānurūpādānaṃ prakṣipatasya dharmasādhanam|
yathaitāni dharme sādhayanti tathā kathayati|
duṣṭaṃ ceti|
duṣṭaṃ cāpuṇyajātidayojanamadṛṣṭeṣvānabhisaṃdhyāya yadetāni sādhanāni kriyante tadetāni sādhanāni bhāvapradhānaṃ cābhiprāyaviśuddhiṃ cāpekṣyātmamanasaḥ saṃyogādudbhavantīti|
pratyarthaṃ duḥkhairabhihanyamānasya tattvato vicāreṣu duḥkhaikāntadāneṣu viṣayeṣu viraktasyātmantike duḥkhavirodhinicchanna cātmavyātiriktamanyā hānopāyaḥ tasyālpa kṣamāviśeṣe nibandhanamiti śrutavataḥ saṃnyasya sarvakāmyakarmaṇi kṣamāvimanutiṣṭhataḥ tattvasyānaikapramṛṣṭaṃ yānaṃ parityajya dhanamāditasya yama-niyamābhyāṃ kṛtātmasaṃskārasya kṣamābhyāsādhanivekādu dharmo jāyate|
tasmādyamaḥ prakṛṣṭaḥ kṣamāvistato'nyaḥ prakṛṣṭataro dharmaḥ tasmādapyuttamaḥ prakṛṣṭatamaḥ kṣamāvistyajena kṣameṇānte janmāni ca sādhuṣu kṣamāviśeṣaiḥ pari-bhramati|
ye dṛṣṭenādhyavasādhitaṃ na śakyate dṛṣṭā hi kiñcidunmataṃ viṣaya-mādarśanānucintayataḥ tadekāgrībhūtacittasya śuddhihetuṣu pravekṣyati viṣayeṣuguṇapracye dharmanihnavādam |
uktaśco yatheṣṭākāra iśe labdhalabdhyātmayogo gacchannāmapi rājānaṃ na bubudhyate |
tathā ca bhagavān patañjaliḥ |
kathyādhavairāgyābhyāṃ tannirodha iti |
evaṃ pariṇate samādhāvātmasvarūpāvaśātkārivijñānamudeti |
yathāhuḥ kāpilāḥ |
evaṃ tattvābhyāsānnāsmi na me nāhamityapariśeṣam |
aviparyayādiśuddhaṃ kevalamutpadyate jñānam ||
iti |
ata ātmajñānārthinā yatinā yogaśāstramanuṣṭhīyate |
jñānaṃ caiyādiṣṭādhisādhanaphalam śrotātmajñānenāpyātmasvarūpaṃ prāpyate kimasya dhyānābhyāsāt pratyavekṣaṇeneti cetra |
parokṣasya pratyakṣadhāyane sāmarthyāsaṃbhavāt |
svarūpatastāvadātmā na kartā na bhoktā kintudāśīna eva |
tatha dehendriyamanvanyāyupādhikṛto'haṃ mameti kartṛtvabhoktṛtvadhratyayo mithyātmakeistāditi bhāvāt |
yattu-kṛtasyānubhūteṣu rāgaḥ pratikūleṣu dveṣaḥ tābhyāṃ pravṛttinivṛttī tato dharmādharmo tataśca saṃsāraḥ |
yathoktaṃ vaiyānaiḥ |
ātmani khalu parakhabrahmā svaparavibhāgān parigṛhadevam |
anayoḥ saṃniyogādyāḥ sarve bhāvāḥ prajāyante ||
anādivāsanāvākita iti prabandho niruddhuḥ sarve: sāṅkhyabauddhādikaiḥ pratyagātmaiṣa pratyayaḥ |
śrautamātmatattvajñānaṃ tu varṣiṣṭhamanupalabdhasaṃvādaṃ parokṣaṃ ca na ca duḥkhataraḥ pratyavāyabhāvaḥ parokṣābhāvena yacchate niṣeddhum |
na hi yatne'pi pramāṇāntarābhāve guḍasya mādhuryaṃ duṣṭendriyajaḥ tittiribhāsastṛṣṇātaśca duḥkhāvāmo nivartate |
tasmāt pratyakṣajñānārthaṃ samādhirupādīyate'yaḥ yaśceta samādhau tattvasākṣātkṛtau kartṛtvabhoktṛtvapariṇamyātmatattve sphuṭībhūte samāne viṣaye vidyavividyorvirodhādahaṅkāramamakārāvanecchade śavarṣo prapañco nātmānaṃ spṛśati |
tathā ca kāpiladṛṣṭāntaḥ |
tena nivṛttaprasavāmadheyaśāhti sadarūpavinivṛttau |
prakṛtiṃ paśyati puruṣaḥ prekṣakavadavasthitaḥ svasthaḥ ||
tena tattvajñānena satā nivṛttaprasavā nivṛttopabhogajananasāmarthyod jñānapramavetarasyarṣaprotyamokṣānāvekṣapramāṇamavagamyaḥ sadarūpe'yo vinivṛttau prakṛtiṃpūrvaḥ|
naiṣakarmyasiddhauṣīnaḥ svastho rajastamobhiranibhūyamāno buddhyā capramiśraḥ paśyatītyarthaḥ|
yadvāpyamandādariya mokṣavāsanā ādimadhya tattvajñānāṃ guruḥ tathāpyanena vā nirdvandvena tattvāvabodho hi dhiyāṃ paramaṃ balam ||
adharmo'pyātmaguṇaḥ|
kartṛtvahitapratyavāyahetur-tīndriyo'ntyaduḥkhasaṃvijñānavirodhī |
tasya tu sādha-nāni śāstre pratiṣiddhāni dharmasādhanaviparītāni hiṃsācauryasteyādīni vihitākaraṇaṃ pramādaśce-tāni duṣṭānisargasya cāpekṣyātmanasoḥ saṃyogā(3)-dadharmasyotpattiḥ ||
adharme ityātmaguṇaḥ|
na kevalaṃ dharmo'dharmo'pyātmaguṇaḥ|
kanūriti|
kanūrahitaṃ du khasādhanaṃ pratyavāyo duḥkhaṃ tayoradharmo hetuḥ|
antyaduḥkhasaṃ-vijñāneti|
antyasya duḥkhasya saṃpravijñānaṃ tena vināśane|
tasya tu sādhanaṃ na śāstre pratiṣiddhāni dharmasādhanaviparītāni hiṃsācauryasteyādīni|
hiṃsā parā-niṣṭhehaḥ|
anṛtaṃ mithyāvacanam|
steyamaśāstrapūrve parasvagrahaṇam|
prabrajā-dīni dharmasādhanaviparītāni śāstre pratiṣiddhāni yāni nāpyadharmasādhanāni|
vihitākaraṇaṃ vihitasyāvaśyakaraṇīyasyākriyaivyatā|
pramādo'buddhipūrvako 'nikramaḥ|
yanadapi dṛṣṭamadharmasādhanam|
prayatnebhyo 'dharmasyotpattistadvarṣa-yati|
etāni ceti|
yatra kāmānuvekamadharmasādhanānuṣṭhānaṃ tatra duṣṭo'bhi-sandhiḥ adharmakāraṇam|
akāmakṛte tu pramādenāsya hetutvam ||
aviduṣo rāgadveṣavataḥ pravartekādvarmot prakṛṣṭāt svalpādharmasaṃvalitāt brahmendraprajāpatipitṛmanuṣyaloke-ṣvādhāyānubhūtihṛṣṭatarerindriyaviṣayasukhādibhiryogo bhavati|
tathā prakṛṣṭādadharmāt svalpadharmasaṃvalitāt pretati-ryagyonisthāneṣvaniṣṭapraterindriyaviṣayaduḥkhā-guṇapañce idharmasaṃvāraṇavarge'ṣṭaṣaṇṇam |
dvimiśrīgo bhavati |
evaṃ pravṛttilakṣaṇādgīvadharmasaṃhitādvevamanuṣyatiryaṅnārakeṣu punaḥ punaḥ saṃvārabandho bhavati ||
yathamadharmasya vādhamanabhiprāya raṃgati śāstraṃ kathayati |
avidyuṣa ityādinā |
yaḥ kartā śrotrāśrotryātmānamabhimanyate paramārthato duḥkhavādhanaṃ ca bāhyādhyātmikaviṣayaṃ sukhasādhanamityabhimanyate ye'vidvāṃsa śa ca svopabhogatṛṣṇāparigataḥ sukhasādhanatvatoṣite viṣaye rajyate taduparāgiti ca dviṣṭo bhavati tasya pravartakādgarmodveśo vā syo gandharvo vā syāditi punarbhavaprāyaithanā kṛtādṛ dharmāt prakṛṣṭāt phalātiśayahetorāśrayānurūpaiḥ karmottarairupaśobhitādibhiḥ sambandho bhavati brahmendrādityānepi bhāvyo duḥkhasambedo'sti |
na cādharmodayadduḥkhavedo'sti na cādharmodayadduḥkhasya kāraṇam ataḥ svalpadharmo'pisādityuktam |
yasya prakṛṣṭo dharmastasya prakṛṣṭāni śarīrādīni bhavanti yasya su prakṛṣṭataro dharmaḥ tasya prakṛṣṭatarāṇi bhavanti yasya tu prakṛṣṭatamo dharmastasya prakṛṣṭatamānīti pratipādayitumāśrayānurūpairityuktam |
kṣupraśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ śarīrādiṣu sambadhyate |
dravyunnataraṃ prayogāt |
tathā prakṛṣṭāddharmet pretayonīnāṃ tiryagyonīnāṃ ca sthāneṣvindraiḥ śarīrādibhiryogo bhavati pretādisthāne iti bhāvānuguṇamasti taddha dharmasya kā'raṃ ataḥ svalpadharmo'pisādityuktam |
upasaṃharati |
yathemiti ||
jñānapūrvakātu kṣatādurṣakāṣittaphalā dviguṇe kule jātasya duḥkhavipākopāya jijñāsorācāryaudasadṛnyotpatraṣaṣṭapadāptaitattvajñānasyājñānanivṛttau viraktasya rāgadveṣakṣayabhāvāt tajjayordhvaurdhvamairyogatulyato pūrvasaṃcitāghraupabhogātnirāse dantoṣasukhaṃ śarīram-vicchedairśrotrādau tāgādinicitau cihnatilakṣaṇaiḥ kevalo dharmaḥ paramārthadarśanajaṃ sukhaṃ kṣatvā nivartate |
tadā nirodhāt nirvījasyātmanaḥ śarīrādinivṛttiḥ punaḥ śarīrādacutacyate'to duḥkheṣvanānulavadrupadharmo mokṣa iti ||
evaṃ dharmāt saṃsāraṃ pratipādyāpubhayaṃ pratipādayati |
jñānapūrvekāru ityādi |
svargāpavargaśarīradāsīnāṃ bāhyādhyātmikānāṃ ca viṣayāḥ sarve bhavete duḥkhasādhanamiti yasya jñānamutpannaṃ sa dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayavaitṛṣṇya yadamaha prabhāvāmiti vai prabhāsūriti phalākāṅkṣayā binā nivṛttasādhanetayā vihitāparaṃ bādhyakaraṇīyaṃ karma karoti |
tasmāt karmaṇo jñānapūrvekāt kṛtādasya viśuddhe kule janma bhavati akṛśīnasya śraddhā na bhavati na cāpadravyānasya jijñāsā sampadyate na cājijñāsostattvajñānaṃ tadvimuktasya ca nāsti mokṣaprāptiḥ |
ato mokṣānuguṇadharmaphalitvātphalaṃ karma viśuddhe kule janma prāpnuyāt viśuddhe kule jātasya pratyahaṃ duḥkhairabhihanyamānasya duḥkhavinirmokṣāya jijñāsā sampadyate kṛte'pi khalvasya mama duḥkhāpagamaḥ syāditi sa caivamabhijātajijñāsu ācāryamupasampanneti tasya cāyāyopadeśāt ṣaṇṇāṃpadārthānāṃ prīteḥ tattvajñānaṃ jāyate |
tadanu praśamayamananididhyāsanādikrameṇa pratyakṣaṃ bhavati |
utpannatattvajñānasyājñānanivṛtto saṃśayanivṛtteścājñānanivṛttau ciragṛhītasya vicchedāgṛhārthasaṃkārasya rāgadveṣayorabhāvāt tattvajñānadharmādharmayorutpādaḥ kriyāvaśenaenibaddhā hi pravṛttayasturābaddhāḥ iva tatpuruṣāḥ pravartante bodhiṣu kriyeṣu nivṛttaṣu iva tatpuruṣāḥ kārye na pratiṣandhanyate |
yathāha bhagavān pātañjaliḥ |
kṣati dhūle tadvṛttiko jātyāyurbhogā itiḥ |
yathāha bhagavāncakrapādaḥ |
na pravṛttiḥ pratiṣandhavyānāṃ hīnakṣetrasyeti |
pūrvadharmajanitayośca dharmādharmayo-śubhapakṣe upakarmanirūpaṇam |
nirodha upayogādyu nivṛttiniścalahetostatha karmotpādādyu khantoṣasukhaṃ śarīraparicchedaṃ cātvāca ragādinivṛtto nivṛttilakṣaṇa: kleśo yathā: paramārthadarśanaṃ sukhaṃ kṛtvā nivartate |
nivartate saṃsāro'nayeti nivṛtti: nivṛttilakṣaṇo yathārthaṃ nivṛttilakṣaṇo nivṛttisvabhāvo yatho ragādinivṛtto bhūtāyāṃ kleśo vyavasthita: khantoṣasukhaṃ śarīraparicchedaṃ cātvāca paramārthadarśanenajamātmadarśanaṃ sukhaṃ karoti tatkṛtvā nivartate |
āmimānikārthavinirodhād tadā nirbandhyātmana: śarīrādinivṛtto puna: śarīrādyanutpatto durgatyacanāvalambadukhayamo śoka: |
yadā paramārthadarśanaṃ kṛtvā nivṛtto yatho: tadā nirbandhyātmana: śarīrādivināśayamoyamerahitatvātmana utpādānāṃ śarīrādīnāṃ karmabandhanivṛtto bhūtāyāmanāgatānāṃ kāraṇābhāvādanutpatto yathā durgatyacanāvalambayoṣama: punaranutpāde yathaṃ puna: śarīrādutpādo śoka: ||
idaṃ tu nirudhyate |
kiṃ jñānamātranivṛtti: uta jñānakarmasamuccayādyu jñānakarmasamuccayāditi vadāma: |
nivṛtterapinilāpasya kāmyakarmeyo nivṛttasyāpi nityanaimittikakarmopakāri na nivartate tāni hyupanītaṃ brāhmaṇamānamavidhṛtya vihitāni samuburūpi brāhmaṇa eva jāteranucchedād va yathā'dhikāritve ṣaṭtriṃśatkaraṇīyāccalikamedu pratyavāyotya pratyavahūpanītena tadupakṣayād baddho na mucyate |
yathoktam |
yāni kāmyāni karmāṇi pratiṣiddhāni yānyapi |
tāni badhnantyakurvantaṃ nityanaimittikānyapi ||
iti |
vihitākaraṇaprabhāvo na vābhāvo bhāvasya heturiti|
ato nākṣād pratyavāyotpattiriti cetu na vihitākaraṇe'nyakāraṇād pratyavāyasya sambhavād abhāvasya hi svātantryeṇa hetutvaṃ neṣyate na tu bhāvopasarjanatayā na ca śarīre ṣaṇyādikāle kāryena vācā manasā vā kiñcid karoti śarīradhāraṇādīnāmapi karaṇāt |
yathoktam |kṣaṇikāṃ prāgabhāveṣu yā vihitākaraṇādiṣu |
na cānarthekaratvena vastutvādyapanīyate ||
svakāle yadvṛttiśevatankarotyanyadacetanam |
pratyayo'pyasya tenaiva nāmnaiva ca janyate ||
iti |
kṣaṇikena kevalena nāśe janyate ityarthaḥ |
pratibimbāropaṇādvā pratyavāyaḥ śarīradhāraṇādikaṃ ca na pratibimbadravyaṃ tatkartṛvediti yadi saṃśayāḥ yogyamapyasyati ko doṣa iti cet tatkāle vihitasyākaraṇakṣatistadāviśeṣeṇāt kevalasya śarīradhāraṇādeḥ karaṇe pratibimbadravmiti |
tadākarto bhavatyeva pratibimbācaraṇanimittaḥ pratyavāyaḥ |
kathocyate |
dīrghakālāvasthitatvamevāvijñātaghanāhitaviruddhabhāvanātmajñānameva rāgadveṣo mohaśca kṣutpipāsāṃ kṣamadvihitakaraṇānimittaṃ pratyavayamapi kṣamatīti cet |
tadayuktam |
yacca ityādyāḥ praśīdanti tacca tattvamahaṃ jātaḥ saṃśayaviṣayoyaṃ vyavasyanti na tvasya śaṅkantaranirdeśe sāmarthye draṣṭavyeṣu |
yadi punarātmajñānaṃ kṣamīti nirūpādi upāhutyaktakṣamokṣamadi karaṇe nirūpyās tataḥ sudūraṃ gatā jīvanmuktiḥ tattvadarśanānantarameva vikṣīṇākhilakarmeṇo dehapātāt |
kasmin vārthe paramārthe'drutanihatukhilābhikhyaupi cintyalikhitaniśāyāsamāṇena sarve jagatu praśyante'nāsthābhibhavivāḥ prārabdhaphale karmaviśeṣamūpabhuñjānāḥ kulālaśabdāpāravimo cakrabhrāntivaśu śaṅkāravaśādvanavanīmānasya dehapātamudīkṣamāṇāḥ |
tathā ca śrutiḥ jīvanmeva hi vidvān vaṃhagopāyasādhyāṃ vimucyate iti |
tathā cāhuḥ kāpilāḥ |
ṣaṣṭhyajñānādiyamādurmokṣamākāraṇāmāge |
nivṛtti saṃskāravaśāttadbhramavad vṛtapūrvakaḥ ||
iti |
dharmodīnāmākāraṇāmārge'pi tattvajñānenocchinneṣu dharmādharmakleśeṣu dharmodīnāṃ ṣaḍkārikāraṇāgrāprabhāve kṣatītyarthaḥ |
akṣabdaśrutīni karmāṇi tattvajñānādvinīyante iti cet tathāpi karmatvādārabdhaphalamevecchānaiva vināśamādhmāpnāt |
yo'pi boddhane vācya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare ityupadeśaḥ tasyāḥvyayamapi|
jñāne bati canātmakatvātkarmaiṇi na kriyante iti na punaratyamvyāghe: ḍatyadvāni karmāṇi jñānena vināśyante iti|
tathā cāgnimāntaraṅga|
nābhukta kṣīyate karma kalpakoṭiśatairapītyādi |
jñānaṃ yadi na kṣiṇoti karmāṇi janekajanmasahasrabarṣitānāṃ karmaṇāṃ kṣaya: parikṣayo mokṣāt karmakṣipraddha tadvai coditair ratnānāṃ karmabhekasmin janmani parikṣaya iti cenna kālāniyamāt |
yathaiva tāvat pratijanma karmāṇi kṣīyante tathaiva pretāt kṣīyante ca yādi tvaparikṣīṇāni tānyātmacaitanyānubhave bahiścintanā ca karmayogamevāgāt karmakṣipraddha nāśyante|
yathoktam|
kūrvannātmasvarūpajño mokṣāt karmaparikṣayam|
suptakoṭisahasreṇa kaścideko vimucyate||
iti|
tadevaṃ vihitamakurvanta: pratyavāyo'banestasya ca bandhahetutvādanyato virāmābhāvāt pratyavāyanirodhārtha muktimicchatā yogābhyāsāvirodhena niṣkāmojanādivacayākālaṃ vihitānyanuṣṭheyāni yāvadadhyātmasattvaṃ na sphuṭībhavati sphuṭīkṛtātmatattvasyāpi jīvanmuktasya tāvatkarmāṇi bhavanti yāvyānubandhatete ātmaikapraniṣṭhasya tvabhyaṇe'nauṣadhyasya parikṣīṇaprāyakarmaṇa: sāni naśyantyeva bahi: saṃvidativistārā|
pariṇatasamādhisāmarthyaviśadīkṛtasuṣuptisadṛśyāhitaparipākaprathatnasāpakṣitaviṣayāditvanunmattatanirvikalpaviṣayamātramanekāśīkṛtānta:karaṇakāraṇamātmanasvajñānadeva kevalaṃ sadānaṃ vṛjjāyate na bahi:khavedanaṃ bāhyendriyavyāpāroparamāt|
tatha ka: kaṃbhaya: bhermadhām|
tathā ca śruti: na yajetītyāhurkemavati na paśyatītyāhurekībhavatītyādi|
tadā cākṣuṣānimitta: pratibodho'pi nāsti dhanmeyamanupasthitetyādikamājñātaṃ|
brāhmaṇo'smīti pratītirahitasya karmādhikāraparikṣepāt|
yathoktam|
brāhmaṇatvānusaṃdhāno kṣayaṃ karmāṇi saṃdadurnidrati|
na cāsyoparatasamastavyāpārasya kṣādracarvaṇasyānuṣṭhāni prāṇinaṃ bibhatikabodhakaraṇādekatvamudbuddhaḥ duḥkhavijñānatvāt pratyakṣanayavabhāserapi śarīraṃ kiṃcanna citraṃ kālānuvṛttete saccharīrasthitiniyāmakatvenāpurvekākya karmaṇaśceratvasyedānaṃ |
yadvā tu prāktanaṃ kālānubandhivākyena karmaṇā śarīraṃ vyāptimatvād tāvatkālamaparidhāvatu tadā svakāryakaraṇāt karmakṣayacchede tatkāryasya śarīrasya nivṛttiriti nivṛtte: tatkāryatvaṃ tattvajñānasyāpi vinaśādātmaviśeṣyamāpadyate |
tattvajñānatvajñānasya vihitānāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ sandhyetarakṣaṇapratibandhyavyāpārādasti ghumraprakāritā śarīrādivividhasāttvānāṃ jñānatvasya sahupakārāpakārātmakatvāttattvajñānasyāhaṃkāramamakārayorapare vastupakāriprakākāriṇi ca tāgṛdvayormayāhatāionadhyāpanādānāditapoḥ kṛtacetarakṣaṇotsaṅgāt buddhigocaratveprekṣaṇe kṣamatvaśca parikṣayādihetutvākaraṇanimittasya pratyavāyasya ca vihitānuṣṭhānenaiva pratibandhāt śeṣe karmayogikasya dehasya nivṛttau kāraṇāntarābhāvādutpakṣasya dehasya punarutpattyabhāve matyāntara: svarūpeṇāvasthānam |
yathoktam |
nityanaimittikārtheṣu kurvāṇo duritakṣayam |
jñānaṃ ca vimalīkurvanmuktyarthaṃ tu prayatnavān ||
kramādvāt pūrvavijñāna: kaivalyaṃ jāyate naraḥ ||
iti |
tathāparerapyayaṃ gṛhītā mārgaḥ |
karmeṇā śatvaghuṇadvardvainenātmaviniścayaḥ |
bhavedihamunistyājñāt nayereva śaśamudrayāt ||
iti |
kiṃ punarātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ yenāvasthitamuktirucyate |
ānandātmeti kecit |
tadayuktam |
vikalpāvahatvāt |
sa kimānando mukhāvanubhūyate vā na vā |
yadi nānubhūyate sthitopyasthitātra viśiṣyate |
anupabhogyatvāt |
anubhūyate cet anubhavasya kāryatvaṃ vācyam na ca kāryakaraṇādivibhāge tadutpattikāraṇānāṃ pacyāya: |
anta:karaṇadeyoga: kāraṇamiti cenna yadṛyogyamapṛthagvṛttatva hi manasa: bahuśayatvāt tadviśalakṣaṇānubhavījanāśopagamaṃ nātmādṛpūrvakalpaṃ vartate yogakṣameṇānugrahītvātmatāmanukūlayati cet yogino'pi sarve: kṛtakṛtyādavaśyaṃ vijñā-kṣīti tattvatraye manavaḥ ko'nubhavītā|
ayaṃ manavaḥ|
acetanattvātmano mūkatvāpi pāṣāṇādiviśeṣaḥ vairapi hi na sukhāyate na duḥkhāyate mūko'pi yadi tathaiva ko'yoviśeṣaḥ|
tasmādvaktvātmanaḥ svābhāviko vikṛtiḥ ṣā yadaindriyadveṣo'haṃkṛcchayate tadā bahirmukhobhavati yadā tvindriyāpuyaparatāni bhavanti tadā svātmanyevānandavyabhāve nimajjati|
ayaṃ hi cittarātmā yadi ya kaścidavabhāsayati yadi punarage mūkāvasthāyāmupāste tarhi sthito'pyasthita yaṣa varamātmā jaḍa yava kṛtyatāmiti cet|
ayocyate|
kiṃ cittarānandātmanā svābhāviko kāraṇāntarajanyā vā na tāvadavabhāsakāraṇaṃ mūkatāvaitti kārayakaraṇādīnāṃ tatkāraṇānāṃ vikalpyādityuktam|
svābhāviko cetu saṃvārāvasthāyāmapyānando'nubhūyeta viniveśyo'smayoge'pi ṣamabhavāt|
acittyāṇanibandhādanubhava iti cetu na nityāyāścitterānandānubhavasyobhāvāt|
svarūpasyāpacyuteḥ|
kaḥ pratibandhā'thaḥ pracyuto vā svarūpasya kā nityatā tasmādvicitya ānando nityayā cityā cetayamāno dṛgrotayavasthayoraviśeṣeṇa cetyate|
na caivamasti saṃvārāvasthāyāmutpatrāvavamiṇo viṣayendriyādhīnajñānasya sukhasyānubhavāt ato nātyātmano nityaṃ sukhamu tadabhāvāca tadanubhavo mokṣāvasthā kiṃ tu samastātmaviśeṣaguṇocchede'pyavidyā svarūpasthitisva|
yayā cāyaṃ puruṣā'rthestathopapāditam||
śabdo'kṣaraguṇāḥ śrotragrāhyaḥ kṣaṇikaḥ kāryakāraṇobhayavirodhī saṃyogavibhāgaśabdajaḥ pradeśavṛttiḥ samānāsamānajātīyakāraṇaḥ|
sa dvividho varṇolakṣaṇo dhvanilakṣaṇaśca|
tatra akārādivarṇolakṣaṇaḥ ghañurādinimitto dhvanilakṣaṇaśca|
tatra varṇolakṣaṇasyotpattirātmanamanasoḥ saṃyogāt spṛtyepekṣābuddhiprayatnecchā tadanantaraṃ prayatnamapekṣamāṇādātmavāyusaṃyogādvāyo karma jāyate sa ghoṣṭhaṃ gacchat kāṇṭhādīnanabhighṛntitataḥ sthānavāyusaṃyogāpekṣamāṣāt sthānākāśasaṃyogāt varṇotpattiḥ|
śravaṇendriyasaṃyoge'pi bhedodaṇḍasaṃyogāpekṣādvāyorākāśasaṃyogādutpadyate|
vaiśeṣapadavibhāgādvai traidhākāśavibhāgācca śabdācca saṃyogavibhāganiṣpannādvāyoravacchedastantāna ityevaṃ santānena śrotrapradeśamāgatasya grahaṇam śrotraśabdayormithāgasanāmāvādagrāptasya grahaṇaṃ nāsti pariśeṣāt santānasiddhiriti||
iti praśastapādabhāṣye guṇapadārthaḥ samāptaḥ||
śabdo'pyasyaguṇaḥ ākāśaguṇaḥ|
nanvekadravye'pyākāśaguṇāḥ ṣanni  kathamidaṃ śabdasya lakṣaṇaṃ vyāghata āha|
śrotragrāhya iti|
śrotragrāhyatve satyākāśaguṇo yaḥ sa śabda ityarthaḥ|
parasya dviguṇanipātanirākaraṇārthamāha|
vyañjaka iti|
ākṛtiravināśī śabdo na tu nitya ityācāryamate'numānamāha|
yaddravyaṃ pramāṇābhāvena vyajjakatvakalpanānavakāśāt pratyabhijñānasya vyāghātādityāsāmānyaviṣayatvenāpavargasyottaramantrādivedasya ca vyaktibhedaśaṅkatvāt|
kāryakāraṇamedavirodhī|
ācaḥ śabdaḥ svakāraṇe vinaśyate anyaḥ svakāraṇenotpannaśabdena vinaśyate anyasya vināśakāraṇavyāptāvapyadhamarṇinastamayadā vinaśyante|
saṃyogādvibhāgaśabdavat|
ācaḥ śabdaḥ saṃyogādvibhāgācca jāyate tatpaścekastu śabdāditi vivekaḥ|
pratyo'ghvaniḥ adhyāpyaghvanirityarthaḥ|
etaccopapāditam|
samrājādhyāpañjātīyo'yam|
śabdatvaḥ śabdaḥ samānajātīyakāraṇaḥ|
saṃyogājjavibhāgācca asamānajātīyakāraṇaḥ|
sa dvividho varṇolakṣaṇaḥ sphoṭalakṣaṇaśca|
akārādivarṇalakṣaṇaḥ śabdādinimitto sphoṭalakṣaṇaḥ|
tatra tayormadhye varṇolakṣaṇasyotpattimucyate|
ātmasamavetaḥ saṃyogātapūrvonumeyavaśīkṛtyaprabhedānāṃ tattvanuśabdabodhāṅgārāṃ kartavyaṃ icchā bhavati tataḥ prayatnaṃ sa prayatna nimittakāraṇamuttamāṣāṇātsadāyusaṃyogādutpattivyādhikāraṇāt koṣṭhavāyoḥ karma jāyate da ca vāyukumbhe pacchadanu kaṇṭhādaunirgacchanti hṛtkaṇṭhatāluvyādīn pradeśānabhihanti tato'bhighātānantaraṃ sthānasya kaṇṭhādeḥ koṣṭhavāyunā vaha yaḥ saṃyogaḥ tatnimittakāraṇamuttamuttamāṣāṇāt sthānākāśasaṃyogādutpattivyādhikāraṇādvarṇotpattiḥ |
avayavailakṣaṇyo'pi bhedodaṇḍasaṃyogādudaṇḍajate vegabhedamāṣāṇādvegākāśasaṃyogādupajāyate bhedokāśavayogo'utpattivyādhikāraṇam bhedodaṇḍasaṃyogo dravyajanatva vego nimittakāraṇam |
vegupadevibhāgādutpadyakāśavibhāgātra śabdo jāyate |
śabdācca śabdaniṣpattiṃ krayati |
śabdātu saṃyogavibhāganiṣpattāvadrīvīdhvanānamacchancchannānāṃ yathā jalaghoṣyā tadvyavahite deśe vīcyantaramupajāyate tato'pyanyat tato'pyanyadityanena krameṇa vīcīdhvanānāṃ bhavati tathā śabdādutpannāt tadvyavahite deśe śabdāntaraṃ tato'pyanyonyamanāgamanāmābhāvu prāpsyovāpalabdhiriti tato'pyanyat tato'pyanyadityanena krameṇa śabdaghvanānāṃ bhavati |
śabdaṃ ghanānena śrotradeśe upāgatasyāntyaśabdasya grahaṇam |
nanveṣā kalpanā kutaḥ vidyudālīyata āha |
śrotraśabdayoriti |
na śrotra śabdadeśamupagacchati nāpi śabdaḥ śrotradeśaṃ tathotpattikramasyāt aprāpsya grahaṇaṃ nāstītyādiṣaṇāṃ prāpyakāritvāt |
prakārāntareṇa ghoṣanabdhine ghaṭate dṛṣṭā ca ghoṣadhvanāne svotpattideśe vinaśyatāmapi khvāptaprātisamavedaya tadvyavahite deśe vaduśabdayopārambhaparamparayā deśāntaraprāptiḥ tena śabdaghvanānāḥ kalpyate |
na cānavasthā yāvaddūraṃ nimittakāraṇabhūtaḥ koṣṭhavāyuranuvartate tāvaddūraṃ śabdaghvanānānuvṛttiḥ |
ata eva pratiśabdaṃ śabdānupalambhaḥ koṣṭhavāyupratīghātābhu |
ato'vaśyaṃ sāgaradhvānādeḥ ghuṇāstenātyantamiha bhāṣyatātparyamāvaṃ vyākhyātaṃ nāparā yuktiruktā ||
guṇopabṛṃhitādvaitānāṃ yuktiyuktiprabhāvinaḥ |
munīhāra iva svaccho hṛdi cintyastvasāyukham ||
iti bhaṭṭaśrībhodhavakṛtāyāṃ padārthapraveśanyāyakandalīṭīkāyāṃ guṇapadārthaḥ samāptaḥ ||atha karmapadārthanirūpaṇam |
utkṣepaṇādīnāṃ pañcānāmapi karmetvasambandhaḥ |
ekadravyavṛttitvaṃ bāhyakatvaṃ prūtaiṃdravyavṛttitvamaṃ aṇuvācatvaṃ gurutvadravatvaprayatnasaṃyogajatvaṃ svakāryasaṃyogavirodhitvaṃ saṃyogavibhāganirapekṣakāraṇatvam asamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ svaparamaparasamavetakāryāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ samānajātīyāsamārambhakatvaṃ dravyānārambhakatvaṃ ca pratiniyatajātīyogitvam |
dvividhaguṇāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ ca viśeṣaḥ ||
jñāta'nubandhāya saṃśāro'bhidhīyate |
namo jñānamṛtasyandicandrāyārthendumaulaye ||
utkṣepaṇādīnāṃ ca parasparaṃ sādhamryamitarapadārthebhyaśca pratipādayannāha |
utkṣepaṇādīnāmiti |
karmatvaṃ nāma sāmānyam tena bahu sambandha utkṣepaṇādīnāmeva |
ekadravyavṛttatvamu ekadā ekasmin dravye ekameva karma vartate ekaṃ karma ekameva dravye vartate ityekadravyavṛttatvam |
yadaikasmin dravye guṇāddrahudravyāyakarmesamavāyaḥ syāt tadā tayoḥ parasparamapratibandhādadviśeṣasaṃyogavibhāgānutpattau saṃyogavibhāgayorapekṣakāraṇaṃ karmatilakṣaṇāhāniḥ syāt |
athāpi dvayordravyayorekameva tadekasmādeva taddravyasaṃyogavibhāgayorutpatteḥ dvitīyakalpanāvaiyarthyam |
evamekaṃ karma nānekatra vartate ekasya utpattau tatsthāt karmaṇo'pyanyatra calanānupalambhāt |
bāhyakatvamāpūrtaravināśitvam |
pūrtaiṃdravyavṛttitvamu abaddhimatparimāṇadravyavṛttitvam |
aṇumātreṣu guṇavattvarahitatvam |
gurutvadravatvaprayatnasaṃyogajatvam |
svakāryati |
svakāryeṇa saṃyogena vināśyatvam na tu vibhāgavināśyatvasutarasaṃyogamātravaśyam |
saṃyogavibhāgeti |
yathā vaigātkarme nodvāmidhātatiśeṣaṇatvaṃ naiva saṃyogavibhāgātkarme nodvānādayupacatvamityartheḥ |
asamavāyikāraṇatvam yathā guṇānāṃ nimittakāraṇātmakamapi naiva karmaṇām kiṃ tvasamavāyikāraṇa-karmayogya ukteṣaṇāṇernabhāṣyamupaniṣatprabhṛ|
svamevanayoḥ|
svarūpayoriti|
svāpye prasānne ca vyākhyeya dharmayorete yatkāye saṃyogavibhāgalakṣaṇe tadātmakamekatvam|
vyatireki|
kramataḥ karmantarārāmbhe baddhato gativināśau ca vyāptu|
śabdādyākāśādibhirāmādānte yathoktadiśām  iti cet nahīcchāmayatvādikamevaitatsaṃyuktasyaikasyāpi kāryasya ca na tu krame viśeṣādhyāsitam karma karmāntaraṃ ca pravṛtti krametyanvād yatyakṣamevetat|
athavā viśeṣādyākhyātaṃ karma karmaśārthaṃ ca pravṛtti pareṇāvādyasyamevetat|
dṛśyaiti|
uttarayogānuvṛtte karmaṇi ityapyotpādanam|
pratiniyateti|
ukteṣaṇādiṣu pratyekamukteṣaṇāvyatirekāṇa ityarthaḥ|
dhanañjayenaitad yajuṣāṃ vyākhyeyam|
dvividhañjuṣṭi|
dvividhañjuṣṭākāryakartṛtvapekṣa kāyamiti|
tatheti||
tatrokteṣaṇāṃ śarīrāvayaveṣu tatsadṛkṣeṣu ca yaduṣvarmātibhiḥ pradeśaiḥ saṃyogakāraṇamayobhāgibhistat pradeśaiḥ vibhāgakāraṇaṃ kramītyucyate yugasvapracayasaṃyogobhayastadukteṣaṇam||
śarīrāvayaveṣu indrādiṣu tatsadṛkṣeṣu puṣpādiṣu ca yadūṣvarmātibhiḥ pradeśaiḥ saṃyogakāraṇamayobhāgibhiśca vibhāgakāraṇaṃ yugasvasaṃyogapramāṇenaiva krāyate tadukteṣaṇam||
tadviparīta saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇaṃ karmopaceyaṇam||
tadviparīteti|
ayodravyasaṃyogakāraṇamṛṣvarevavibhāgakāraṇaṃ karmopaceyaṇamityarthaḥ||
kṣaṇunau dṛśyasyādhārayavānau taddhetauvibhāgaḥ saṃyogasya mūlapradeśordhvena karmaṇādhārayato kuṭilaḥ saṃjāyate tadāpakṣunam||khracuna iti |
taddvaireyāṣayasabdadrudrākṣāṣādidravyaiḥ sajjāyate iti yena karmaṇeti sambandhaḥ ||
taddviparyayeṇa saṃyogavibhāgotpattau yena karmaṇā-dhayavī kṛtaḥ sañcayate tatprasāraṇam ||
atha cayanānāṃ mūlapradeśavibhāgānusareṇasaṃyogotpattau satyāmityarthaḥ ||
yadaniyatadikpradeśasaṃyogavibhāgakāraṇaṃ tadvṛ-kṣanamiti ||
etatsaṅgrahavidhamapi karma dharīrāvayaveṣu tatsamba-ddheṣu ca sadṛśyasamavetatvatpayaṃ ca yadanyattadvṛttyameva teṣvanyeṣu ca tadvṛkṣanamiti |
karmaṇāṃ jātipañcakatvama-nuktaṃ gamanādiśeṣāt |
sarve hi draviṇike karme gamanamātramutpacya svāśrayasyordhvamadhastiryagvāy-śumārgaiḥ pradeśaiḥ saṃyogavibhāgān karoti sarvatra gamana pratyayo'viśiṣṭastasmādvṛkṣanameva sarvemiti |
na vargādyāḥ pratyayānuvṛttivyāhṛtidarśanāt ihotkṣepaṇaṃ patanāpatanaśabdityevamādi sarvatra vargādyāḥ pratyayā-nuvṛttivyāhṛtī dṛṣṭe taddvetuḥ sāmānyaviśeṣabhedo-'vagamyate |
teṣāmudānudānasargaviśeṣāt pratiniyatadi-gviśiṣṭaprakāryotpattvādupalabdhabhede'pi siddhuḥ |
sarvamapi pañcaivetyavadhāraṇānupapattiḥ |
niṣkarmaṇaprade-śānādiṣvapi vargādyāḥ pratyayānuvṛttivyāhṛtidarśa-nāt |
praculotkṣepaṇādiṣu sarvatra vargādyāḥ pratyayānuvṛ-tiṣṭhādyutidarśanājjātibheda iṣyate sarva ca niṣkāmagrāmaveśanādiṣvapi|
kāryabhedāttu teṣu pratyayānuvṛttivyāhatī iti cet na utkṣepaṇādiṣvapi kāryabhedādeva pratyayānuvṛttivyāhatiprasaṅgaḥ|
atha samāne varge'ḥ pratyayānuvṛttivyāghātisaṅgāve utkṣepaṇādīnāmeva jātibhedo na niṣkāsaṇādīnāmityatra viśeṣaheturaktīti cet jātisaṅkaraprasaṅgāt|
niṣkāsaṇādīnāṃ jātibhedāt pratyayānuvṛttivyāhato jātisaṅkaraḥ prasajyate|
katham dravyauddeśyenotkṣepaṇādupavarakādupavarakāntaraṃ gacchato yugapattiṣṭhanasagrāmaveśanapratyayo dṛśyau tathā dvārapraveśo pratiṣati niṣkāmatīti ca|
yadā tu pratibīrācayanītaṃ bhavati tadā na praveśanapratyayo nāpi niṣkramaṇapratyayaḥ kintu gamanapratyaya eva bhavati|
tathā nālikāyāṃ vaṃśapañjādvau patati bahūnāṃ dravdūṇāṃ yugapadbhramaṇapatanapraveśanapratyayo dṛśya iti jātisaṅkaraprasaṅgaḥ|
na caivamutkṣepaṇādiṣu pratyayasaṅkaro dṛṣṭaḥ tasmādutkṣepaṇādīnāmeva jātibhedāt pratyayānuvṛttivyāhato niṣkāmaṇādīnāṃ tu kāryabhedāditi|
kathaṃ yugapatpratyayabheda iti cet|
atha matam yathā jātisaṅkaro nāsti evamanekasamavetamāveśo'pi nāstītyekasmin karmaṇi yugapadūhu dvayāṃ bhramaṇāpatanapraveśanapratyayāḥ kathaṃ bhavantīti|
atra brūmaḥ na avayavāvayavinorvivarto vividhaprayoga-vibhāgānāṃ bhedāt‌ |
yo hi draśya dravayavānāṃ pāśvaitaḥ paryāyeṇa vikramadeśaiḥ saṃyogavibhāgān‌ paśyati tasya bhavanāpratyayo bhavati ye|
dravayavit‌ ūrdhvamadheṣorvibhāga mayaḥ saṃyogaṃ cāvekṣate tasya patanapratyayo bhavati |
yaḥ punarnolikā tvaddeśe saṃyogaṃ bahirdeśe ca vibhāgaṃ paśyati tasya praveśanapratyayo bhavatīti siddhaḥ kāryedavānikṣakaṇādīnāṃ pratyayabheda iti |
bhavatuccheṣaṇādīnāṃ jātibhedāt‌ pratyayabhedaḥ nikṣakaṇādīnāṃ tu kāryabhedāditi ||
vatpratyayamiti |
pratyakṣavekṣāparatyakṣavekṣe ca bhavatītyarthaḥ |
yaddṛśyaditi |
pratanu śarīrādyaveva sukṣmādiṣvapyeṣa vā|
dravyeṣu yat‌ tadgatyātmakaṃ kāryaṃ jāyate vatpratyayādyat‌ tadanumeyam‌|
ceṣṭayati|
karmeṇāmiti|
utkṣepaṇādīnāṃ karmaṇāṃ jātiṣu yuktatvamuktam‌ gamanāt‌ sarveṣāmaviśeṣādbhedāditīti cedanāryaṃ |
svargo gamanādaviśeṣeṇa kathyati |
sarve hītyādinā|
utkṣepaṇādidṛṣṭe gacchati cakṣo gacchatīti pratyayadarśanāt‌ sarvaivedamutkṣepaṇādikaṃ gamanameva |
vamādayo |
naiti|
yatra tvaghordhvaṃ tatra utkṣepaṇādiṣu varṇayaḥ|
pratibadhaṃ pratyagānuguṇitvādanuguṇyedhaināt‌ todanaṃ cyavanādivarṇo'yāccyā pratyagānugamadvaiguṇyādū gotvaṃ kalpyate yathā tathotkṣepaṇādiṣu pratibadhamitatvarṇo'yāccyā pratyagānugamadvaiguṇyādutkṣepaṇatvādidhāmānyakalpanaivyaṃbhicāraḥ|
cakṣuḥ vivaraṇaṃ sugamam‌|
neṣāmiti|
upalabdhāpravedopadhautyāviśabdaḥ kāryotmādyupalabdhāt‌ paro draṣṭavyaḥ|
upalabdhate unyavilakṣaṇatayā pratipattite vyaktiratyetapupalabdhaṃ jātistadayamacāryaḥ |
na kevalamanuvṛttivyāptipratyayadvainādutkṣepaṇādupakṣepaṇādīnāṃ jātibhedaḥ siddhaḥ udāharaṇabobhedāt‌ pratiniyatādiliṅgātkāryotmāderapi viduḥ |
apare tu neṣāmutkṣepaṇādīnāmudāharaṇabhedo'viśeṣāduṇaviśeṣakāryotpādupalabdhibhedo'pi pratipattibhedo'piṣaṣṭhyanto gamanivṛttyartham |
vidva iti karmabhede hi yathotpannāpūrvasaṃyogavibhāgahetutvamapavargaṇādikamapi syāt |
punarutpadyata iti |
yavamayoti |
yadi pratibaṃdhaṃ pratyayānuvṛttinityānuvṛttirūpo nāhantveṇādiṣu sāmānyamanuguṇate tadā niṣkarṣaṇādiṣvapi pratibaṃdhaṃ pratyayānuvṛttinityānuvṛttirūpaniṣkarṣaṇatvādikamanuguṇeyam tataśca evaitetyavadhāraṇānupapattiḥ |
atha niṣkarṣaṇādiṣu kāryabhedāt pratyayabhedo na jātibhedāt tadotpeṣaṇādiṣvapi tathā syādityāha |
kāryabhedāt siddhamiti |
samādhatte ceti |
yadi niṣkarṣaṇatvādijñātīya icyante tadā jātisaṅkaraprasaṅgaḥ |
yakṣayāṃ vyaktau vidvadunikaṣātisambhavāḥ praśakyate ityarthaḥ |
kāryamityanuṣaḥ padārtha |
dṛṣṭo'dṛṣṭo'tiriti |
dṛṣṭo'dṛṣṭo're karatvādapavarkādapavarkāntare |
gacchataḥ puruṣasya yo dṛṣṭo're nayorikṣayāṃ vyakto niṣkarṣaṇapraveśanamṛtyayo dṛṣṭau matopavarkāt pūrvadho nimajjati tatra sthitasya nimajjatīti pratyayaḥ yatra praviśati tatra sthitasya praviśatīti pratyayaḥ yadi jātikṛtādidho pratyayo dṛṣṭau tadekṣayāṃ vyaktau parasparaviruddhaniṣkarṣaṇapraveśanatvajātidvayasamāveśo durghaṭaḥ syāt |
yathā dravyadeśe praviśati niṣkāmatīti yathekasminneva bahuvrīhau mukhe prakoṣṭhāt prakoṣṭhāntaraṃ gacchati puruṣe pūrvoparakoṣṭhasthitayordṛṣṭyormadhye nimajjati praviśatīti pratyayo bhavataḥ |
yadā tu pratibaṃdhāpannīte madhyasthite cāpratibaddhāpanīte bhavati tadā na praveśanapratyayo nāpi niṣkarṣaṇapratyayaḥ kiṃ tu gamanamātrasya yatha bhavati tasmātkramanayaiva sarvopapattiḥ |
kṛtasya pratyayabheda ityabhiprāyaḥ |
udāharaṇāntaramāha |
tathā cālikāyāmiti |
cāliketi gartasthāniyānam |
svapakṣe viśeṣamāha |
na caivamiti |
uparyadhariti |
tasmāditi |
ekadeśasmin dravye tāvadekameva karma bhavati tatra kathaṃ yugapadanekārmakalpanam ityāha |
kathamiti |
sāṃdṛṣṭiketi |
atha matabhedamādinā |
atra brūma iti vidvāntopakramaḥ |
yataḥ tvayoktaṃ tatra traya yathānāmadheyaviśeṣaṃ dvideśāvasthitānāṃ saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ bhedāt |
atha sugame vivaraṇam |
athayayakṣameṣu pāśveṣu saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇeṣu bhramaṇapratyayaḥ traya yatkriyāyāṃ kāryabhedāt patanapraveśanapratyayāvityarthaḥ ||
bhavatustvevamadoṣāṃ jātibhedāt pratyayabhedaḥ ||atha gamanatvaṃ kiṃ karmatvaparyoyaḥ āhosvidaparaṃ sāmānyamiti|
kṛtaste saṃśayaḥ|
samasteṣvutkṣepaṇādiṣu karmapratyayavadgāmanapratyayāviśeṣāt karmatvaparyāya iti gamyate|
yatastūtkṣepaṇādivadviśeṣasaṃjñayābhihitaṃ tasmādaparaṃ sāmānyaṃ syāditi|
na|
karmatvaparyāyatvāt|
ātmatvapuruṣatvavatkarmatvaparyāya eva gamanatvamiti|
atha viśeṣasaṃjñayā kimarthaṃ gamanagrahaṇaṃ kṛtamiti na bhramaṇādāvabarodhārthatvāt|
utkṣepaṇādiśabdairanavastutānāṃ bhramaṇāpatanaspandanādīnāmavarodhārthaṃ gamanagrahaṇaṃ kṛtamiti|
anyathā hi yānyeva catvāri viśeṣasaṃjñayoktāni tānyeva sāmānyaviśeṣasaṃjñāviṣyantīti prasajyeranniti|
athavā astvaparaṃ sāmānyaṃ gamanatvamaniyata digdeśayogavibhāgākāreṇaiṣu bhramaṇādiṣveva vartate gamanaśabdastūtkṣepaṇādiṣu bhākto draṣṭavyaḥ svāśrayasaṃyogavibhāgakartṛtvasāmānyāditi||
atha gamanatvaṃ kiṃ karmatvaparyoyaḥ|
āhosvidaparaṃ sāmānyamiti vidvānto pṛcchati|
kṛtaste saṃśayaḥ saṃśayo'nupapanna ityarthamāyaḥ|
paraḥ saṃśayamupapādayati|
samasteṣviti|
utkṣepaṇādiṣu caturṣu yathā karmapratyayaḥ khalanātmakastathā teṣu gamanapratyayaḥ śaṅke gacchatyayo gacchati mṛṣūddeśe gacchatyadeśe gacchatīti pratyayo bhavatīti tena gamanatvaṃ karmatvaparyāya iti gamyate samasteṣvityanupakramāt|
yatastūtkṣepaṇādivadgamanamapi pṛthagabhihitaṃ viśeṣasaṃjñayā tasmādu gamanatvamaparaṃ sāmānyaṃ syāt|
athāntaravedanikāmkarmaṇyacve khatsādhakamarmānahupuṣām |
ṇāvare tasya saṃkīrtanāt |
yathamuṣaṇādite pareṇa saṃghaye iti sūtiḥ prāha |
neti |
na kartavyaḥ saṃśayaḥ kṛto gamanatvasya karmatvaparyāyatvāt |
yatodruṣṭaśātiḥ |
ātmatvapuruṣatvasatkarmatvaparyāya eva gamanatvamiti |
yathātmatvasya paryāyaḥ puruṣatvaṃ samastavedyavyāpakatvāt tathā gamanatvaṃ karmatvasya paryāyaḥ |
atha viśeṣe viśeṣasañjñā pṛthaggamanaśabdaṃ kṛtamiti vodvyati |
kṛṣṭiti |
uttaramāha |
neti |
utprekṣaṇādiśabderanavacchudya na saṃgṛhītāḥ śramaṇādayo yadi gamanaprakṛtaṃ na kriyeta tadā teṣāṃ karmatvena saṃgraho na syāt |
kiṃ tu viśeṣasañjñoddeśānāmūtprekṣaṇādīnāmeva paraṃ karmatvasaṅkāviṣayatvaṃ bhavet |
śramaṇādayo'pi ca karmatvena lokaprasiddhāḥ atasteṣāṃ parigrahārthaṃ pṛthaggamanaśabdaṃ kṛtamiti yanyāthaiḥ |
athavā astvaparaṃ kāmānyaṃ gamanatvaṃ tatkṛtaṃ bhanate tatrāha |
aniyateti |
kṛtatvādetutprekṣaṇādiṣu gamanapratyayaḥ ata āha |
gamanaśabdastviti |
gamanaśabdayaheṇayogalakṣaṇārthatvāt gamanapratyaya utprekṣaṇādiṣu bhāto draṣṭavyaḥ |
upacārasya prayojanamāha |
svāśrayasaṃyogavibhāgakartṛtvakāmānyāditi |
gamanaṃ svāśrayasya saṃyogavibhāgaṃ karoti utprekṣaṇādayo'pi kurvanti yatābatā kāmanyayogatprekṣaṇādiṣu gamanavyavahāraḥ anena kāmanyeṇa gamane kasyānutpekṣaṇādivyavahāro na bhavati paiṅgatyapāṭatvādidaśāyāṃ tu bahuvidho māṣaṇakavyavahāraḥ kṛtyāt bhavati |
abocyate |
na kāraṇamadvāve kāryapucārakalpanā kintu sthite vyavahāre kāraṇakalpaneti |
evaṃ vedanāpi sa yatha parihāraḥ |
satsatyaprakamavidhiḥ |
kathamu svidātīṣiteṣu yajñādhyayanadānakuṅkhyādiṣu yadā hastanuttaurmubhicchatyapanetuṃ vā tadā hastavatsātmapradeśo prayataḥ saṃjñāyate taṃ prayatnaṃ śuktvaṃ cāpekṣamāṇādātmahastasaṃyogā haste karma bhavati hastavat sarvadhāritābayaveṣu pādādiṣu śarīre veti |
tatsambaddheṣvapi kathamu yadā hastena mudgalaṃ gṛhītveccho karoti utkṣipāmi hastena mudgalamititadanantaraṃ prayatnasapekṣamāṇādātmahastasaṃyogāccalitraiva kāle haste utkṣepaṇakarmotpadyate tasminnaiva kāle  tveva prayatnasapekṣamāṇāduttamukhalasaṃyogāt subale  api karmoti |
tato durasutpanne subale tadarthecchā  nivartate punarāpyapadeśaparokṣotpadyate tadanantaraṃ prayatnatsa mapekṣamāṇācayoktāt saṃyogāduttamukhalayoryugapadupa kṣepaṇakarmaṇī bhavata: tato'nyena subalakarmaṇo'tyabala subalayorabhighātākhya: saṃyoga: kriyate sa saṃyogo'nuba lagatavegamapekṣamāṇo'pratyayaṃ subale utpatanakarma karo ti |
tatkarmābhighātāpekṣaṃ subale saṃskāramārabhate tasmaye ccha subalahastasaṃyogo'pratyayaṃ haste'pyutpatanakarma karo ti|
yadyapi prāktana: saṃskāri vinaṣṭa: tathāpi subalotpu tvalayo: saṃyoga: padukarmotpādaka: saṃyogaviśeṣabhāvāt  tasya saṃskārārambho sācivyasamartho bhavati |
athavā  prāktana eva padu: saṃskāro'bhighātādvinaśyatyanava sthita iti |
ata: saṃskāravati puna: saṃskārārambho  nāstyato yasmin kāle saṃskārāpekṣādabhighātādya tyayaṃ subale utpatanakarma tasminnaiva kāle tameva saṃ skāramapekṣamāṇāt subalahastasaṃyogādapratyayaṃ haste  'pyutpatanakarmoti ||kāryamadhye ghanatvayaktamanicchupuḥ |
hastamudyamyamicchanti tadā hastalaghutvātmapradeśe prayatno jāyate taṃ prayatnaṃ nimittakāraṇamupādānamāṇādātmahastasaṃyogādasamavāyikāraṇādudravye kāryaṃ bhavati gha hastādi prayatne gurutvabahigatatva utkṣepaṇāpakṣepaṇayorapakṣakṣaṇānyanādṛguṇatvasyāpi kā raṇatvam |
hastalaghutvaivadhārītakriyāvege pādyādiṣu gatīre veti |
pādai karmotpattau pāda śaktyātmapradeśe prayatno nimittakāraṇam pādātmasaṃyogo'samavāyikāraṇam |
pūrvaṃ  kharveva śarīralaghuprayatniyatnau dṛṣṭavyau |
śarīrakriyotpattāvapi śarīrātmasaṃyo go'samavāyikāraṇam śarīradātmapradeśe prayatno nimittakāraṇam |
tatsvabhāvadṛṣṭe  śarīrakhandṛṣṭe śarīralaghuprayatnandṛṣṭeṣvapi kāryaṃ karmotpattiriti praṣṭavyaḥ |
yadā  hastena subalaṃ gṛhītvocchāṃ karoti utkṣipāmi hastena subalaṃ'miti |
tadyannataraṃ  lakṣyā icchāyā janantaraṃ prayatno hastena subalamuccheṣutvāpāmiti hastasubalayo rmadhyagurutveṇecchātaḥ prayatno jāyamānaḥ tayormadhyagurutveṇasamavāyo viśiṣṭa eva  jāyate taṃ prayatnaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ nimittamupādānamāṇādātmahastasaṃyogādasamavāyikāraṇā dyātmaneva kāle haste utkṣepaṇakarmotpadyate tasminneva kāle tadeva prayatnasuvalayo rgurutvaprayatnamāṇāduttamasubalasaṃyogādasamavāyikāraṇāt subale'pi karma bhavati  kāraṇayogapadyāt tato dūrabhūtvam subale taducchā nivartate utkṣepaṇecchā niva rtate punaraprayatneṇecchātyavate hastena subalatyāpakṣepaṇecchāpajāyate ityarthaḥ |
tadyannataraṃ prayatna yo'pi jāyamānaḥ utkṣeṇaprayatnavat viśiṣṭa eva jāyate taṃ ca  prayatnamupādānamāṇādyotkāt saṃyogādudyādātmahastasaṃyogāduttamasubalasaṃyogādutta mubalayorgurutvaprayatneṇakarmaṇo bhavataḥ tato'nyena subalakarmaṇā utkabalasubalayo rmithyātmakaḥ saṃyogaḥ kriyate |
apakṣipya subalatyāntaścena karmaṇā utkabalasu balasambandhau subalatyotpannahetuḥ saṃyogaḥ kriyata ityarthaḥ |
sa saṃyogo subalagataṃ  vegamavegamāṇau pratyayamaprayatnapūrvake subale unyatakarma karoti vego nimittakāraṇaṃ  subalaṃ samavāyikāraṇam |
tatkarmotpadyātāparyaṃ subale saṃskārarambhate utpatana karmaṃ tvakāraṇamabhidyātmakaṃ saṃyogamupādānamāṇaṃ subale vegamārabhate |
taṃ saṃskāramupā daya ittasubalasaṃyogo'samavāyikāraṇameto'pratyayamaprayatnapūrvakaṃ hastepyupatana karmaṃ karoti yo'yaṃ prākteno'pyeṣaṇaparamparā prayuñjagataḥ yo'pyabhidhyātatādiguṇaḥ  tadabhāve kāryaṃ subale'pratyayamapyatanakarmotpatanavegakāramārabhate cāpekṣākāraṇā bhāvādana kāhaṃ |
yadyapi prāktanaḥ saṃskāro vinaṣṭaḥ tathāpi subalotkabalasaṃyogaḥṣaṭkarmopadeśamādhye  padmanābho'vadat: ṣaṭkārajanakarmopyavadat: krataḥ saṃyogaviśeṣamātrāt kuṃyo: gaviśeṣatvādu kriyato yaccaiva tatkāha |
tasya krameṇa: ṣaṭkārātmano kartavya vāvidyāsamadhau bhavati |
śāhāyye samadhau bhavati |
cālaneṣu pake hastamukhalayoścālananakrameṇau krameṇa bhavataḥ |
āśubhāvāttu yogapadyamahaṇāt |
prakārāntaramāha |
athavā prāktana eva paṭhaḥ saṃskārābhighātādaviṣayatvacitsthita iti viśiṣṭakāyajanyādiniyamaḥ: ṣaṃskāraḥ svadhaivṛddhamavya saṃyogenāpi na vinaśyati ataḥ saṃskārati saṃskārāntarasmadhau nāsti yataḥ prāktanāpekṣāṇaṃsaṃskāro na jighraḥ ataḥ prāktanasaṃskāravati sukhale saṃskārāntārasmadhau nāsti iti pratīyate |
yasmin kāle saṃskārāpaghātamighātādāpratyayaṃ sukhale utpattanakrame tasminneva kāle tameva saṃskārapreṣamāṇādattanusukhasaṃyogapadyapratyayaṃ haste'pyutpattanakrameti |
cālaneṣu pake hastasukhaleyorutpattanakrameṇauvekṣatvameva yogapadyam |
pāṇisukhaleṣu gamanavidhiḥ |
