kaṃcanu yadā tomaraṃ hastena gṛhītvotkṣiptumicchettyajate tadanantaraṃ prathamaḥ tatpayekṣamāṇātthasyotkṣāt saṃyoganuddhyāt tomarahastayoryugapadākṣepaṇakarmaṇau bhavataḥ |
prakṣārite ca haste tadākarṣaṇāthaiḥ prathamo nivartate tadanantaraṃ tiryagūrdhvaṃ dūramārvaṃ vā kṣipāmītīcchā sajjāyate tadanantaraṃ tadyuktaḥ prayattastatpayekṣamāṇāstomarahastasaṃyogā nodanākhyāḥ tasmāt tomare karmotpannaṃ nodanāpekṣaṃ tasmin saṃskāramātrasave tataḥ saṃskāranodanārthau tādṛśu karmāṇi bhavanti yāvaduktatomaravibhāga iti tato vibhāgānodane nivṛtte saṃskārādūrdhvaṃ tiryagadūramārvaṃ vā prayatnānukūlapāṇi karmāṇi bhavantyāpatanāditi ||pāṇimuktau gamanavidhiḥ|
kathaṃ pāṇimuktau dravyasya gamanavidhiḥ|
gamanasaṃ kāraḥ kathamanuvartate iti praśne kṛte brūmaḥāhu|
yadvā tomarādibhiḥ|
sūtrapadākṣepe ṇeti|
karmakaraṇotve'pyupasthitatvāt|
prathamo nivartate iti|
tayorekataro satyorākṣepaprayojanaprathamo nivartate nādviriti prathamārādhyaprathamotpādādivacaneḥ|
ta danantaramiti|
pravāraṇānantaraṃ niṣeṇuprathame vā drutamāghaṭṭe vā kṣipāmītīcchātmakena tadanantaraṃ tadanurūpaḥ prathamaḥ tiryakūrdhvamadhaścādhāṃ tiryakūrdhvamadhaḥprathamo jñāyate iti jñāte'pyakādhāṃsuṣṭhe'pyadhaḥprathamo jñāyate dūradeśe 'dhvānaṃ maṅktvā prathamaḥ ākarṣaṇe'pyākāśe ca vidhivat|
prathamo jñāyate iti tadanurūpaśabdāthaiḥ|
tatra ṇeṣamāṇastomarahastasaṃyogo nodanākhyo nākhyasya tomarasya nodakatva ca hastasya khar̤āyamane hetutvāt tasyānodanākhyatvayoktādicchānurūpaprathamādi bhāve tomare kramotpattau tatkarme nodanāpekṣaṃ sadvittu tomare saṃskāramārabhate|
nataḥ khaḍgā reti|
tomarasya etatvaṃ yāhamu khaḍgāranurūpāṇi kramāṇi bhavantītyarthaḥ||
tathā yantramukteṣu gamanavidhiḥ|(1)|
kṣayadyu yo vajra yānū kṣatavyāyāmau kṣāmyena kṣureṇa(2) dhanuvidyusya dakṣiṇena yārye caryāya saṃghaṭarāṃ jyāṃ sṛuṣṭenā yṛhītvā ākarṣaṇecchāṃ karīrāti vajjyeṣṭhākarṣaṇāstrītahanuriti|
tadanantaraṃ prathamastanapekṣamāṇādātmāhustasaṃyogādākra ṣeṇākarme haste yadevotpadyate tadaiva tameva prathamasta pekṣamāṇādāhustakṣepāṇaparasaṃyogād jyāyāṃ yāre ca karme prathamavidhiṣṭahastajyākṣarasaṃyogasapekṣamāṇāsaryāṃ jyā koṭisaṃyogāsyāṃ karmaṇo bhavato dhanuśkojyorityetat sarvaṃ yugapat|
evamākarṣaṇādākṣepe dhanuṣi nātaḥ pura tatena(3) gantavyamiti yajjñānaṃ tatpūrvadākṣepeṇārthe sya prathamasya vināśastataḥ punarnodbodhyā sṛjjā yate tadanantaraṃ prathamastanapekṣamāṇādātmāhutpikṣayogāṅkulitvapuraṃ hastātsvāṅguṣṭhāgrairniṣīdatibhāga:|
tato vibhāgāt sarveṣāmaṅguṣṭhāgrā: sthiteṣu vinyaste pratibandhakāmābādhvadrā yanubi darśalāñ: sthitisthāpaka: saṃskāro maṇḍalībhūtaṃ dhanuryethāvasthitaṃ sthāpayati tadā tameva saṃskāramapekṣamāṇā hanuyuktāṅguyogājjyāyāṃ śare ca kramotsṛjyate tatsaṃskāraṇāpūrvaṃ jyāyāṃ saṃskāraṃ karoti tatpaścātmāṇe iṣujyādiyogo nodanaṃ tasmādviṣāvāṇaṃ karma lohanāpekṣākṣipro saṃskāramātmate|
tasmāt saṃskārātroḍhanasahāyāt tābat karmāṇi bhavanti yāvadiṣujyādisāmagrī tribhāgānvicute nodane karmaṇyuttarottarāṇīṣusaṃskārā devapatanāditi|
bahūni karmāṇi kramaśa: kramāt saṃyogabahutvāt yuktastu saṃskāro'ntarāle karmayogāpekṣākāraṇābhāvāditi||
kṛtavyāghāta: kṛtānugrāhyādau śamena kareṇa prajanitrīcu gādaṃ gṛhītvā dakṣiṇena śaraṃ vacyagraṃ jyāyāṃ śaraṃ saṃyojya sadharma jyā|
śareṇa yaha vartamānāṃ jvāṃ mukttvā gṛhītvā paccāt karoti paścotkṣipteṇāntenānuvṛtti|
jyoti dhanurmukhyāsyā dhanuriti śastrābhidhānam jyā ca iṣuśca jeṣe saha jeṣujyā vartate iti saṅgrahadhanuraṭaśakriyāsamīcīnachāyā ākāro dakṣiṇa:|
tadanantaraṃ prayatnamapekṣamāṇādātmahastasaṃyogādaṅguṣṭhāṅgule haste yadaivātmaghāte tadaiva taṃ prayatnamapekṣamāṇādastajyāśarasaṃyogājjyāyāṃ śare ca karma hastaśarasaṃyogāt prayatnaviśiṣṭajyāhastasaṃyogāmapekṣamāṇābhyāṃ jyākṣeṭiyogābhyāṃ karmaṇo dhanukkodyotiti|
etatsarvaṃ yagapatkāraṇayogapradyāt|
evamākṣepādaṅkure dhanurvi nāta: śaramena hastena gṛhṇatyamiti pratyajāṃ tasmāt tadākṣepaṇeyereti dhanurākṣepaṇādhesya prayatnasya vināśa iti|
tata: śarasya guṇasya ca mokṣaṇecchā ca tadanantaraṃ prayatno mokṣaṇāye: tatpaścātmāṇādātmahastasaṃyogādaṅgu-karmendriya prātyakṣakarmanirdeśaṇam |
liṅgadehe tattvācchāyākṛtivibhāgāḥ śaraguṇākhyāśca tato vibhāgācchruṇādvisaṃyogavibhāgayatvamityanuṃ saṃyoge vinaṣṭe pratibandhakābhāvācchudā yanuṣi calemānaḥ sthitisthāpakaḥ saṃskāro bhūyaṣṭhlībhūtaṃ yanuṣayāvasthitaṃ sthāpayati sa saṃskāraprabandhamāśudurbodhayogācchāyāyā śre ca kramotpadyate tatkṣame tvaṅkāraṇāpūrvaṃ yanuṣordhayogāpūrvaṃ cchāyāṃ saṃskāraṃ vegākhyaṃ karoti taṃ ca saṃskāramapekṣamāṇā chuñjyādayogo nodanaṃ nairāśyeṣornodakasya guṇasya vahvagamanahetutvādū tasmāttu nodanodanāvācya krame saṃskāramārabhate tasmāttu saṃskārāt nodanaghaṅkāyātū sākṣāt kramoṇi bhavanti yāvadicchāvabhimāno vibhāgādivṛtte nodane kramoṇi uttarāṇi saṃskārādeva vegākhyādudbhavanti yāvatpatanamiṣṭotpatteśca ca pāto gurutvanimittañcaksaṃskārañcayātū|
atha codyati |
vahūni kramoṇi kramaśaḥ kasmāditi |
vyabhicāreṇyogottarāle kramaśo bahūni kramoṇi bhavantīti kasmāt kalpyate yakrameva karma kuto na kalpyatāmityabhiprāyaḥ |
yathāyate |
saṃyogabahutvāditi |
uttarasaṃyogāntaṃ karmetyavasthitaṃ tripratyeśottarāle bahavaḥ saṃyogā dṛśyante tena bahūni kramoṇi bhavantītyāśrīyate |
yatkṛttu ṣaṭkoṭoruttarāle karmaṇo'pyavākāraṇābhāvāt nodanāmighātayoruttararūpeṣu karma saṃskāramārabhate na karmamātraṃ vegākhyābhāvāt|
na cvāntarāle nodanaṃ nābhyamighātastatkāle ka pratha śarajyāyogāyeṣu śarakarmaṇā prathame kṛto viśiṣṭaḥ saṃskāro yāvatpatanamanuvartate yathā yathā cāsya kāryakaraṇacchaktiḥ kṣīyate tathā tathā kārye mandataratamādikrameṇvasupyajāyate yathā tarottaraṇasya phale prakṛṣyate upakṛṣyate ca jīryeccha ||
evamātmāmihiteṣu bāhyeṣvapyadhyātmyaṃ na kalpīkam|
anabhihiteṣu bāhyeṣu catuṣu jalapavanatejapratyayaṃ karma gamanameva nodanādibhyo bhavati |
tadvibhāgaḥ gurutvadravatvavegaprayatnāt kṣamastyadhyastānapekṣa nānā |
ca saṃyogaviśeṣo nādanajavibhāgahetorakṣasya karmaṇaḥ kāraṇam tasmādbahutvepi mahābhūteṣu karma bhavati |
upapaharati |
yaditi |
anabhihiteṣu bāhyeṣu catuṣu mahābhūteṣātmayaṃ gamanameva nodanādibhyo bhavati cātmanā cathāyāraṇa dharmatvintanā mi-dvitīyaṃ sāṅkhyoktamanupūrvakaṃ gamanātmakameva karma bhavati notkṣepaṇāpakṣepaṇādikamityapi: |
mahāpralaye nodanādibhya: karma bhavatītyuktam |
atha kiṃ nodanamatra āha |
tatra nodanaṃ gurutvadravatvāpekṣavegāt samastavyastātsanvāno ya: saṃyogaviśeṣa: kartuśca saṃyogaviśeṣo nodanamucyate tatrāha |
nodanamavibhāgaheto: karmaṇa: kāraṇamiti |
nodyanodakayo: parasparavibhāgaṃ na karoti pratyakṣaṃ tasya kāraṇaṃ nodanam ||
yathā paṅkasthāyāṃ pṛthivyām |
vegāpekṣo ya: saṃyogaviśeṣo vibhāgahetorekasya karmaṇa: kāraṇaṃ so'bhighāta: |
tasmādapi caturṣu mahābhūteṣu karma bhavati yathā pāṣāṇādiṣu nihure vastunyabhighātiteṣu tathā pādādibhiranucamānāyāmabhihanyamānāyāṃ vā paṅkasthāyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ ya: saṃyogo nodanamabhighātayoranyatarapekṣa ubhayāpekṣo vā sa saṃyuktasaṃyoga: tasmādapi pṛthivyādiṣu karma bhavati |
ye ca pradeśā na nudyante nāpyabhihanyante teṣvapi karma jāyate|
pṛthivīdudrayogurutvavibhāgakavayogaprayatnavegābhāve sati gurutvādvahadhogamanaṃnatadvatasm|
yathā subala śarīrādidṛktam|
tatrāpi gurutvāddhidvitīyādīni tu gurutvasaṃskārāpekṣām ||
kimuktaṃ syādanena saṃyogena yaha nodako nodaṃya nodayati nānyathā tenārthaṃ nodanamucyate nodanaṃ tu na karmakāraṇamāha |
yathā paṅkasthāyāṃ pṛthivyāmiti |
yadā paṅkasthopari mandavyavasthāyito prastarapuṭikā kṣamya: pūrvena samartho sakṣāti tadā gurutvāpekṣa: prastarapuṭsaṃyogo nodanam |
yadā prayatnena dūrasthutyācca prastaro'bhihanyate paṅkastadā gurutvaprayatnavegāpekṣa: saṃyogo nodanaṃ yadā jalenāhanyate tadā samastāpekṣa: saṃyogo, nodanamiti yathāsambhavamuhyamiti ||karmaṇyaśva pratyakṣakarmanirūpaṇam |
kegoṣṭhau ya: saṃyoga ekasya vibhāgakṛta: karmaṇa: kāraṇaṃ so'pekṣavāt:  kṣityavāyvagnivātakayo: parasparavibhāgo yata: karmaiṇo jāyate tasyaivaikasya hetuvai:  saṃyogaviśeṣa: so'pekṣavāt: tasmādapi bahuṣu mahābhūteṣu karma bhavati|
yathā pāṣā ṇādiṣu nirdūre vastunyabhiṣātiteṣu |
nodanaṃ parasparāvibhāgahetorevaikasya karmaṇa: kā raṇaṃ na parasparavibhāgaheto: kṣayamagnivāto'pi parasparavibhāgahetorevaikasya karmaṇa:  kāraṇaṃ na parasparāvibhāgahetorityanumeyaksya karmaṇa: kāraṇam |
saṃyuktasaṃyogaṃ  vyākhyaṃ |
pādādibhirnaṃśvamānāyāmiti |
ekaśca pṛthivyāṃ pādena nudyamānoyāma bhihanyamānāyāṃ vā ye pradeśā na tudyante nāpyabhihanyante teṣvapi karma dṛśyate|
tatra  kṣatātaṃ pradeśāntarāṇāṃ nudyamānābhihanyamānapradeśai: saha saṃyuktameva saṃyoga:  kāraṇam |
yathābhiyātakaṃ durvaṃ bhūpradeśamabhihatya kiṃciduddeśaṃ notpatati tatra  pradeśāntarakriyāyāmuṣayāṇeta saṃyuktasaṃyogo hetu: ||
gurutvasya karmakāraṇatvamāha |
pṛthivyavakāśayorekatvavidhārakaṃsaṃyogā yatvenābhāve gurutvāccadyogamanaṃ tattvanam |
yathā muñjatagarīrajvādiśūnyam |
gurutvapratibandhakasya hastasaṃyogasyābhāve mṛgalasya yadyogamanaṃ tattvanaṃ  gurutvādudbhūti |
evaṃ gurutvavidhārakaṃpravāhābhāve śarīrasya patanaṃ ciṣprayogā ntarāve vegābhāvāt patanaṃ gurutvāt |
tatrācaṃ kutre gurutvād dvitīyādauni tu  gurutvasaṃskārobhyām |
teṣu mṛgalādiṣvādyaṃ karma gurutvādudbhūti tena karmaṇā  saṃskāra: kriyate tadanantaramunarakrmāṇi gurutvasaṃskārobhyāṃ jāyante dvayorapi  pratyekamanyat sāmagrīvidhāraṇāt ||
srotobhūtānāmapāṃ sthalā tridhābhisarpaṇaṃ yatta dṛḍhvatvāt spandanam |
katham kṣanatādroṣa:saṃyoginā vayavidravatvaṃ pratibaddhya avayavadravatvamapekṣārthasama vetaṃ tenaiva pratibaddhamuttarottarāvayavadravatvāni saṃyukta saṃyogai: pratibaddhāni |
yadā tu mādyayā cetuṣeda:  kṣato bhavati tadā kṣamatāt pratibaddhatvādavayavidra-dravyasya kāryotpattau nāsti hetuḥ samīpasthasyā-vayavadravyasyottarottarabhāgavayavadravyāṇāṃ pratiba-ndhakābhāvāddṛttilābhaḥ |
tataḥ kramaśaḥ saṃyuktānāmevābhimukheṇām tataḥ pūrvadravya vināśe sati pravakṣya-mānāvasthiteravayavairbhaṅgaṃ dravyamārabhyate tatra ca kāra-ṇaguṇapūrvakameṇa dravyatvamutpadyate tatra ca kāraṇānāṃ saṃyuktānāṃ pravalyena gamane yadavayayiti karmotpadyate tadu spandanākhyamiti ||
dravyasya kāraṇatvaṃ kathayati |
kṣitīpsutānāmaṇāṃ sthalādiprāptiṣeṇaṃ yatnaddravyāt spandanam aṇau yaśa sthalādiprāptiṣeṇa tatspandanaṃ dravyā-nugrāhi ityarthaḥ |
kathamiti praśnaḥ |
samantādiṅgunatvam |
samantātu ṣaktau raupyaḥsaṃyoge kṛtsnasaṃyoge sati cakramavaddhine dravyatvaṃ pratibaddhaṃ spandanaṃ vā na karoti cakramayaddravyasamavekāryamavetaṃ tenaiva pratibaddhaṃ yasminprathame vācādoṣaḥsaṃyogo rakti tadavayavaprathamadravyaṃ tenaiva raupyaḥsaṃyogena pratiba-ddhamuttarottarāṇi tvavayavaddravyāṇi saṃyuktasaṃyogaiḥ pratibaddhāni raupyaḥsaṃyuktenā-vayavena saha saṃyogādavayavāntarasya dravyatvaṃ pratibaddhamiti |
tatsaṃyogāduparasya pratibaddhamityanenaiva nyāyenottarottaradravyāṇi pratibaddhāni |
yadā tu mādhya hetubhedaḥ kṛto bhavati tadā samantāt pratibaddhasyāpyaviddravasya kāryotpattau nāsti doṣatareṇa hetunā samantāt pratibaddhasyāpyaviśo mahāparimāṇa-sthaikadeśakṛtenālpīyasā mārgeṇa nirgamāmāvāt hetubhedopasthasya tvapayavaddrava-tvasya vṛttilābho bhavati alpasyāpyadravasya tena mārgeṇa nirgatisambhavāt |
tasya vṛttilābhe śātarāmavayavaddravyānāmapi pratibandhakābhāvādvṛttilābhaḥ svakāryakartṛtvaṃ syāt |
tataḥ kramaśaḥ saṃyuktānāmevābhimukheṇām |
hetusamīpasthā uprayeḥ prathamamabhimukhīti tadvat tatsamīpasthastatastatsamīpasthaḥ ityevaṃ krameṇa sarve uprayā abhimukhīti te cābhimukhatvo na parasparapratibaddheśo abhi-karmaṇye drākṣāyanakramadiṅniyamañca|
varṇanti kiṃ tu tathābhisaṃdhinta yathā parasparasaṃyuktā bhavantītyetaduktautanā|
themuktaṃ veyūktānāmevābhisaṃdhiṣaṇ||
na punarakhyāmameyeṣu|anyūnatā|adhyardhayogānyamidhā|
niṣadhiṣaṇamiti saṃkhyānāntaropalabhyate||
tataḥ prāktanardhayogavināśe prabuddhaṣyavināśe  prabandhenādhiṣṭhitairadhaye: veyuktobhidaināptiṣyate|tadabadaidairva dravyamātmabhūte tacca  ca kāraṇaguṇapūrvakatveṇa dravyatvamucyate|
avayavadravyatvebhyāṃ draurghatvarekhāvidhi  dravyatvamucyate tacca ca kāraṇānāṃ saṃyuktānāṃ prabandhena yasmane yadavayavini kramī|
tyajate dravyatvāt tatsyandanam|
tacca tasmin dravyatve utpanne sati kāraṇānām|
vayavānāṃ prabandhena gamyate paṅkṣībhavinā('|')vijṛmbhaṇasya gamyate yadavayavini drava|
tvāt kramītyajate tatsyandanābhyam||
saṃskārāt karme itvādisūtram|
tathā cakrādi|
svavayavānāṃ pāṣvataḥ pratiniyatadigdeśasaṃyogavibhā|
gotvato yadavayavinaḥ|(2) saṃskārādaniyatadigdeśasaṃyo|
gavibhāganimittaṃ karme vahudravyasaṃyogiti|(3) |
evamā|
vayo gamanaviśeṣāḥ ||
saṃskārāt karmetvādisūtram|
tathā cakrādiṣu tathāśabdo yathāśabda|
mapekṣate yatraurniśyapratyayāt|(4) |
yathā kuṣyādiṣu saṃskārāt karme kāṣṭasya  tathā cakrādiṣvapi bhavatītyarthaḥ|
dhanadaiva draṣṭavyati|
avayavānāṃ pāśvaitaḥ prati|
niyatadigdeśasaṃyogavibhāgotpattau yadavayavinaḥ saṃskārādaniyatadigdeśasaṃyoga|
vibhāganimittaṃ karme taddraṣṭavya|
pāśvaitaḥ pratiniyatā ye dvigdeśāstaiḥ sahāva ya|
vānāṃ saṃyogavibhāgayorutpattau satyāṃ yadavayavinaḥ saṃskārādaniyatadigdeśaiḥ saṃyo|
go vibhāgaścayoganimittaṃ karme jñāyate taddraṣṭavyam|
prathamaṃ cakrādayavini  daṇḍaveyogāt karmotpadyate tatarottarāṇi karmāṇi nodanādiniyatāt karmejāt  saṃskārācca bhavanti|
evaṃ vegāt daṇḍaveyukte cakrādayave kācaṃ karme daṇḍaveyogā|
davayavāntareṣu ca saṃyuktasaṃyogāt daṇḍaveyuktasyāvayavasyottareṇakarmāṇi cakrā|
rāraudanācca apareṣāṃ saṃskārāt saṃyuktaveyogācca daṇḍavibhame na cakre svadeśavaleṣa  ca saṃskārādeva kevalāt|
upasaṃharati|
evamādayo gamanaviśeṣa iti||prāṇākhyai tu vāyau karme jñātmavāyusaṃyogādi-cchādveṣapūrvakaprayatnāpekṣājjāyate icchānuvidhānadarśenāt suṣupte tu jīvanapūrvakaprayatnāpekṣāt |
ākāśakāladigātmanāṃ satyapi dravyabhāve niṣkriyatvaṃ vāmanatvādivadadrutatvāt |
mūrtestvanekagatadravyaparimāṇāt tadanuvidhāyinī ca kriyā sā cākāśādiṣu nāsti tasmānna teṣāṃ kriyāsambandho'stīti ||
prāṇākhyai vāyau karme jñātmavāyusaṃyogādicchādveṣapūrvakaprayatnāpekṣājjāyate iti |
kathamidaṃ jñātamata āha |
icchānuvidhānadarśenāt dehakṛpūrvakādiprayoge tva-cchānuvidhāyinī prāṇakriyopalabhyate nākāraprayatnāviṣu rajāvi tadviruddhārthe prāṇakriyā dveṣādapi bhavati ataḥ prayatnṛtviśeṣyadapekṣate |
suṣupte jīvanapūrvakaprayatnāpekṣātsuṣupte prāṇakriyā prayatnakāryī prāṇakriyātvāt jāgrataḥ prāṇakriyāvat |
sa cecchādveṣapūrvako na bhavati suṣuptecchādveṣayorabhāvāt tasmājjīvana-pūrvaka eva niścīyate prāṇadhāraṇasya tantratvekṣatvāt |
catuṣu mahābhūteṣvanākāśā-diṣu kasmāt kriyotpattirnaṃ dvinihitetyāha |
ākāśakāladigātmanāmiti |
kriyatvaṃ mūrtatvena vyāptaṃ mūrtatvaṃ cākāśādiṣu nāstītyataḥ kriyāsatvamapi na vidyate ityarthaḥ |
etadeva vivṛṇoti |
mūrterityādinā |
taddhetukaṃ ||
svaviṣaye manasīndriyātmasaṃbandhamātre jāyataḥ karme ātmamanaḥsaṃyogādicchādveṣapūrvakaprayatnāpekṣāt anyābhiprāyasiddhyā ntareṇa viṣayāntaropalabdhidarśenāt |
suptasya prabodhakāle jīvanapūrvakaprayatnāpekṣāt |
aparvaṇi karmotpattiparvakarme cātmamanaḥsaṃyogādahetupe-kṣāt |
kathamu yadā jīvanasahakāriṇordharmādharmayorupa-yogāt prabodhe'smītyābhimavo vā tadā jīvansa-hāgyorghvakalyāt tatpūrvaikamayadvaikalyāt prāṇāniśāne balyamyābhyāṃ labdhavṛttibhyāṃ dharmoṣadhībhyāmātmamanaḥsaṃyogabahāyābhyāṃ vratadhārīrādibhāgākāraṇamapavargeṇakṣamotpadyate|
tataḥ ṣarīrādihirupagataṃ tābhyāmeva dharmoṣadhībhyāṃ bajurudrenātivāhikaśarīreṇa sambadhyate tatsaṅkrāntaṃ ca svagaṃ narakaṃ vāgatvā āśrayagurukapeṇa śarīreṇa sambaddhyate tatsaṃyogārthaṃ karmopasarpeṇamiti|
yogināṃ ca bahirantaścittasya manaso'bhipretadeśagamanaṃ pratyāgamanaṃ ca|
tathā sarvakāle pratyakṣeṇa śarīreṇa sambandhārthaṃ karmāhṛṣṭakāritam|
evamanyadapi mahābhūteṣu yatkalyatrānumānābhyāmanupalabhyamānakāraṇamupakārāpakārasamarthaṃ ca bhavati tadapyadṛṣṭakāritam|
yathā saṃgītāvayuktame śrutivādyorutthaitiryaggamane mahābhūtānāṃ prabodhaṇam|
śramiṣiktānāṃ maṇīnāṃ tatkaraṃ prati gamanañca|
ayaśco'yaskāntānisarpaṇaṃ ceti||
iti praśastapādabhāṣye karmapadārthaḥ vasāntaḥ||
manasi karmakāraṇamāha|
ṣaḍigraha iti|
jāgrataḥ puruṣasya ṣaḍigrahe manasi ṣaḍrīre manaso'ndriyāntarasambandhārthe karma ātmamanaḥsaṃyogādikacchadradrapūrvakaprakṣepekṣānuvṛtti icchādveṣapūrvakaḥ prayatno jāyate|
manasi kriyāheturiti|
kañcayetadvacanaṃ tadvāha|
antaḥkaraṇaprāptyamindriyāntareṇa viṣayopalabdhidarśanāt jāgratavasthāyāmindriyāntarakrameṇendriyāntareṇa bahutādinā viṣayopalabdhidṛśyate|
yadā rūpaṃ jighṛkṣate puruṣastadā rūpaṃ paśyati yadā rasaṃ jighṛkṣate sadā rasaṃrakṣayati na bāntaḥ|araṇyakhaṇḍasyamantareṇa bāhyendriyasya viṣayagrāhakatvamuktaṃ tasmādicchādeṣupūrvakānāṃ pravṛttāntanavi kriyā bhūteti gamyate|
dṛṣṭasyoti|
suṣuptu puṣuptasyendriyāntarasyanyāye prabodhakāle manavi kriyā jīvanapūrvaikapratyabhijñā-dātmanaive saṃyogādu|
capaṣapeṇoti|
etadapi kathamityādinā praśnapūrvakaṃ kṣayati|
ciciśātmanaḥ|saṃyogo jīvanamu tasya svakāryaikaraṇe yathoyamaiva saha-kāriṇo yadā tathāpanogād prabodho vināśo|nyonyābhimukho vā paramparāpatiba-dhyāt svakāryaikaraṇaṃ vā tato jīvanasahāyanoḥ yathoyayorvaitklye|abhāve iti tatpūrvaikapratyabhivekṣyāt jīvanapūrvaikasya pratyakṣasya vaikalyādamānāt prāgnā-yonityathe iti pacite usmin śarīre prāptasyāṃ yathoyayomyāṃ dehāntare phalaṃ bhojayitavyaṃ to|
labdhavṛttiko bhavatohiṃkaśarīropabhogayathoyayopamativadgutvādu dehāntaropabhogyā yathopayathomyāṃ kāryaṃ na kṛtamu yadā tvehiṃkaśarīropabhogo yathoyamo prabodho tadā dehāntaropabhogayathoyayorvṛttilābhaḥ pratibanthābhā vājjñātaḥ tāsyoṃ labdhavṛttisyamohakṣaye dehāpabhogayāt karmeṇo|nyāyyāmātmama-naḥ|saṃyogasahāyasyāṃ punaḥśarīrātmanaive vibhāgākāraṇamapavargeṇakṣīyanyate|
capaṣapeṇakṣamītyanātmanmānamanaḥsaṃyogotsamavāyikāraṇamu manaḥ samavāyikāraṇamu labdhavṛtto yathoyamo nimittakāraṇamu|
tatastadanantaraṃ tattvajño munuṣarīradṛhi-nirmate tāsyāmeva labdhavṛttisyā yathoyamomyāṃ ikāśayatyavetotivāhikaśarī-reṇa sambaddhūte tatpaścāntaṃ tadātivāhikaśarīraikāntaṃ manaḥ svagaṃ narakaṃ vā gacchati taca gatvā āśrayānurūpeṇa karmānurūpeṇa śarīreṇa sambaddhūte|
svagaṃ narake vā gṛhagamanaṃ śarīraṃ taca tāvanmanaḥsambandhena bhavinavyamu anyathā tasmimu deśebhogāsambhavāt|
na cātmavadgāvyameva manaso dehāntarasambandho|sti avyāpa-katvāt|
gaṅgaṃ ca tasyetāvadvarṇanaṃ kevalasya na sambhavati mahāpralayānantarāva-sthāyātirekeṇaśarīrasya manasaḥ karmobhāvāt|
tasmānnūtanaśarīrasyānyāvadmanu-dguvaṇānupātanikriyāyubhisaṅguṇādiprakarṣeṇosthamatisūkṣmamanupalabdhiyogyaṃ śa-rīraṃ parikalpyate|
tattu punaḥśarīramatikramya manasaḥ svarganarakādideśagatiśa-dmathamaikatvādativāhikādebhyucyate|
maraṇajanmanorantarāle manasaḥ karme śarīroparaktairatyaivopapadyate mahāpralayānantarāvasthāmatikramankarmeṣyatiriktve iti manaḥkarmatvāt dṛśyayānaśarīradṛṣṭimankarmeṣu|sāmānyapadārthanirūpaṇam |
dṛśyate iti |
tattvayogārthe ca kramopasargaṇamiti |
tena dṛśyaṃ nastṛ vā pratyakṣajātena śarīreṇa manaḥsaṃyogārthe kramopasargaṇamiti |
yogināṃ ca bahirdṛdṛṣṭitasya bahirleṃ, khātisya manaso'bhimatadeśagamanaṃ pratyāgamanaṃ ca |
tathā sarvakāle pratyakṣeṇa śarīreṇa sambandhārthaṃ manaḥkramodyuktāritam |
na kevalametāvanna sarvamanyadrapi mahābhūteṣu yatpratyakṣānumānābhyāmanupalabhyamānakāraṇamupakāraṇakārakhamathe tadapyudyuktāritam |
yathā saṃgīdāvaṇukrame cānivācyoprathodyuktāritaṃ, tathegamane mahābhūtānāṃ bhūyo'ṅkādrīnā ca prabodheṇa calanam |
parīkṣākāle'bhiśikṣānāṃ mayonātaskandha prati gamanam |
cakṣuṣo'pyakāntānabhiṣayeṇa ca camavetatvadyuktāritamiti ||
hitāhitaprāptiparihāraprāptiryannibandhanam |
karmeti paramaṃ tattvaṃ jñātaḥ kriyatā buddhi ||
iti bhaṭṭaśrīmīmāṃsakṛtāyāṃ padārthapraveśanyāyakandalīṭīkāyāṃ kramavadārthaḥ ṣamāptaḥ ||
atha sāmānyapadārthanirūpaṇam |
sāmānyaṃ dvividhamaparamaparaṃ ca |
svaviṣayasarvegatamabhinnātmakamanekavṛtti ekadravyabahutvātmasvarūpānugamapratyayakāri svarūpābhedenādhāreṣu prabandhena vartamānamanuvṛttipratyayakāraṇam |
kathaṃ prativiśiṣṭaṃ sāmānyāpekṣaṃ prabandhena jñānotpattāvaśyā'pratyayajanitāśca saṃskārāvatītvātprabandhapratyavekṣaṇād yadanugataṃ nāsti tatsāmānyamiti |
tatra ghaṭādāvanyaṃ paramanuvṛttipratyayakāraṇameva |
yathā parasparaviśiṣṭeṣu camavastukṛṣṇalādidravyekasmā nīladravyā niṣasvasthāt nīlaṃ nīlamiti pratyayānuvṛttiḥ tathā parasparaviśiṣṭeṣudravyaguṇakarmedavaviśiṣṭā dravyāditi pratyayānuvṛttiḥ sā cārthāntarādravyavittu mahattvīti yattadarthāntaraṃ sā dravyati siddhā |
sattānusambandhāt dravyāditi pratyayānuvṛttiḥ tasmāt sā vācyānyameva |
aparaṃ dravyatvaguṇatvakarmetyādi anuvṛttivyāptitvāt hetutvāt sāmānyaṃ viśeṣaśca bhavati |
tatra dravyatvaṃ parasparavidhiṣṭeṣu pṛthivyādiṣvanuvṛttihetutvāt sāmānyam guṇakarmabhyo vyāvṛttihetutvāt viśeṣaḥ |
tathā guṇatvaṃ parasparaviśiṣṭeṣu rūpādiṣvanuvṛttihetutvāt sāmānyam dravyakarmabhyo vyāvṛttihetutvāt viśeṣaḥ |
tathā karmatvaṃ parasparaviśiṣṭeṣūtkṣepaṇādiṣvanuvṛttipratyayahetutvāt sāmānyaṃ dravyaguṇebhyo vyāvṛttihetatvāt viśeṣaḥ |
evaṃ pṛthivītvarūpatvotkṣepaṇatvagotvacandratvapaṭatvādīnāmapi prācyapratīcīgatānāmanuvṛttivyāvṛttihetutvāt sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvaḥ siddhaḥ |
etāni tu dravyatvādīni prabhūtaviṣayatvāt prādhānyena sāmānyāni svāśrayaviśeṣakatvāttucchatā viśeṣākhyānīti ||
jayanti jagadutpattisthitisaṃhṛtihetavaḥ |
viśvasya paramātmāno brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ ||
sāmānyaṃ vyākhyai |
dvividhaṃ sāmānyaṃ paramaparaṃ ceti |
kṛtavyākhyānamanudduṣṭāvaraṃ |
sarveṣavemat sāmānyamiti kecit tanniṣedhārthamāha |sāmānyasyaiva viśeṣaprāmāṇyanirūpaṇam |
svaviṣayavedanātmiti |
yatsāmānyaṃ yatra piṇḍe pratīyate sa tasya svo viṣayaḥ tatra sarvātmanā yato dharmavedaṃ khaśeṣa tatpratyayāt |
khaśeṣavedanatvabhāve tvatupalabdhireva pramāṇam |
kāṃcidātmaka(1)pramitratvabhāvādyyena svabhāvenaiva piṇḍe vartate  sāmānyaṃ tenaiva svabhāvena piṇḍāntare'pi vartate tatpratyayātviśeṣādatyayeḥ |
anekeṣu piṇḍeṣu itiyeśya tadvedakañceti kāṃcidasvabhāvamanekatra vartate iti ca pratītisāmarthyāt samarthanīyam |
nahi pramāṇāvagate'rthe kāṃcidanupapattināma dvitvādikāmayamitratvabhāvanekatra vartate tasmāt sāmānyasya viśeṣo na spṛśyate tathāhi |
yaktradravyabuddhiriti |
sāmānyamekasmin piṇḍe draṣṭā: piṇḍāntare'pidṛṣṭaṃ vā piṇḍeṣvātmasvarūpānugamātprayaṃ karoti yaktrasya piṇḍasya draṣṭo'dṛṣṭenāṃ jñāpakatve yadi gauritipratyayasya bhāvād dvitvādikaṃ tvevaṃ na bhavatīti viśeṣaḥ |
anekavṛttitve vati yadekadravya svātmasvarūpānugamātprayayakāraṇaṃ tadṛ sāmānyamiti lakṣaṇāyoḥ |
śabadeva vivṛṇoti |
svarūpabhedeneti |
yakasminnu piṇḍe yatra svarūpaṃ tatprapañcāntare'pi tatsvādevetyatho'taḥ pravacanenānupadameva pūrvoktaviśeṣāparityāgena vartamānaṃ khadṛṣṭavuddhipratyayakāraṇaṃ svarūpānugamapratītikāraṇaṃ sāmānyam |
kathamiti parasya praśnaḥ |
kathamanekeṣu piṇḍeṣu sāmānyasya vṛttirityarthaḥ |
uttaramāha |
pratipiṇḍamiti |
piṇḍe piṇḍe prati sāmānyāpekṣaṃ yathā bhavati tathā jñānotpattau budhyā yo'pyāsādayatyanena yaḥ saṃskāro janitaḥ tatsādanītasya jñānapravāhasya jñānapravāhasya pratyavekṣaṇāt smaraṇād yadanugatamasti tatsāmānyam |
kimuktaṃ syāt yakasmin piṇḍe sāmānyamupalabhya piṇḍāntare tasya pratyabhijñānādekasyānekavṛttitvamavagamyate |
ata eva tatra bādhakahetavaḥ pratyakṣavirodhādupāsyante |
yatpratyakṣamuktaṃ paramaparaṃ ca dvividhaṃ sāmānyamiti savidvāṃso bibudhya kathayanti |
tatra paraṃ sanātanasāmānyamanuvṛttipratyayakāraṇameva |
yadyapi pratyekaṃ pratīyate sanā tathāpi viśeṣapadaṃ pratyanumānatvāh |
yathā parasparaviśiṣṭotpatti |
tattulyam |
dravyādiṣu ṣaṭsvaditipratyayānuvṛttiḥ vyatiriktapratyayanivandhanā triṣeṣu pratyayānuvṛttitvād karmatvādiṣu (2) nīlādyānuvṛttivat |
yasmāt sanā ṣiṅgu dravyādiṣu pratyayānuvṛttiṃ karoti na vyāghnoti tasmāt sāmānyameva na viśeṣa ityupadehā-rāje: |
apara dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvādau anuvṛttivyāvṛttihetutvāt sāmānyaṃ viśeṣaśca bhavati |
dravyatve dravyeṣvanuvṛttipratyayahetutvāt sāmānyam guṇakarmebhyo vyāvṛttihetutvādviśeṣaḥ |
guṇatvaṃ guṇeṣvanuvṛttipratyayahetutvāt sāmānyam dravya-karmebhyo vyāvṛttipratyayahetutvādviśeṣaḥ |
tathā karmatvaṃ parasparaviśeṣaṇatveṇādāvanuvṛttipratyayahetutvāt sāmānyaṃ dravyaguṇebhyo vyāvṛttihetutvādvi-śeṣaḥ |
dravyatvādiṣanyāsaṃyogināmapyanuvṛttivyāvṛttipratyayahetutvāt sāmā-nyaviśeṣabhāvo'stītyāha |
yadyamiti |
prāṇimātrāṇi sāmānyāni gotvāśvatvā-dīni aprāṇimātrāṇi ghaṭatvapaṭatvādīni |
kiṃ dravyatvādīnāṃ sāmānyavadbhūmau vāstavaṃ kiṃ vā viśeṣavadbhūpanā āhosvidbhumayavadbhūpanā abhāva |
yatnāntīti |
sāmānānāṃ bhāvaḥ sāmānyamiti sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ dravyatvādiṣu vidyate svārthasaṃvedī viśinaṣṭīti viśeṣa iti tu lakṣaṇaṃ nāsti|
ata yatnān mukhyayā vṛttyā sāmānyānyeva na viśeṣāḥ|
viśeṣalakṣaṇāṃ tūpacāreṇa labhante|
viśeṣo hi svārthasaṃvedī viśinaṣṭi dravyatvādikamapi vijātīyebhyaḥ svārthasya viśeṣaṇa, mi-tyetāvatā sāmarthyeṇopacārapravṛttiḥ ||
lakṣaṇabhedādeśaṃ dravyaguṇakarmebhyaḥ padārthāntaratvaṃ siddham|
ata eva ca nityatvam|
dravyādiṣu vṛttiniya-māt pratyayabhedācca parasparataccānyatvam|
pratyekaṃ svārtheṣu lakṣaṇaviśeṣādviśeṣalakṣaṇābhāvāccaikatvam|
ataevaparicchinnadeśāni sāmānyāni bhavanti tathā-pyupalabdhiniyamāt kāraṇasāmagrīniyamācca svaviṣa-yapravṛttāni |
antarāle ca saṃyogasamavāyamutpattimāhādyapadeśyānīti ||
iti praśastapādabhāṣye sāmānyapadārthaḥ samāptaḥ ||sāmānyasya sāmānyasya dravyādyaniriktatvanirūpaṇam |
dravyatvādīni dravyādivyatiriktāni na bhavanti vyaktarūpaṃ pṛthaktvārthanirūpaṇanyāyasyanityathāha |
lakṣaṇabhedāditi |
caturṇāmākārāṇudravyāṇāṃ dravyatvādīni vyāccurnibuddhavedyānāñca dravyādivyatireka: tasmādeṣāṃ dravyatvādīnāṃ  lakṣaṇabhedāt pratītibhedād dravyaguṇakarmabhya: pṛthaktvāntaratvam |
ata eva ca  nityatvam |
yata eva sāmānyasya dravyādibhyo bheda: ata eva nityatvam dravyādyabhede sāmānyasya dravyādivināśe vināśastadanutpāde cotpāda: syāt bhede tu nārya vidhiriti pratijñate iti ||
athaike vadanti(1) bhinneṣvanugatā buddhi: sāmānyaṃ vyavasāyamiti sā ca pratipiṇḍaṃ dravyapuruṣādiṣu na svātantryeṇa(2) sāmānyaviśeṣatvena dve vastunī pratibhāsayati nāpi samerviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaṃ gotvaṃ gotvavānityevamanudayāt |
kiṃ tu tādātmyagrāhiṇī pratītiriyam caitrasyamatyakṣātmatoparāmaśād ubhayoranyānyagrahaṇena svarūpāntarasaṃbhāvāca catuṣtvatā hi gotvasyaiva sāmānyāntarasyāpi svarūpaṃ vyāghṛttasāpi gotvatve'thasantarāṇāmapi(3) svabhāva: sāmānyāntarasyānugataṃ tu gotvasya svarūpam vyasantaravyāghṛttiñca goṣakte: svabhāva: parasparātmatāmantareṇānyo(4) na śakyate nirdeṣṭum |
na ca tasya sa eva svabhāva: sa eva ca sambandho'tyupapadyate ni:svabhāvasya sambandhyabhāvāt tasmātsvajātisaṃyoga: parasparātmanaiva tattvam |
eva kṣati bhedābhedavādo'pi viruddhyati |
yathā hi śāvaleyo(5) gaurityatra pratīyate tathā bāhuleyo'pi |
na cāsti kaścidvidvān śāvaleya eva gaurna bāhuleya iti ki tu sarveṣāmekajātirnaiva sa gaurayamapi gauriti |
tatra pratītibalena śāvaleyātmakasya gotvasya bāhuleyātmakatve siddhe śāvaleyādbhedo'pi viruddhyati |
ayameva hi sāmānyasya pūrvepiṇḍādbheda: yatpiṇḍāntarātmakatvam |
idameva ca sāmānyarūpatvaṃ yadubhayātmakatvam |
bhedābhedābhekṣasya viruddhātvāti cet na yuktibaddhya bhavata: sāmatatvametadabhidhātum |
tadviruddhaṃ yad buddhiviṣayati yatra sarvedā pramāṇena tathaiva pratīyate tatra virodhābhidhānameva viruddham |
anyathaivana dṛṣṭamiti cettu kiṃ vai pratyakṣamavapi anumānādiva dṛṣṭamanuvādāt itye tattvaudānamaṃśasyatham |
ayaivaṃ svārthamadhyāptṛ pravartate sadā yathā bahutsu yadṛdṛśyati  tathaiva tatu na svārthadṛṣṭamātroddeśena pratyākhyātamarhati sarvaprāptāsyātmānavasthṛtiḥ |
tasmātu sāmānyaṃ vyāpramanyādavinābheyo'nyādavināpravatsyādvāsmantarālpa sthāne cātuḥsthānāt nityamanityaṃ ca na punarniruddheṣe |
artha prāptaṃ tadviśīyokte |
kiṃ jātisaṃjñayorabilakṣaṇāmākāraṃ gṛhṇīte tatpratīteḥ uta tayorabhede gṛhṇāti  cāhosvitu parasparabilakṣaṇāmākārau |
kāce kalpe tāvadekameva vastu syāt  naikayorekātmakatvamabilakṣaṇākārabuddhivaicitryāmaidalakṣaṇatvāt |
dvitīye  tu kalpe vyāhatireva |
bilakṣaṇākārayorvatinaiva hi bhedavaiśiṣṭistasyāḥ sambhave  jñātiṃ tayorabhedagatipatteśca nāsti kathaṃ bhinnayorekadeva vyavasthāpyate kathaṃ  tāhi sādātmyapratītiḥ na kathañciditi vadāma |
yadi tāvadeka ākāro 'nubhūyate ekasyaiva vastunaḥ pratītiriyaṃ naikayoḥ |
atha dvāvākārā vanubhūyete sadātmyāḥ pratītirasambhava eva |
yatpunarāgaurityayamabhilāpo'nna  saṃvedanaṃ tatsamavāyasāmarthyāt saṃyoge hi dvayoḥ saṃyogemāvaḥ samavāyasya  punareva mahimā yadeva sambandhināvayaḥ piṇḍavadvivṛt piṇḍo'bhedavacca pratīyete  jātiśca na ca vyakteḥ svarūpaṃ tena satyapi bhede baṭarādivat kuṇḍasya  jātito vyakteḥ svarūpaṃ pṛthaktvena niścīyate parasparāpekṣārūpaṃ tūpalabhyotpakṣa  dūre gotvāyaṣṭake api piṇḍasya grahaṇāt pūrvaipiṇḍaprahaṇo'pi piṇḍāntare gotva grahaṇāt tasmādukterityanyaṃ bhinnameva sāmānyamiti tākikakāṇāṃ prakriyā |
dṛvyādiṣu vṛttiniyamāt pratyayabhedācca parasparatasteṣānyatvam |
dṛvyatvā dayaḥ pratyekaṃ dṛvyādiṣveva niyatāḥ pratyayabhedaścaiteṣu dṛśyate tasmāddṛvyādiṣu  vṛttiniyamāt pratyayabhedācca dṛvyatvādīnāṃ parasparato bhedaḥ |
abhedātmakaṃ  sāmānyamiti pūrve pratisāmānyenoktaṃ tahidyānīṃ pramāṇairbhedaṃ karoti |
pratyekaṃ  svārthayogitvāt |
lakṣaṇairevaiti |
lakṣaṇamanugalakṣakajñānam |
tasya pratyekaṃ  pratipiṇḍamabhiśāsedaikapūrṇāyāsāhādyape bhede lakṣaṇasya pramāṇavyāpāracca  sāmānyasya svārthayogaikatvamavi svalakṣaṇāviyogaḥ |
"svaviṣaye sarvaṃ sāmānyaṃ  samaveti nānyatreti yatvemuktaṃ tasya kāraṇamāha |
yathokteti |
yathoktaṃ sāmā-sāmānyamanye sāmānyasya dravyādātiriktatvanirūpaṇam |
nyāni yatha tatropajāyamānena piṇḍena sambandhādaparicchinnatve'pyanyaniyamate'pi tathāpyupalabdhavyāptyābhivyañjakatvādyavayavasthānaviśeṣasya niyamādviyatatvāt piṇḍotpādakṣaṇārambhātprāgabhautikayamācu svaviṣaye sarvaiva samavayanti nānyatreti |
yatastatramevatrāpi |
ḍākṣādisasthānaviśeṣe gotvasya vyañjakaḥ kaivaraḥ diṅsthānaviśeṣo'śvatvasya viciguṇādideśasthānaviśeṣo ghaṭatvasya pratītiniyamāt |
yate ca saṃsthānaviśeṣā na sarveṣu piṇḍeṣu sāyāraṇāḥ api tu pratiniyateṣu bhavanti |
tatra yadyapi sarve sāmānyaṃ sarveṣopajāyamānena svaviṣaye'pi piṇḍāntareṇāpi sambaddhuṃ kṣamate tathāpi yatyābhivyañjakaṃ yatra piṇḍe dṛśyate tasya tatraiva samavāyo nānyatra |
evaṃ sāmasya niyamādapi sāmānyasambandhaniyamaḥ |
yatha hi tantvādīnāṃ kāraṇānāṃ svabhāvo yatraikatrapradhāne dravye paṭatvameva samavaiti nānyat   yatha hi pṛthivyādīnāṃ mahimā yatraiḥ kriyamāṇe dravye ghaṭatvameva samavaiti nānyatr na tāvat sāmānyamanyato gatvācyate śabdavyate nirjigamiṣatvāt |
tathāpi yadi pūrve nāśotr tatropajāyamānena piṇḍenāsya sambandho na syāt durutte ca sarvatropajāyamānena piṇḍena sambandhaḥ tasmāt sarve sarvasāmī|ta kṣaya cintanaṃ tatirāmbuvedrāh |
antarāle saṃyogasamavāyarūpyabhāvādvyapadeśānaucityāt |
antarālamiti ākāśaṃ vā dvidravyaṃ vā hastādimadhyavartinaidravyabhāvo vā teṣu gotvādisāmānyānāṃ na saṃyogo nāpi samavāyaḥ |
na cābhavyadrutānameva teṣāmavasthāne pramāṇamasti ato'ntarāle na sāmānyāni vyapadiśyante na cāntotpadyaḥ |
atha yahīṃ tatropajāyamānena piṇḍena sambadhyante kāraṇasāmarthyāt |
saṃyogo svanyataḥ samavāyasya bhavati saṃyogāvasthitasya vā bhavati tasmādvijalaṇastu samavāyo yatha yathaiva piṇḍotpattau kāraṇāni vyāpriyante tatha tathaiva kāraṇānāṃ sāmarthyāt piṇḍe'nyatonāgatasya samāśritasyāpi sāmanyasya bhavati vastuśakteraparimeyatvāccetyarthaḥ ||
abhāvaḥ so'gatāḥ |
pratīyamāneṣu bhedeṣu bhaviṣyatvadevakasyākārasyānupalambhāt sāmānyaṃ nāstyeveti |
tadayuktam |
anekārtha gobyaktiṣvanuduṣṭamyamānāsvakṣajādivyaktivilakṣaṇatayā sāmānyākārapratītisambhavāt |
yadi śāba-tyādiṣu parasparanirapekṣamanubhūyamānaṃ na kiṃcidasti yathā gavāśvatvatayoḥ parasparavilakṣaṇāḥ khabedyante tathā go vyaktayo'pi khabedyāḥ syuḥ yathā vā govyaktayaḥ ṣaḍbhyaḥ pratīyante tathā gavāśvavyaktayo'pi pratīyeran viśeṣābhāvāt |
niyamena tu govyaktayaḥ pratīyamānāḥ ṣaḍbhyāḥ ḍajavīsādhāraṇasvakṣādivyāhṛtaṃ kiṃcidekaṃ hṛdayādivati yathārthamiyamākāṃkṣitvādikahetutvācca |
govyaktīnāmekatvamiti cet nākhati sāmānye vyaktīnāmiva vyaktihetunāṃ vyaktikāryāṇāmapi parasparavyāhananānabhikatvādasambhavāt |
kiṃ ca yadyekahetutvādekatvaṃ mithaḥ kāraṇaṃ pramāṇāṇāṃ vyaktīnāmekatvaṃ na syāt dṛśyate cākṣicakṣurbhāvānumāni kāraṇamedo yathā cakṣurādinirvartanādidṛśāna śādityaprabhāstakṣobhisāt sūryakāntādau prayokṣyati: |
ekakāryatvaviśiṣṭe ca vijātīyānāmapyekatvāpattiḥ dṛṣṭāpi vāhyadehādikriyā gavādivyaktīnāmiva matsyādivyaktīnāmapi yā ca gotve dṛśyate na ca vāhyate ṣa gotve syāt |
api ca sāmānyābhāve ko'rthaḥ śabdasaṃsargaviṣayaḥ na tāvat svalakṣaṇaṃ tasya kṣaṇikasya khavedyo vyāhṛtasya ṣaḍgunirviṣayatvabhāvāt |
nāpi vikalpaḥ śabdārthaḥ tasya kṣaṇikatvādyadhāraṇatvāt |
vikalpākāraḥ śabdārtha iti cet kiṃ vikalpākāro vikalpavyatiriktaḥ avyatirikto vā yadi bhinnaḥ sa kiṃ sarve vikalpasādhāraṇaḥ kiṃ vā prativikalpaṃ bhidyate sādhāraṇatve tāvadetasya sāmānyādabhedaḥ yadi pūrva tada jñānacaittayamasyākaṃ cā''śraye iti śaktimukhenaiva pratīyamānatvāditi kaiścidiṣyeḥ |
yadi vyatirikto'yamākāraḥ pratijñānaṃ bhidyate athavā jñānādavyatirikta eva ubhayathāpi na śabdasaṃsargayogyatāsānavadyaścayavaṅgulatvāt vikalpaḥ paraspayeṇa tadarūpaniyatitvenyādvāharaṇasvarūpatayā svākāramāropya vikalpayati |
tenāyaṃ śabdasaṃsargī iti cet vāhyatvenāropito vikalpākāra ekārthīnaikabhāvatvāt vikalpe jāyamāne jāyamāna eva vinaśyati vinaśyanniva pratīyamānaḥ prativikalpaṃ bhinna evāvatiṣṭhate |
na ca bhedānupalabdhirniṣeddhumahartītyanubhūyate |
apohyate |
yādṛśameko govikalpo vāhyātmatayā svapratibhāsamāropayati govikalpāntaramapi tādṛśamevāropayati vikalpasya pratyekaṃ svākāramāpaṇamahimnāna parasparāropitānāmākārāṇāṃ bhedagrahaṇāya paryāyujyanti |
tasyopeyagrahaṇādhīnatvāt tadagrahaṇādvā vikalpyāropitānāmākārāṇāmekasyabhāropya vikalpyanāmeko viṣaya ityucyate tadeva ca sāmānyaṃ bahiraṃgopagineyo vikalpākāribhyo'pyantamedagrāhiṇāpābhāsahetupaṃ svalakṣaṇānnabhadākāraropiteṣvatulyaiḥ sahābhiḥ samasyādyeṣusamākāra ityucyamānamāropitabāhyatvaṃ śabdābhidheyaṃ śabdasaṃsargaviṣayaḥ tadarthagrahāya eva svalakṣaṇārthagrahāya: tadātmatayā tasya sadāropāt anyatrāpravṛttitvabhāvaṃ bhāvābhāvasādhāraṇa ceṭ gorasti nāstīti prayogāt bhāvātmakatve hyasya gorastīti prayogādabhāvaḥ punaruktatvāt nāstīti ca na prayujyate virodhāt evaṃ tasyābhāvātmakatve nāstīti punaruktaṃ astīti virudhyate |
yathoktasu |
ghaṭo nāstīti vaktavyaṃ vaktavyaṃ hi yato ghaṭaḥ |
nāstītyapi na vaktavyaṃ virodhāt ghaṭaśūnyayoḥ ||
iti |
yatastādeva ca mithyānāmapi vyaktināmaikatāyābhāvaḥ |
idaṃ hi sarvathāmeva vikalpānāṃ viṣayo'syekatvādikalpanāmayoktatve teṣāmekatvāt talkārakāṇāṃ pratiniyadabhāvināṃ nirvikalpakānāmayoktatva teṣāmekatvācca tatkāraṇānāṃ vyaktināmaikatvābhāvaḥ |
yathoktasu ||
yakrutvaḍamūtrasya hetutvādvairibhedinī |
yakṛttohetumadvaiva vyaktināmapyabhinnatā ||
iti |
etadupyayuktam |
vikalpānupapatteḥ vikalpākārāṇāṃ bhinnaprahaṇādāropitamekayam sāmānyamācakṣate mithyāḥ atha vayaṃ |
kimākārāṇāṃ bhedāgrahaṇamevāmedasamāropaḥ āhosvidamedagrahaṇamevāmedaropaḥ |
na tāvadvādyaḥ kalpaḥ bhedasamāropitasyāpi pravṛttatvāt |
yathā vikalpyākārāṇāṃ bhedo na gṛhyate taddvamedo'pi na gṛhyate tava bhedāgrahaṇādbhedāropavadamedāgrahaṇādbhedāropasyāpi pravaktavyamedācintyatvahārapravṛttyayogāt |
abhedagrahaṇasamedāropa ityapi na yuktam abhāvāṭeko hyanekadarśanaṃ teṣāṃ bhedābhedau pratyeti nairātmyavāde tu sarvonaikārthadṛṣṭā na kaścidasti vikalpānāṃ pratyekaṃ svākāramātraniyatatvāt |
astukāmasti bhavadvṛtta mavāṅgamahimāyākāratvādi bhavedviśeṣāt gavākāratvaprayovyāhṛtireva nimittamastīti cet ke punaragāvo madgāvutpatyā gavākāratvekatvamāropyate |
ye gāvo na bhavanti te ugāvaḥ iti cet gāvaḥ ke te ye ugāvo na bhavantīti cet yavāṅgasvarūpe nirdṛṣṭe tadgāvutpattyenābhāvo svarūpaṃ nirdṛśyate kabhāvo svarūpe nirdṛṣṭite tadgāvutpatyā bhāvo svarūpanirdṛṣaṇamityekāpratipattāvataratātriyate'pyathāpratipattiḥ |
yathāha tatrabhavān |
vidrutva gorapohyena goniścāyakatvataḥ ṣa: |
tatha goreva vaktavyo nañā ya: pratiṣidhyate ||
gavyamiddhe svagonosti tadabhāve tu go: kṛta: ||
iti |
kathānyayogeṣu śabdāyojanātopinavāṅgasvarūpatattvamucyate |
ko'pyago'ho  nāma kimagoraho'ho bhāvo'bhāvo vā|
yadi bhāva: sa ki gopiṇḍasabhāvo'bhāvo piṇḍātmaka: gopiṇḍātmakatve tāvadasvādhāraṇatā na vācyādhāraṇātmake urthe śabdapravṛttirapyuktam|
kogopiṇḍātmaketyapyanena traiśi durghaṭāntaraṃ caitadvisakam|
yadyoṣaśabdasya gorityayamartho na prāpnoti(1) yadi tu piṇḍavyatiriktamanekādhāraṇaṃ vastubhūtamagohetvabdamidheyate śabdamātraviṣayā vidhṛtipattiḥ |
kathāgo'ho'styavyāhṛtipūrvatvadabhāvatvabhāva kathyate(2) tadākhya pratya yatvena śaṅkā na syāt jñānajanakatvena vāhya(3)lakṣaṇatvāt kabhāvasya ca samastārthakriyāvirahalakṣaṇatvāt |
na ca pratyayādhārīkṛte'rthe saṅketagrahaṇamattītyasāve śabdasyāpravṛttireva na ca tasmin pratīyamāne bhotṛbuddhiviṣayā pravṛttiḥ syāt bhāvābhāvayoranyatvādaprasaṅgātca |
svalakṣaṇātmakatvenābhāvapratītāvavivekena svalakṣaṇo pravṛttiriti cet(4) dṛśyavikalpayāyojakīkṛtyāttvahevikalpyo dhātvarthā pratipattiḥ(5) pratipūrṇabhūmiti |
tadayuktam |
kabhāvote tadātmakatvayā kabhāvaprakāropānupapatteḥ |
na ca bhotṛsvadānīmaśeṣanipattirasti śabdasyātadrūpavyatvāt pramāṇāntarasyābhāvāt |
kartṛtva ca śabdādarthe pravṛtti(6)statvātmābhāvo'pi śabdārthaḥ |
na jñānecchādi nimittaṃ kiñcidyasti sarvamidamathejātaṃparasparasyāśunaṃ pratikṣaṇamanṛrvaṃmanumeyamanupramāṇaṃ ca śabdāt pratīyate nārthe pratyakṣāgrahītamapi jñānopādānaviśeṣe bhedetu aparicālatasāmarthyetvāt |
asti ca śabdo vyavahāraḥ asti ca prāṇṛgunnīcānuvartinī pratyakṣapūrvikā hitārhitaprāptiparihararāśau lokayātrā yena ca mithyātva vyaktau kṣāmānyamekaiṃ vyavasthāpayati yadṛdṛśā śabdāt pratyayā|*uktopasthāne tajjñātīyatvena tadapratiṣeghopayogāt vicikitsyāpūrvānumāne'pyathai lokaḥ pravartate iti ||
mithyetyanumālakṣaṇā buddhirutpannibandhanā |
anyā amṛte cetreya lokayātrā pravartate ||
iti |
iti bhaṭṭaśrīdharabiracitāyāṃ padārthapraveśanyākhyaktantralokāyāṃ ṣāmānyapadārthaḥ samāptaḥ ||
atha viśeṣapadārthanirūpaṇam |
anteṣu bhavā antyāḥ svāśrayaviśeṣakatvādviśeṣāḥ |
vināśāsamarahiteṣu nityadravyeṣvaṇuvākāśakāladigātmanaścataṣu pratidravyadaiśikāśo vartamānāḥ atyantāvyākhyattibuddhihetavaḥ |
yathātmahādīnāṃ bhāvādivyathādibhyastulyākṛtiguṇakriyāvayavavaiśeṣoganimittā pratyakṣavyāhṛttidṛṣṭā gauḥ śuklaḥ śīghragatiḥ pīnakṣakudamān mahāvṛṣabha iti|
tathātmadviṣidhānāṃ yogināṃ nityeṣu tulyākṛtiguṇakriyeṣu paramāṇuṣu muktātmanastu ca anyonyanimittāvasaṃvādi cettho nimitteyaḥ pratyāyyārtha vilakṣaṇo'sau vilakṣaṇo'yamiti pratyakṣavyāhṛttiḥdeśakālaviśeṣarthe ca paramārtho ca svabhāvamiti pratyabhijñānaṃ ca bhavati te'nyathā viśeṣā: |
yadi punaratyantaviśeṣamantareṇa yogināṃ yogajāhutmot pratyakṣavyāvratti: pratyabhijñānaṃ ca syāt tata: kiṃ syādeve bhavati |
yathā na yogajāhutmīvadupar̤he śukrapratyaya: sañjāyate anyatrāhuṣṭe ca pratyabhijñānam |
yadi syānniścayā bhavet tathaihātyantaviśeṣamantareṇa yogināṃ na yogajāhutmīt pratyakṣavyāvratti: pratyabhi-jñānaṃ vā bhavitumarhati |
athānyaviśeṣeṣviva para-māṇuṣu kasyāt svata: pratyakṣavyāvratti: kalpyate iti cenna tādātmyāt |
ihātadātmakaiṣvapyanimitta: pratyayo bhavati yathā ghaṭādiṣu pradīpāt na tu pradīpe pradīpāntarāt |
yathā gavāśvamahiṣādīnāṃ svata evā-śuddhitvaṃ tathogādnyeṣāṃ tathaihāpi tādātmyāda-nyaviśeṣeṣu svata eva pratyakṣavyāvratti: tadyogāt paramāṇubadiṣyati ||
iti praśastapādabhāṣye viśeṣapadārtha: samāpta: ||viśeṣapakṣe viśeṣasya dravyādyatiriktatvanirūpaṇam |
viśeṣasyākhyānā'thamāha |
anteṣu bhavā antyā iti |
utpādavināśayorante uvasthitatvādantaśabdavācyāni nityadravyāṇi teṣu bhavā: sthitā ityartha: |
svāśrayasya sarvato viśeṣakatvādanudravyatvādṛheto: |
yataddravyaṇoti |
vināśārambharahitebhivyaktyādinā |
vināśārambharahitebhirityasyapadasya vivaraṇamṛ |
atyantasyāvṛttibuddhihetava iti ca svāśrayasya viśeṣakatvādityasya vivaraṇamṛ |
pratidravyamekaikaśo vartamānā iti |
dravyaṃ dravyaṃ pratyekaiko viśeṣo vartata ityartha: |
yaktenaiva viśeṣeṇa svāśrayasya vyāvṛttibuddhirekāviśeṣakalpanāvaiyarthyāt |
yathā caida viśeṣaṇā lakṣaṇa bhavati tathā pūrve vyākhyātam |
bindu viśeṣabuddhāve teṣāṃ lakṣaṇābhidhāyaṃ yuktaṃ nābindu ityāśaṅkya viśeṣaṇāṃ bahutve pratipādayituṃ yanyasavanārayati |
yathetyādinā |
yathā gavādiṣvakhādiṣyastutyāṃkṛtinimittā goriti guṇānimitā śukla iti kriyānimitā śīghragatiriti avayavanimitā ghonakarṇadṛgniti saṃyoganimittā mahākāyaṭa iti aṅgadādīnāṃ pratyayavyāvṛttidṛṣṭā |
tathāsmadviśeṣāṇāṃ yogināṃ tulyākṛtiguṇakriyeṣu tulyākṛtiṣu tulyaguṇeṣu tulyakriyeṣu paramāṇuṣu muktātmanastvu cānyanimittā'sambhavā(1)tyo'yo nimittebhya: pratyādhārasamavetasāddravyaṇa iti pratyayavyāvṛttirbhavati te'ntyā viśeṣā: |
yathāsmadādīnāṃ gavādayevyaktiṣu pratyayabhedo bhavati tathā paramāṇvādiṣvapi tadṛdvinā parasparabhedayā pratyayabhedena bhavitavyaṃ vyaktibhedasambhavāt |
na cāsya vyaktibheda eva nimittam |
tadṛdguṇakarmaṇopi sthāṇādiṣu saṃśayadarśanāt |
nimittāntaraṃ ca nāsti ākṛterūpasya kriyāyāśca tulyatvāt |
na ca nirnimitta: pratyayabhedo dṛṣṭa: tasmādavaśyaṃ nimittaṃ ca viśeṣa iti |
deśavigrakarṣeṇa kālaviprakarṣeṇa ca dṛṣṭā: paramāṇava: kasyacitsyātkasyacinnasyāt |
sāmānyaviśeṣavattvāt ghaṭādivat |
na ca pūrvadṛṣṭe'pi pratyabhijñāna(2)viśeṣāvagatirantareṇa bhavati ato'sti tasya nimittaṃ viśeṣa: |
aca codyati |
yadi punariti |
yathā yogagrahaṇenābhyāsayogināmatīndriyārthadarśanaṃ bhavati tathā viśeṣapratyantareṇa pratyabhyāvṛtti: pratyabhijñānaṃ ca bhaviṣyatīti codanārtha: |
samādhatte naivamiti |
yathā yogināmayukte śukrakṣatrayo na bhavati atyantādṛṣṭe ca pratyabhijñānaṃ na syāt yadi syāt tiryagādayo bhavet |
tathā antyaviśeṣamantareṇa pratyabhyāvṛtti:pratyabhijñānaṃ ca na bhaviṣyatīti vaiśeṣikādyumatenendriyārthasaṃbandhaṃ na punarasmākṣibhimataṃ yaca pratyayo bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ |
punaruktidoṣāt |
caśvāntyaviśeṣekṣiti |
na śabdatvaviśeṣo'sti viśeṣāntarameva tu |
travyatvānāt |
yathā ca teṣu  viśeṣāntaramantareṇa svata eva pratyakṣavyāptirbhaviti yogināṃ tathā paramāṇuṣvapi  bhaviṣyati kiṃ viśeṣakalpanayetyantaramāha |
neti |
granthaśeṣaṃ tatra kutastādātmyāt |
etadeva vivṛṇoti |
iheti |
atadātmakatvasyaitannimittaḥ pratyayo bhavati  na tadātmakeṣu yathā ghaṭādiṣu prakṣāśasvabhāveṣu pradīpādeḥ prakāśasvabhāvāt  prakāśo bhavati na tu pradīpe pradīpāntarāt prakāśaḥ kiṃ tu svata eva |
yathā gajāśvamahiṣādīnāṃ svata evānuṣaktiḥ svaguḥ pratyakṣaprakāratvaṃ tathogināṃ  tatputhaktvāttadanyeṣāmapṛthaktvam tathaivāpi tādātmyādravyatantvādutisvabhāvatvāt(1)dravyaviśeṣeṣu svata eva svarūpādeva pratyakṣavyāptinaṃ viśeṣāntarasvabhāvāt |
atadātmakeṣu tu paramāṇuṣu sāmānyadharmeṣu(2) viśeṣayogādeva  pratyakṣavyāptiryuktā na svarūpamātrāditi ||
nityadravyeṣu dravyeṣu parasparasamanvaye |
pratyekamanuvṛttena viśeṣā medahetavaḥ ||
iti bhaṭṭaśrībhāskarakṛtāyāṃ padārthavivecana(3)vyākhyāsamandaloṭīkāyāṃ viśeṣapadārthe. pañcamaḥ ||
atha samavāyapadārthanirūpaṇam |
ayutasiddhānāmādhāryādhārabhūtānāṃ yaḥ sambandha ihamatyayahetuḥ sa samavāyaḥ |
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣāṇāṃ kāryakāraṇabhūtānāmakāryakāraṇabhūtānāṃ vāyutasiddhānāmādhāryādhārabhāvenāvasthitānāmihedamitibuddhiyeto bhavati yatastāvayogāt(4)sadhigatātya-svānāmaviṣṭaprabhāva:(1) sa sambāyākhya: sambandha:|
ayathā yathaiha kumbhe dadhyītipratyaya: sambandhe sati dṛṣṭadravyairha tantuṣu paṭa: iha vīraṇeṣu kaṭa: iha dravye guṇakarmeṇī(2) iha dravyaguṇakarmeṣu sattā iha dravye dravyatvam iha guṇe guṇatvam iha karmeṇi karmatvam iha nityadravye antyā viśeṣā itipratyayadarśīnādastyeṣāṃ sambandha iti jñāyate ||
antardhvāntavide(3) viśvacehārirūpahetave |
nirmelajñānadehāya nama: vāmāya śambhave ||
samavāyanirūpaṇāgraivāha |
ayutasiddhānāmādhāyādheyabhūtānāṃ kāryakāraṇabhūtānāmakāryakāraṇabhūtānāṃ ya: sambandha ihetyapradeha: sa samavāya: |
tavetatkṛtavyākhyānamudbhūṇamahaṃ |
ke te ayutasiddhā: yeṣāṃ sambandha: samavāyo bhavedata āha |
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣāṇāmiti |
kāryakāraṇabhūtānāmakāryakāraṇabhūtānāmiti niyamalakṣaṇam(4) |
ayutavākyavināśamityatra vyatirekamukhena nityadravyatatsamavetānāmanityaguṇānāṃ karmaṇāmuttarāṃ kāryakāraṇabhūtānāṃ samavāya: nityadravyatatsamavetānāmanyataranityaviśeṣabhūtānāṃ vākāryakāraṇabhūtānāṃ samavāyo'yutasiddhānāmiti niyama: |
yadyapyāyogyāgrāmilanāvasthitānāmityapi niyama eva |
ehedamitibuddhimeta: kāraṇātputrati yatsatyāvarjanatānāṃ niyatadeśāvasthitānāmapigatānyatvānāmapigatasvarūpabhedānāmavirūbho'prathagabhāvo''tmānantya sa samavāya: pricayo: parasparāpekṣabudhyā sambandhabuddhatvenāvagamyāt|
etadeva kathamityādinā praśnapūrvekamupapādayati |
yathā iha kumbhe dadhyītipratyaya: kumbhadadhno: sambandhe sati dṛṣṭa: |
tatheha tantuṣu paṭa ityādipratyayānāṃ darśanāvaśyeṣāṃ tantupaṭādīnāṃ sambandha iti jñāyate |
iha tantuṣu paṭa ityādipratyayā: sambandhanimittakā: avayāvipratyayatvāt(5) iha kumbhe dadhyītipratyayavat ||na cāsau saṃyogaḥ sambandhināmayutasiddhatvāt anyatarakarmādinimittātsamavāyāt vibhāgāntatvādarśanādabhikaraṇābhikartavyayoreva bhāvāditi ||
nanvaya saṃyogo bhaviṣyatītyata āha |
na cāsau saṃyoga iti |
casau tatpuṭayornāṃ sambandho na saṃyogo bhavati kuta ityacāha |
sambandhināmayutasiddhatvāditi |
saṃyogā hi yutasiddhānāmeva bhavati |
ayaṃ tvayutasiddhānāmiti|
tathā saṃyogo'nyatarakarmeva ubhayakarmaivaḥ saṃyogajo vā syāditi|
iha tu anyatarakarmādīnāṃ nimittānāmabhāvo bhavetyādirakāraṇasāmarthyenā vidyāt|
saṃyogasya vibhāgāntatvaṃ vibhāgavināśatvaṃ dṛśyete na samavāyasya saṃyogaḥ svatantrayorapi bhavati yayoryorasthitayoraṅgulyoḥ ayaṃ tvabhikaraṇābhikartavyayoreva bhavati tasmādyāyaṃ saṃyogaḥ kiṃ tu tasmāt pṛthageva ||
sa ca dravyādibhyaḥ padārthāntaraṃ bhāvabalakṣaṇamedāt |
yathā bhāvasya dravyatvādīnāṃ svāśrayeṣu ātmānurūpa pratyayakartṛtvāt svāśrayādibhyaḥ parasparatattvādhīnatarabhāvaḥ tathā samavāyasyāpi pañcasu padārtheṣvahetitvapratyayadarśanāt tebhyaḥ padārthāntaratvamiti |
na ca saṃyogavajñāterna bhāvabalakṣaṇaviśeṣāt viśeṣaliṅgābhāvāśca tasmādbhāvatvasarvaikaḥ samavāya iti ||
yadā sthite samavāye tasya dravyādibhyo bhedaṃ pratipādayati |
sa ca dravyādibhyaḥ padārthāntaramiti |
kuta ityata āha |
bhāvabalakṣaṇabhedāditi |
yato'dravyaṇīti |
yayokta |
bhāva iti ghaṭāsāmānyamucyate |
dravyatvādīnādipadena guṇatvādiparigrahaḥ |
yathā bhāvasya svāśrayeṣu dravyaguṇakarmasu ātmānurūpaḥ pratyayaḥ ghaṭvāditipratyayaḥ dravyatvasya svāśrayeṣu dravyeṣvātmānurūpaḥ pratyayaḥ dravyaṃ dravyamitipratyayaḥ guṇatvasya svāśrayeṣu guṇeṣvātmānurūpaḥ pratyayo guṇo guṇa itipratyayaḥ karmatvasya svāśrayeṣu karmasu ātmānurūpaḥsamavāyaśca ṣamavāyyasya dravyādītirittvanityatvāniṅkuṇatvāt |
pratyaya: kārye kāryātinatyaya: tasya kartṛtvādṛ bhāvadravyatvādīnāṃ svāśrayādravya: parasparatattvādhīnatatbhāva: tathā samavāyasyāpi ṣaṅṣu padārtheṣvahetipratyayadarśanāt tebhya: pṛthaktvaḥ padārthāntaratvam |
kimayameka āhosvidaneka ityākāṅkṣā na ca saṃyogavadanānatvasiddhi: |
yathā saṃyogo nānā naiva samavāya: |
kutaṃ ityacāha |
bhāvaboddhṛṇāṃ viśeṣādṛdravyaliṅgabhāvācca |
yathā sattvādinimānasya lakṣaṇasya sarveṣāmaviśeṣadarśanajanyapratyakṣe'pi bhede lakṣaṇasya pramāṇasyābhāvācca sarvaikaśo bhāva: taddviśeṣanimittapratyasya lakṣaṇasya sarvaikacailakṣaṇyāddve pramāṇābhāvācca sarvaika: samavāya iti |
upasaṃharati |
tasmāditi ||
nanu yadyeka: samavāyo dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvādiviśeṣaṇai: saha sambandhekatvāt padārthaṣaṭkaprasaṅga iti na ābhāsābheyaniyamāt |
yadyeko'ka: samavāya: sarvetra svatantra: tathāpyādhārādheyaniyamo'sti |
katham dravyeṣveva dravyatvaṃ guṇeṣveva guṇatvaṃ karmasveva karmatvamiti |
evamādau kasmādanvayavyatirekadarśanāt|
ihetisamavāyanimittasya jñānasyānvayadarśanāt sarvaikaḥ samavāya iti gamyate |
dravyatvādinimittānāṃ vyatirekadarśanāt pratiniyamo jñāyate|
yathā kuṇḍadaṇḍīno: saṃyogaikatve bhakṣalyāśrayāśrayibhāvaniyama: |
tathā dravyatvādīnāmapi samavāyaikatve'pi vyaṅgyavyañjakasukṣmasthūlabhedādādhārādheyaniyama iti ||
bodhayati |
yadaika iti |
samavāyaikatve ya eṣa dravyatvasya pṛthivyādiṣu yoga: sa eṣa guṇatvasya guṇeṣu karmatvasya ca karmasu tatha yathā dravyatvasya yoga: pṛthivyādiṣaktīti taiṣāṃ dravyatvam tathā tadyogasya guṇādiṣvapi sambhavāt teṣāmapi dravyatvam |
yathā ca guṇatvasya yogo rūpādiṣvaktīti rūpādīnāṃ guṇatvam tathā tadyogasya dravyakarmayorapi bhāvāt tayorapi guṇatvaṃ syāt |
evaṃ ca karmatvāpipadārthānāṃ saṅkīrṇatā dravyādiṣaḍbhya|
samavāye neti|
na ca padārthānāṃ saṅkīrṇatā kṛtaḥ ādhārodheyaniyamāt|
na samavāyamadṛṣṭasamavetaṃ dravyatvamu kintu dravyasamavāyāt dravyatvaṃ samavāyasya dravye buddhau ca guṇakarmeṣu jñātau ca teṣāṃ dravyatvamu|
evaṃ guṇakarmeṣvapi vyākhyeyam|
etatsaṅgrahaślokaṃ vivṛṇoti|
yadyapyekaḥ samavāya ityādinā|
svatantraḥ saṃyogavat sambandhāntareṇa na badhyata ityarthaḥ|
aśaktamaparam|
punaruktadoṣāt|
evamādi kalpāditi|
dravye-ṣveva dravyatvaṃ vartate guṇeṣveva guṇatvaṃ karmeṣveva karmatvamityevamādi kalpāt|
tvayā jñānamityarthaḥ|
uttaramāha|
anyonyāśritaikadravyaṇāditi|
dravyatvani-mittasya pratyakṣasya dravyeṣvanvaye guṇakarmobhyasya vyatirekaḥ guṇatvanimittasya pratyakṣasya guṇeṣvanvaye dravyakarmobhyasya vyatirekaḥ tathā karmatvanimittasya pratyakṣasya karmeṣvanvaye dravyaguṇobhyasya vyatireko dṛśyate tasmādanyonya-nirekadravyaṇād dravyatvādīnā niyamo jñāyate|
atharva vivaraṇaṃ samavam|
samavā-yāviśeṣe kṛta evārtha niyamaḥ dravyatvasya pṛthivyādiṣeva samavāyo guṇatvasya rūpādiṣeva karmatvasyotkṣepaṇādiṣeva nānyathetyata āha|
yadyeti|
saṃyogābhye-katve'pi kuṇḍabadarīrāgaprācīnādasya niyamo dṛṣṭaḥ śaktiniyamāt|
kuṇḍabada-radyoye dṛṣṭevārthi|
evaṃ samavāyokte'pi dravyatvādīnāmādhārādheyaniyamo vyāhṛtyasvajñakāśaktimedāt|
kimurta syāt|
dravyatvādibhiravyajjakā śaktidravyāṇā-meva tena dravyeṣeva dravyatvaṃ samaveti nānyeti|
evaṃ guṇakarmeṣvapi vyākhyeyam||
sambandhanityatve 'pi na saṃyogavadanityatvaṃ bhā-vavadakāraṇatvāt|
yathā prasaṅgataḥ kāraṇānupalabdhe-rnityo bhāva ityuktaṃ tathā samavāyo'pīti|
na hyasya kiñcit kāraṇaṃ prasaṅgataṃ upalabhyate iti|
kutra puna-raikya dravyādiṣu samavāyo vartate|
na saṃyogaḥ sambha-vati tasya guṇatvena dravyāśritatvāt|
nāpi samavā-yastasyoktatvāt na jānyā vṛttirastīti|
na|
tādā-tmyāt|
yathā dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ svarūpasya bhāvasya nānya saṃyogo'sti|
evamavibhāgino ityātmakasyasamavāyasya nānyā vṛttirasti tasmāt svātmavṛttiḥ|
ata evātīndriyaḥ sattādīnāmiva pratyakṣeṣu dravyaguṇādiṣu svātmagatasaṃvedanābhāvācca|
tasmādihabuddhyanumeyaḥ samavāya iti||
iti praśastapādabhāṣye samavāyapadārthaḥ samāptaḥ||
kiṃ punarayamanitya āhosvinnitya iti saṃśaye brūmaḥ|
sambandhyanityatve'pīti|
yathā sambandhyoranityatve saṃyogasyānityatvaṃ na tathā samavāyinoranityatve samavāyasyānityatvaṃ bhāvaprakāraṇatvāditi|
etadvivṛṇoti|
yathetyādinā|
yukto hi sambandhivināśe saṃyogasya vināśaḥ tadṛtpāde sambandhinoḥ samavāyikāraṇatvāt|
samavāyasya tu sambandhino na kāraṇaṃ sambandhamātratvāt|
yathā na kāraṇaṃ tathopapāditam|
tasmādetasya sambandhivināśe'pyavināśaḥ|
anāderapyayāntare'pi pratyabhijñāyamānatvāt|
kṣamasambaddha eva samavāyaḥ sambandhino sambadhyati sambaddho vā|
na nāvasambaddhasya sambandhakatvaṃ yuktaṃ śaktiprasaṅgāt|
sambandhasyāsya na saṃyoga rūpaḥ sambhavati tasya dravyāśritatvāt|
nāpi samavāya ekatvāt|
na ca saṃyogasamavāyabhayāṃ vyatiriktamasti|
tatkathamasya vṛttirityata āha|
kriyā punarakriyā dravyādiṣu samavāyo varteta iti|
anyabhāvādvā varteta ityabhiprāyaḥ|
paraḥ samādhatte|
naiti|
vṛttyabhāvād varteta ityetanna tādātmyādṛ vṛttyabhaktatvāt svata evāyaṃ vṛttiriti|
kṛtakeṣu hi saṃyogaḥ tasya vṛttyatmakasyāpi vṛttyantaramastīt kāraṇasamavāyasya kāryelakṣaṇatvāt|
samavāyasya vṛttyantaraṃ nāsti|
tasmādasyasvātmanā svarūpeṇaiva vṛttinai vṛttyantaraupamyathe|
ata evātīndriyaḥ|
vanādīnāmiva pratyakṣeṣu vṛttyabhāvāt|
yathā ghaṭādīnāṃ pratyakṣeṣvapi vṛttirasti tena te dhṛyuktasamavāyādindriyeṇa gṛhyante naivaṃ samavāyasya vṛttisambhavaḥ|
ato'tīndriyo'yam|
saṃyogasamavāyāpekṣayaivendriyasya bhāvagrahaṇasāmarthyopalambhāt|
yadi samavāyaviṣayamendriyakaṃ khaṃvedanamasti sambandhāsākṣātkriyāva prasaṅgaḥ|
atha nāsti tadeva vācyamityāhuḥ|
svātmagatakhaṃvedanāsāvāditi|
yathendriyeṇa saṃyogāttadbhāṣo naivaṃ samavāyapratibhāsaḥ sambandhinoḥ pṛthaksaṃbhāveṣalambhāt ato'yamalaukikaḥ|
upasaṃharati|
tasmāditi||
parasparopasaṃśleṣo mitrāṇāṃ yatkṛto bhavet|
samavāyaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ svātantryapratirodhakaḥ||
suvarṇamayakhaṃkhyānarūpyā khavetārasthitiḥ|
suvarṇaḥ śakravījaiva ṭīkarva nyāyacandrikī||
ayoganijacampuṣu khyāpayanto guṇānakhe|
pañcakṛdvuddhikhuddhāntan dvajanti nyāyacandrino||
ājīrdviṣaṇarāṭkāyāṃ dvijānā bhṛgukulīyām|
bhṛrisvṛddhihitavidyā bhūrikīrtijainabhuyaḥ||
abhyorāśeravelasyādubhaya cinicandramāḥ|
jagadānandavaddravyo vṛhaspatiriva dvijaḥ||
tasmādiduṅgaṇitamahāmumudre vidyālanāyasamavalambanabhṛtahetoḥ|
svacchāyayā vidhivaśakīrtinadīpravāhasyando'namanajaro baladevanāmnā||
tasyāpraduriyamabhavi viṣudūkūlasambhavā|
abjoke rvastinaguṇā giriṇāṃ gṛhamedhinī||ghacchāya: sthalaphalado bahupuṣpāḍhyo dvijāśraya: |
sacyo bhrīdhara ityucyate yaskalpadrumo'bhavat ||
kadho vidyāviracanāmapravṛta yacchuṃgitānām |
ṣaṭpadāśehināmitā sūcitaṃ nyāyakandalīm ||
vyadhikandaśottaratabandhanaśākābde nyāyakandalo racitā |
śrīpāpaḍudrāthevinaṭapraṭībhīśereṇeyam ||
samāpteyaṃ padyārthapraveśanyāyakandalīṭīkā ||
samāpto'yaṃ grantha: |
